### **About Underfoot**

Underfoot is EverQuest's sixteenth expansion.

- It was announced on July 1, 2009, and will be officially launched on December 15, 2009.

  - Completion of three "Road to Underfoot" tasks allowed for early access to the expansion beginning on December 8, 2009.

- The initial announcement may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=18683) and the early-access expansion release patch message may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/story.html?story=20820).

* * *

#### **New Features**

- Expansion Progression Window â€“ You'll be able to easily see what you have accomplished through Underfoot and which objectives you should achieve in order to travel in your desired progression path.

  - This [Achievement System](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/Category%3AUnderfoot_(EQ_Achievements)) allows you to display and manage your progress through the world of Norrath.


- Focus Target Window â€“ This new target window will let you add specific targets to your list which will be helpful to monitor the health of Friends or Foe.

- All Inclusive - The expansion includes all features and content from the 15 previous expansion packs.

- More Weapon Models - Underfoot will have twice as many weapon models as any of the previous 15 expansions.

#### **Zones**

- See [this page](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zcont=Underfoot) for a list of zones in Underfoot.
- The first tier(Tier 6) is a step above [Korafax](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zones.html?zstrat=628) in terms of difficulty.

- Tier 6:

  - [Brell's Rest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=704)
  - [Cooling Chamber](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=706)
  - [Pellucid Grotto](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=707)

- Tier 7:

  - [Arthicrex](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=710)
  - [The Foundation](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=709)
  - [The Underquarry](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=708)

- Tier 8 zones can be accessed after completing missions in Tiers 6 and 7:

  - [Brell's Temple](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=711)
  - [Fungal Forest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=712)
  - [Kernagir, the Shining City](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=713)
  - [Lichen Creep](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=714)

- Tier 9:

  - [Volska's Husk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=715) (opened after completing Tier 8 missions)
  - [Convorteum](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=733) (opened after completing Volska's Husk mission)

- A screenshot of the in-game map of zone connections can be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/gallery/ingamemaps/underfoot.jpg).

#### **Progression**

- Main article: [Underfoot Progression Guide](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Progression_Through_Underfoot)

  - Six group missions to get to Tier 8
  - Four group missions to get to Tier 9

#### **Armor**

- Main article: [Underfoot Armor Guide](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/wiki/eq:Underfoot_Armor_Guide)

#### **Spells**

- Rank I spells result from group tasks.
- Rank II spells result from gem trade-ins ( [details here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=4958)).
- Rank III spells are gathered through raiding ( [details here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=4959)).

#### **Getting There**

- You can access Underfoot via the [Guild Hall](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zones.html?zstrat=278) portal or through the [Ruins of Old Paineel](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zones.html?zstrat=99) (The Hole).
- Once you have Tier 3 access, you can also get to Underfoot through [Corathus Creep](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zones.html?zstrat=294).

- There are four tiers in this expansion. The first tier is a step above Korafax in terms of difficulty.

- The first two tiers are open zones:


  - Tier 6:

    - [Brell's Rest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=704)
    - [Cooling Chamber](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=706)
    - [Pellucid Grotto](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=707)


  - Tier 7:

    - [Arthicrex](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=710)
    - [The Foundation](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=709)
    - [The Underquarry](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=708)

- Tier 8 zones can be accessed after completing missions in Tiers 6 and 7:

  - [Brell's Temple](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=711)
  - [Fungal Forest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=712)
  - [Kernagir, the Shining City](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=713)
  - [Lichen Creep](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=714)

- Tier 9:

  - [Volska's Husk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=715) (opened after completing Tier 8 missions)
  - [Convorteum](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zstrat=733)

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This shared task (1-6 players) begins with Kronk Kollus in Pellucid Grotto. He's located at +100, +1326, +3._
_This task is the first of two tasks given by Kronk Kollis._
You say, 'Hail, Kronk Kollus'
Kronk Kollus says 'Ah, strange one, I see you. I have seen you. I will see you again. All of Norrath lies before me, within my [view] below.'
You say, 'Your view?'
Kronk Kollus says 'Brell, in His wisdom, has appointed me as the Watcher of this place in the absence of Brell's true foreman. I stand watch, but it is a [troubled] watch.'
You say, 'Troubled?'
Kronk Kollus says 'Troubled, indeed, \_\_\_\_\_! In this realm lie Brell's sacred thoughts, His very memories, stored in crystals of His own manufacture. Even as I watch, even as I oversee, shadowy agents of some foul master work to unravel the memories [stored] in the crystals.'
You say, 'Stored?'
Kut Kollus says 'Brell's mind is vast, his thoughts innumerable. He created the crystals to be bearers of his memories stretching back to the beginning of time. These memories must be [guarded]. If they become [compromised], Brell's ability to rely on them, and trust them, will be shattered. How will our god know himself if his memories, his core and crucial memories, are corrupted?'
You say, 'Guarded?'
Kronk Kollus says 'Ah, \_\_\_\_\_, those are welcome tidings. Brell's memories are kept in crystal clusters near the pond in the northwest. We tend to them and try to keep them safe, but recently bands of Cliknar have been sneaking into our midst and damaging the crystals. If you would seek out and destroy these interlopers and secure the sanctity of Brell's thoughts, perhaps the balance of things may be restored in this area.'
You have been assigned the task 'A Many-Faceted Dilemma'.
Inspect the crystals in the northwest that hold Brell's memories, and defend the memories from any hostile forces. When you have completed this task, return to Kut Kollus.

---

_Three cliknar spawn and rush your group, so be ready. They are aggro linked and cannot be split from one another. However, they are rootable/snareable, mezzable, and stunnable._

Kill 2 Cliknar thoughtstealers 0/2 (Pellucid Grotto)
_Repeat again:_

_And again:_

Entered: Thu Dec 10 01:21:35 2009
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This shared task (1-6 players) begins with Magnite Ordinator Bammor in Pellucid Grotto at location +100, +1314, +3._
You say, 'Hail, Magnite Ordinator Bammor'
Magnite Ordinator Bammor says 'Dark times have fallen over us. Our once magnificent supply of gems here in the [Repository] is now slowly being depleted. To make matters worse, we are also experiencing an [issue] with the fire elementals as well as [sessiloids] depleting the nutrients in the soil.'
You say, 'The repository?'
Magnite Ordinator Bammor says 'Aye, this is the Repository, and while many are no doubt unaware of this, this place is the metaphysical [source] of all gems which appear in Norrath.'
You say, 'Source?'
Magnite Ordinator Bammor says 'Yes, this place is the source. However, I do not have time to explain this further to you, as the rapidly [depleting] supply of gems in the Repository requires my current attention now.'
You say, 'Depleting their supply?!'
Magnite Ordinator Bammor says 'Thanks to the escape of the Primal Crystallines--creatures like the trichordonts, hulks, spheres, and exterminators--plus the civil war going on amongst the Crystalkins, there has not been any meaningful amount of gem-laying happening in the Repository. In this matter I wish to ask for your [assistance].'
You say, 'I'll give you my assistance'
Magnite Ordinator Bammor says 'Thank you. Unfortunately we do not possess the ability to harvest the gems carried by the Primal Crystallines easily, so you will very likely need to destroy them and harvest their remains. Once you do this, have the Blar Gurin process the gems, and hand it to my worker magnite golems after processing. The Blar Gurin is a Sessiloid located in a passage to the north of Geodech, our city in the center of the grotto.'
You have been assigned the task 'Filling the Repository'.
Magnite Ordinator Bammor has tasked you with harvesting various unfinished gems from the Primal Crystallines--creatures like the trichordonts, hulks, spheres, and exterminators. After you collect the unfinished gems, have the Blar Gurin process the gems before giving them to the magnite golems to deposit. The Blar Gurin is a Sessiloid located in a passageway north of Geodech, the city in the center of the grotto.

You say, 'Hail, Berrina Saltidnarik'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Hello there, \_\_\_\_\_. You look like you're a [visitor] to this realm.'
You say, 'Yep, I'm a visitor'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Not all of us are here by choice. Some who dwell in this realm are bound to it due to our devotion to Brell. That is the position that I find myself in, for example. Still, one does what one [can], no?'
You say, 'They do what they can'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Even in the realm of a god, there is always work that can be done. I pride myself on being a problem solver, personally. I try to find and fix [problems] whenever someone comes to me with one.'
You say, 'Problems?'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Take, for example, a gnoll named Dorrvix. He wants to be a part of the Shining City so bad, it hurts him. The poor thing spends all his days looking up and dreaming. The Gatekeeper just says that it isn't Brell's will for Dorrvix to enter. It feels like there's more to the story, but the Gatekeeper and I don't often see eye to eye. Would you be [willing] to go find out what the trouble is?'
You say, 'I'd be willing'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Wonderful. Go speak with Dorrvix near the gnoll camp to the west. If you must speak with him, the Gatekeeper is located at the top of the great bridge under Brell's Temple to the north.'
You have been assigned the task 'Gnoll Who Your Friends Are'.
The direction to someone related to your current Task has been marked on your compass.
Berrina Saltidnarik has befriended a gnoll named Dorrvix who is living amongst the warring clans to the west. Dorrvix wishes to be allowed to enter the shining city, but the way is blocked to him. Berrina would like you to intervene on Dorrvix's behalf, as your powers may be sufficient to remove some of the obstacles that lie in his path.
Make contact with Dorrvix in the gnoll's camp. He will be able to explain himself better.

---

1\. Speak with Dorrvix 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
Dorrvix [npc=34036] is located in the gnoll camp in the western part of the zone. Location: -170, 685, 31.
You say, 'Hail, Dorrvix'
Dorrvix says 'You want help Dorrvix? Find out why Dorrvix can't get in [city]! Dorrvix deserve city! City is shiny! Dorrvix is good. Dorrvix deserve city.'
Your task 'Gnoll Who Your Friends Are' has been updated.
The direction to someone related to your current Task has been marked on your compass.
You say, 'Tell me about the city'
Dorrvix says 'Shining city! Look up! Gate man keeps Dorrvix out, Dorrvix can't get to the shiny! Talk to gate man on the bridge. Help Dorrvix get to shiny. Dorrvix not bad, bad things happen to Dorrvix.'
2\. Speak with the Gatekeeper 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
_Task Window Says:_ Contact the Gatekeeper to the Shining City and see if you can find out what blocks Dorrvix's path.
The Gatekeeper [npc=34033] is located at the entrance to Kernagir, the Shining City (go up one of the two ramps around the lava in the northern part of the zone). Location: 515, 45, 153.
You say, 'Hail, The Gatekeeper'
The Gatekeeper says 'Ah, Dorrvix, another of the tough luck club. Well, I have to give him credit for being persistent. If this will stop people from bothering me, I will allow you to act on his behalf, \_\_\_\_\_. You must give this [device] to Dorrvix's peers. Find five gnolls who are willing to record their testimony and then bring the device back. If you can bring me information that was not considered earlier, perhaps Brell will change his decision.'
Your task 'Gnoll Who Your Friends Are' has been updated.
You say, 'I need the device...'
The Gatekeeper says 'Here you are, \_\_\_\_\_.'
You receive Mnemenic Device [item=85738].
You say, 'Hail, The Gatekeeper'
The Gatekeeper says 'I am known as The Gatekeeper. I allow those Brell desires to [enter] his Shining City. Anyone else is refused entry. If the underfoot is too much for you I can [send you] back to the way people enter here. I believe you mortals from your plane call it Paineel.'
You say, 'I wish to enter!'
The Gatekeeper whispers to you, 'I don't say this to everyone, but if you want to enter Kernagir, you must prove that you understand the values that are important here. Perhaps if you speak to Algera Chindlebolt she can put you to work. Work ethic is one of the prime virtues valued by those in the Underfoot. Or if you are overendowed with allies, perhaps Algera has something more dangerous you could assist with.'
3\. Find some gnolls who are willing to testify about Dorrvix 0/5 (Brell's Rest)
4\. Defend yourself from gnolls who are unwilling to help 0/4 (Brell's Rest)
_Task Window Says:_ The Gatekeeper did not seem to be too eager to help, but he is allowing you to seek the testimony of five of Dorrvix's peers. See if you can find five willing volunteers.
Hand the Mnenemic Device to any gnoll to get these updates. They'll give an update for either step. If the latter step, they'll attack, so be ready. They hit for a max ~5,500 and have a good amount of hps.
Responses you'll see:
a gnoll charger says 'Dorrvix wants fight, but he hate losing, so he no fight. He just mad when others fight now.'
a gnoll charger says 'Dorrvix takes food first, and he mean when he does it. He says it because he got food taken away, but he do it all the time.'
a gnoll brawler says 'Dorrvix drink water all time, and he push others away. He tell me to go drink down by lava, where it hot!'
a gnoll guardian says 'When something bad, it never Dorrvix fault. He always say someone else do it even when he do it.'
a gnoll assassin says 'No! No helping the gate man! Fight now!'
a gnoll charger says 'No! I answer already. Go away now.'
5\. Deliver 1 Mnemenic Device to the Gatekeeper 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
_Task Window Says:_ You've collected the testimonies of five gnolls. Bring the recording back to the Gatekeeper.
Your task 'Gnoll Who Your Friends Are' has been updated.
Your collected testimony did not present any information of which the Gatekeeper was unaware. Dorrvix must first take responsibility for his own actions before he can begin his journey of change.
_Rewards:_
157 platinum, 5 gold

# Berrina #2: Clash of the Kobolds

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 01:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This shared task (1-6 players) is the second in a series of three. It begins with Berrina Saltidnarik at location -650, 200, 37 in Brell's Rest.
This task has a one hour timer.
You say, 'Hail, Berrina Saltidnarik'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Hello there, _____.  You look like you're a [visitor] to this realm.'
You say, 'Yep, I'm a visitor'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Not all of us are here by choice.  Some who dwell in this realm are bound to it due to our devotion to Brell.  That is the position that I find myself in, for example.  Still, one does what one [can], no?'
You say, 'They do what they can'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Even in the realm of a god, there is always work that can be done.  I pride myself on being a problem solver, personally.  I try to find and fix [problems] whenever someone comes to me with one.'
You say, 'Problems?'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Well, the kobolds have been a bit restless recently.  I suppose we all have, but when they get restless, they tend to start fights.  While battle is one of our great pleasures here, their aggression is starting to be a real problem in our day-to-day life.  Would you be [willing] to contact their leader and try to find out what's going on?'
You say, 'I'd be willing'
Berrina Saltidnarik says 'Their leader is named Graztuk, and he's normally found near their camp to the east.  Hopefully you can talk some sense into him.'
You have been assigned the task 'Clash of the Kobolds'.
The direction to someone related to your current Task has been marked on your compass.
Berrina is concerned about the unrest amongst the kobold clans to the west. Even the excitement of Brell's Arena is no longer enough to contain their thirst for conflict. They are leaving their camps and goading others into joining their fights, and that does not appear to be part of Brell's plan for his realm.
Find out what is troubling the kobolds. Seek their leader, Graztuk, and parley with him.
1. Speak with Graztuk 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
Graztuk [npc=34064] is located just outside Brell's Arena (eastern part of Brell's Rest). Location: -215, -580, 17.
You say, 'Hail, Graztuk'
Graztuk says 'Rrrrrrrr... _____, you aren't here to [fight], are you?'
Your task 'Clash of the Kobolds' has been updated.
You say, 'I'm here to fight!'
Graztuk says 'Fighting is great.  It lets us work out anger and energy.  We love to fight, and especially with the gnolls!  They're not around [much] any more.'
You say, 'Much more?'
Graztuk says 'Some of them will fight, but not as many as there were before.  We don't know why.  We try to [start] fights with others, but they never want to fight.'
You say, 'Start fights...?'
Graztuk says 'The Burynai don't like to fight us!  They never counterattack.  Some of the gnomes and dwarfs will fight, but I don't know how to tell if they will fight or not.  If only there were a way to find some [volunteers] for my best fighters to spar with, we could stop bothering others.''
You say, 'I have some volunteers'
Graztuk says 'You volunteer?  This is great!  I'll have Recloso meet you on the battlefield to the north of us, near the lava.'
Speak with Recloso 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
Task Window Says: The kobolds have agreed to reign in their patrols, but only if you are willing to provide them with their entertainment! Meet Recloso near the lava lake.
Head a little ways northwest, by the edge of the lava. Approximate location -70, -275, -8. You'll see four totems and a kobold named Recloso [npc=34065].
You say, 'Hail, Recloso'
Recloso says 'Were you [sent] by Graztuk?'
Your task 'Clash of the Kobolds' has been updated.
2. Kill 5 raging kobolds 0/5 (Brell's Rest)
You say, 'Graztuk sent me'
Recloso says 'Good.  I have some kobolds here who are aching for a good fight.  Let me know when you are ready to join the [battle], or if you want me to explain the [rules].'
You say, 'Tell me the rules'
Recloso says 'The rules are simple.  Stay in the ring and fight.  If you leave the ring, you lose.  If you all die, you lose.  If you wait too long to fight, you lose.  Stay in the ring and defeat all of your opponents to win.'
You say, 'Ready for battle'
Recloso says 'Round 1 begins.  Fight well!'
Five raging kobolds spawn. They each hit for a max ~5,000, so you'll have your hands full. Some are mezzable.
a raging kobold champion says 'A fight!  A fight!'
a raging kobold champion says 'A fight!  A fight!'
Stay within the totems while fighting, otherwise you'll see this:
You have left the bounds of the ring.  Return quickly or be disqualified.
Recloso says '_____ has left the bounds of the ring and must return quickly.'
...And the task will fail.
Upon killing the last of the five kobolds:
Recloso says 'Round 1 complete!  Let me know when you are [ready] to continue, or if you want the [rules] explained again.'
3. Kill 5 raging kobolds 0/5 (Brell's Rest)
Saying "Ready" spawns the second wave.
Recloso says 'Round 2 begins.  Fight well!'
Upon completion:
Recloso says 'Impressive.  Round 2 is complete.  Inform me when you are [ready] to continue.'
4. Kill 5 raging kobolds 0/5 (Brell's Rest)
Saying "Ready" spawns the third wave.
Recloso says 'Round 3 begins.  Fight well!'
Upon completion:
Recloso says 'Round 3 is complete. Let me know when you are [ready] for the final battle.'
You say, 'Ready'
Recloso says 'Round 4 begins.  Fight well!'
Recloso says 'You're a match for my kin, but how about me?'
5. Kill 2 raging kobolds 0/2 (Brell's Rest)
5. Kill Recloso 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
Two raging kobolds as well as Recloso attack. Recloso isn't much more difficult than the rest of the waves, but he AE rampages.
Recloso has been slain by _____!
Recloso's corpse says 'Finally, a worthy opponent...'
Your task 'Clash of the Kobolds' has been updated.
Speak with Graztuk 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
You say, 'Hail, Graztuk'
Graztuk says 'It seems you have more fight than even we could handle.  I'm sure my brethren will desire a rematch at some point in the future.'
Your task 'Clash of the Kobolds' has been updated.
The kobolds appear to be more bloodthirsty because their normal sparring partners, the gnolls, are not as interested in fighting.  Perhaps the ultimate answer lies with them.
157 platinum, 5 gold

7\. Kill Parrmug 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
_Task Window Says:_ Perhaps some force will make him tell you why he's doing this.
Parrmug hits for a max ~6,000. Beat him to 30%, at which point he'll go inactive.
Parrmug says 'Okay, okay, I [talk!]'
You say, 'Then talk!'

You say, 'What secret?'

At this point, he resumes attacking. At the same time, a large number of undead gnolls spawn around you (23 to be exact). They are 2x " an unyielding gnoll reaver [npc=34073]" (yellow-con; max hit ~6,500) and 21x " a frail gnoll reaver [npc=34072]" (blue-weak-con; max hit ~900).

11\. Deliver 1 Tattered Rune Cloth to Berrina 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
Berrina tells you,"This is a very troubling story. Undead have strange properties in this realm, and I'm not quite sure what to do with this knowledge. Perhaps Algera will be able to make sense of it."

# Don't Fear the Destroyer

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This group task begins with Algera Chindlebolt at location -675, 265, 52 in Brell's Rest (by the zone-in from The Hole). In order to request this task you must first have completed Berrina's three tasks.
You say, 'Hail, Algera Chindlebolt'
Algera Chindlebolt says 'You there! By what unnatural means have you come to Brell's Rest? Oh, I suppose it matters not. The [threat] that is coming, after everything else that has happened lately around here, it cannot be the fault of adventurers like you.'
You say, 'What threat?'
Algera Chindlebolt says 'You may have heard talk of something called The Destroyer since you came to Brell's Rest. I assure you, it is real, though the dwarves and gnomes have been confounded in our attempts to learn more about it. The gnolls know of this menace, and though some of them are concerned, others seem to be supporting it with magical means. This must [stop] at once. While Brell does not appear to be present to exert his will in this place of eternal rest, its residents appear to be free to act as they will, possibly with deadly effects on what we thought were our everlasting spirits.'
You say, 'Yes, it must stop'
Algera Chindlebolt says '[Dealing] with gnolls has always been a slippery proposition, even when I was a mortal. Getting them to cease and desist from unwise or unsavory behavior is one of the more futile things one can hope to do. With so many things in Brell's Rest spinning out of their proper order, the last thing we need is a gigantic monster on the loose, much less one that is aided and boosted by a band of death-worshipping gnolls, beloved of Brell though they may be.'
You say, 'Dealing with the gnolls?'
Algera Chindlebolt says 'Loathe I am to say these words in this sacred place, but it may require violence to thwart the plans of these gnolls. Even then, this Destroyer monster may not be stopped, but his further growth prevented. This cannot happen unless we know which gnolls are actually up to no good. If we harm innocents here and cause Brell to have to retrieve them from oblivion, we will be cast out. If you will, please form a party of inquiry into this matter, and [investigate].'
You say, 'I'll investigate'
Algera Chindlebolt says 'The gnoll most open to talking about what the gnolls are up to is Shorf the Shaman. He is the one most willing to part with information regarding the Destroyer. Go to Shady Nook and let him be the beginning of your quest. Tell me when you are [ready] to be attuned to his presence, and I shall aid you in locating him.'
You have been assigned the task 'Don't Fear the Destroyer'.
Algera Chindlebolt is now aware of the existence of the Destroyer, whoever that is, and now believe that the gnolls are taking advantage of the arrival of this 'Destroyer' for the advancement of their own agenda. Seek them out and decide whether or not they are a threat to the general order of things, and learn anything you can about this 'Destroyer'.
You say, 'Ready!'
Algera Chindlebolt says 'May Brell watch over you and keep you safe.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Brell's Rest - Don't Fear the Destroyer.

Shorf the Shaman says 'Yes, the Destroyer. You seek the Destroyer? Why? Destroyer rearrange Brell's Rest soon, they say. Make it more to some gnolls' liking, they say. Maybe you learn more from Zakarf.'
Your task 'Don't Fear the Destroyer' has been updated.
Speak with Zakarf the Assassin regarding the Destroyer's whereabouts 0/1 (Brell's Rest)
Zakarf stands next to Shorf. Be ready for a fight.

Zakarf the Assassin says 'So you think to ply me for talk about the Destroyer? Forget it! You die now! I kill you, and when you dead, you not remember anything, like me telling you where Groth'roth has secret meetings in Watery Nook! After I kill you, maybe I kill that Garrhuff Gabblepaws too!'
Zakarf the Assassin pounces!
Zakarf hits for a max ~6,500.

You say, 'Hail, Rodrick Cleanheart'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Greetings \_\_\_\_\_. I am pleased to see you, although my heart is [heavy].'
You say, 'Why is your heart is heavy?'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Everyone around you has a story to tell, \_\_\_\_\_, and I am no different. My tale, however, is full of [glory] and the favor of Brell. My spirit was shaped by a life lived behind the keen edge of the blade of Justice, and undead horrors from all corners of Norrath learned to tremble at the sound of my boots.'
You say, 'Glory?'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Aye, glory! I've campfire tales a'plenty I could share, young one, but the days are too [dark] for boastful tales of victory. I'll tell no tales, at least not while these halls are filled with the shuffling steps of death.'
You say, 'Too dark?'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Dark indeed, \_\_\_\_\_, dark days indeed! In flat contradiction of the will of my Lord Brell, the dead roam these halls! My stomach turns just to think of it. Any hour I can spare from my post finds me in the depths, blade in hand, vanquishing the dead as I can. But it never seems to be [enough] - each abomination slain is replaced by two more the next day. It's getting worse, \_\_\_\_\_.'
You say, 'What's enough?'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'My blade will never be enough, I know that now. Will you [help] me?'
You say, 'I'll help'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Praise be to Brell! None of these other sheep will raise so much as an axe, so focused are they on the temperature of the waters. Fah, I say! What does that matter in the face of a Banshee? But enough of that, let's get to business, \_\_\_\_\_. We must find the [source] if we are to have any chance at stopping this threat.'
You say, 'The source?'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Journey to the depths of the Chamber and destroy the dead. As you kill them search for a clue that will tell us who the Master is. Once we know that we can resolve this situation.'
You have been assigned the task 'Investigate the Dead'.
Rodrick Cleanheart, the noble paladin, has enlisted you in the effort to cleanse the Cooling Chamber of the undead infestation. Destroy any undead you encounter and search the corpses for any clues or items of power that might be on them that might point towards the Master of this infestation.

You say, 'Hail, Rodrick Cleanheart'

You say, 'What devices?'

1\. Find the next Clue! 0/1 (The Cooling Chamber)
_Click the amulet and you'll see an emote indicating how far away you are from the location you need to be in. The emotes will change in color (similar to the /consider system) as you get closer to the location you need._
The location is random: One location is 2530, -1583, -489; another is -1900, -1500, -382, and also -2054, 159, -379.
Upon successful click, "an undead lieutenant" appears. Four adds called "a hand of death" spawn during the fight (at about 65%). The undead lieutenant hits for a max ~11,500 and has ~950,000 hitpoints.
_Loot a Signet Amulet [item=85898] from the named or the adds._
2\. Give the Signet Amulet to Rodrick Cleanheart 0/1 (The Cooling Chamber)
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Oh, the treachery! I recognize the mark on this amulet! The foul source of this corruption is none other than Tasnise Underbelly! We must deal with this! Return later, friend, and we will discuss how we can best crush this evil scourge in our midst.'
_Reward:_
You gain experience!!

You say, 'Hail, Rodrick Cleanheart'
Rodrick Cleanheart says 'Brell's eyes must shine down on you from below his [heavy] brows, my friend. You have been assigned the task 'Tasnise Underbelly'.
Tasnise has been proven to be corrupted beyond redemption. He has embraced necromancy and has been using his power to disrupt the workings of the Cooling Chambers. Rodrick believes that his power must be vast. The number of undead in the area alone proves this. He has asked you to see if you can defeat Tasnise and end his evil.
Finding Tasnise might be difficult enough. If he knows that his veil of lies has been pierced, he may hide himself.

Defeat Tasnise Underbelly 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
_As you zone in, you'll see a rather annoyed Tasnise Underbelly shouting at his workers._
Tasnise Underbelly shouts 'You WILL do as I say.'
_He is located just below the zone-in point and is aggro._
Tasnise Underbelly says 'Hah! Now you know why you can never hope to defeat me! My life can never be taken!'
Tasnise Underbelly says 'I have traded some fraction of my power to creatures that never die, and I have taken from them their unliving essence. You can never hope to defeat me!'
Tasnise Underbelly says 'I will take your lives as I have so many others. I shall drain what little power you have and add it to my own.'
_At 70%, he will heal to full and translocate group to random locations (the zone-in or the undead tunnels):_
Tasnise Underbelly says 'Enough of this foolishness. I haven't the time to kill you. After all, that's why I have mindless minions! Besides, I have a portal to build and a kingdom to usurp!'
_This updates the step._

Find a way to defeat Tasnise! 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
_Task Window Says:_ It appears that hiding isn't something that Tasnise needs to do. His body recovers from serious wounds instantly! You must regroup and find a way to defeat his necromantic healing.
_In the five deadends of the undead tunnels are five different named "Dark" mobs. Kill these and loot their gems._
_Darkcloth Mummy is at the "Rags" location and drops Scuffed Dark Gem Fragment. It uses:_
Dark Reasoning: Single Target, (-120)

3: Increase corruption counter by 3
Deathcloth Spore: Single Target, Magic (-450)
1: Hitpoints unknown calc: 1999 range: 1000 -> 0
2: Increase Poison Counter by 8

Dark Watchman: Single Target, Chromatic (-1000)

Death's Light: Single Target, Magic (0)
1: Blindness (0)
_Darksoul Ghost is at the "Bonechips" location and drops Frozen Dark Gem Fragment. It uses:_
Dark Reasoning: Single Target, (-120)
1: Decrease Attack Speed by 40%
2: Decrease Movement by 60%
3: Increase corruption counter by 3
Deathshock: Single Target, Magic (0)
1: Decrease AC by 1500

_Darkbone Skeleton is at the "Shrieking Death" location and drops Smooth Dark Gem Fragment. It uses:_
Boneshards: Directional 150', Chromatic (-500)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 20000
Dark Leeching: Lifetap 0', Magic (-500)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 16000
_Dark Spectre is at the "Rampaging Ghoul" location and drops Jagged Dark Gem Fragment. It uses:_
Dark Repulsion: Single Target, Magic (-500)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 14000
2: Unknown #380 (100/4/0)
Scything Slice: Single Target, Chromatic (-450)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 5000 per tick

---

The Dark Gem fragment is obviously magical. Discover its secret. 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
_Click any of the fragments and it will assemble into a Dark Gem of the Underbelly._

---

Destroy the Dark Gem after weakening Tasnise Underbelly 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
_Task Window Says:_ The gem cannot be broken while Tasnise's body is whole, but when he draws from the gem to heal himself, you may be able to destroy it.
_Tasnise Underbelly has relocated to the undead area but does not aggro you. You can clear if necessary and pull him when ready._
_He casts some or all of the following "Dark" spells (how many of these you see may depend on how long you take to kill him):_
Dark Daze: Single Target, Magic (-500)
1: Stun (8.00 sec)
Dark Leeching: Lifetap 0', Magic (-500)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 16000
Dark Reasoning: Single Target, (-120)
1: Decrease Attack Speed by 40%
2: Decrease Movement by 60%
3: Increase corruption counter by 3
Dark Repulsion: Single Target, Magic (-500)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 14000
2: Knockback (4) and Toss Up (6)
Dark Watchman: Single Target, Chromatic (-1000)
1: Summon Pets: CCHMissDarkWatchmenPet x 1 for 60 sec
_Click the Dark Gem any time between 70% and 60% of his health. He regenerates to full health and will now be killable._
Tasnise Underbelly shouts 'Fools! You may have returned to me my mortal weakness, but with that weakness also comes all of my power! You have returned to me that which I gave to my undead agents in exchange for their immortal life. With it I shall crush you! What matter that I will need to rebuild the gem. Time for that once you are all dead!'

---

Kill Tasnise now, while he is vulnerable 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
_NOTE: The task locks as soon as Tasnise dies._
_Finish him off._

---

Speak with Rodrick 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
_You may or may not be able to do this with Rodrick in the instanced zone. If not, say "return" to exit, then hail Rodrick Cleanheart in the regular zone to complete the task._
Defeating Tasnise will help the workers in the Cooling Chambers very much. At least it will if the necromancer remains dead...

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task begins with Kronk Kollus in Pellucid Grotto. He's located at +100, +1326, +3. This follows his first task, "A Many-Faceted Dilemma" (pre-requisite)._
You say, 'Hail, Kronk Kollus'
Kronk Kollus says 'Ah, strange one, I see you. I have seen you. I will see you again. All of Norrath lies before me, within my [view] below.'
You say, 'Your view?'
Kronk Kollus says 'Brell, in His wisdom, has appointed me as the Watcher of this place in the absence of Brell's true foreman. I stand watch, but it is a [troubled] watch.'
You say, 'Troubled?'
Kronk Kollus says 'Troubled, indeed, \_\_\_\_\_! In this realm lie Brell's sacred thoughts, His very memories, stored in crystals of His own manufacture. Even as I watch, even as I oversee, shadowy agents of some foul master work to unravel the memories [stored] in the crystals.'
You say, 'Stored?'
Kronk Kollus says 'Brell's mind is vast, his thoughts innumerable. He created the crystals to be bearers of his memories stretching back to the beginning of time. These memories must be [guarded]. If they become [compromised], Brell's ability to rely on them, and trust them, will be shattered. How will our god know himself if his memories, his core and crucial memories, are corrupted?'
You say, 'How are they compromised?'
Kronk Kollus says 'The Cliknar have already damaged the crystals. Several of Brell's memories have been corrupted, and there is no restoring them. What remains must be protected, but I fear the place where they are kept holds no shelter for the master's memory. They must be moved, yet I must not leave my watch. Another must rise to [protect] the memories.'
You say, 'Protect them?'
Kronk Kollus says 'One must not take this charge lightly, \_\_\_\_\_. The memories of the master must be removed and transported to our city, Geodech, where they may be kept safe. This Lucidic Siphon has been created to draw Brell's memories out of the crystals, but unfortunately it can only hold one per trip. The siphon will provide safe transport to Geodech. Can I count on you to [help]?'
You say, 'I will help'
You have been assigned the task 'A Delicate Matter'.
_You receive a "Lucidic Siphon"._

---

Draw Brell's Memories into the Lucidic Siphon 0/5 (Pellucid Grotto)
Deliver Brell's Memories to Gol Kulnar 0/5 (Pellucid Grotto)
_Go to the place with the Memory Crystals (northwest corner). Hit or nuke a crystal until it reaches 35% or lower (mobs around shouldn't attack you) at which point you see..._
a memory crystal shimmers and fades as it begins to weaken
_Click the Siphon while you have the crystal targetted, turning it into a Full Lucidic Siphon._
_Go to Gol Kulnar and hail him (stay invisible). The Siphon turns into a Lucidic Siphon again. Repeat this four more times, then go back to Kronk._
_Watch out for see invis mobs, particularly the crystalline hulks._
Return to Kronk Kollus 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
Give the Lucidic Siphon to Kronk Kollus 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
_Reward:_
Experience
_The task has a 6-hour lockout timer._

Entered: Tue Dec 15 03:20:03 2009
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is a group task (3-6 players) that begins with Tronten the Subnexus in Pellucid Grotto, location: +345, 360, 15._
_In order to request this task, you must first have completed pre-requisite tasks: "A Many Faceted Dilemma", "A Delicate Matter", and "Filling the Repository"._
You say, 'Hail, Tronten the Subnexus'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Mortals of flesh. I must suffer them. I must use them to maintain control in the Repository. I must have their [help].'
You say, 'How can we help?'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'From the moment time came to this place, until very near to now, all have known the presence of Brell Serilis, our creator. Now he is not present. This distresses us all. Never has there been time passing in Underfoot without him being near. Never until now. I suffer much confusion. We all suffer. Some now question their purpose, to [dangerous] [effects].'
You say, 'Dangerous?'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Aggression from the Autarchian have made production very slow. They constantly get in the way and impede our progress, and I feel it is time someone does the [same] to them.'
You say, 'What effects'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Yes. All of us ask questions. Some ask questions that cannot be answered. Some take the lack of answers to be a loss of purpose. Some come to the conclusion that because change has come to the Underfoot without explanation, certain things that have always been done need no longer be done. Some have put down the duties that have driven them for most of eternity. This is dangerous. They were created by Brell to do what they must. They cannot change. . . they must not [change].'
You say, 'Change?'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'I see no reason for these awkward differences of thought to appear. Yet they have, and now that there are differences of thinking, the owners of those thoughts refuse to change them. I am disturbed by this. We learned a new word from you mortals who now come to visit us. . . [faction].'
You say, 'Faction...'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'The disunity of thought, and the factions among us which have been created by this phenomenon, threaten to destroy this place. All of the Pellucid Grotto is threatened. I understand it not. Those who agree with Tronten remain followers of Brell, though some doubt he will ever return. Those who disagree with Tronten are mostly followers of the Church of Self, who teaches them to abandon their duties and do whatever pleases them. Then there are the [Cliknar] who follow none but the whims of their hive-mind. Gem [production] and distribution has slowed dangerously since this disunity began. I am very frustrated by this.'
You say, 'Gem production?'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'We are the ones who mine, fashion, and polish the gems, crystals, and lesser minerals, which are placed into your mortal worlds. Were our duties to cease, not only would the supply of precious gems come to a halt, but your worlds would erode, the mana would ebb away, the lines of magic would unbind everywhere, and the waking worlds would start to die. All things are touched by what we make for the [Repository]. You should be concerned about this.'
You say, 'The repository?'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'The Repository is breaking down. Loyalists and rebels among the crystalkin continually stop doing their duty, and descend into pointless arguments over belief systems. Even more distastefully, these arguments often descend further until violence happens. [Will] you help us bring stability to the Repository?'
You say, 'We will'
_Without pre-requisites:_
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Flesh being must prove worthiness and skill. Must earn trust of Magnite Ordinator Bammor and Kut'Kollus before being given important task.
_With pre-requisites:_
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Please go and speak with Bammor, leader of the Magnite Golems. These Magnites have served Brell's will in the Repository since he first created it. Bammor knows what needs to be done. If you assist him, there will be materials I could offer as rewards. I would thank you. I am pleased you are helping us. Tell me when you are ready to [go] to the Repository on this mission.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Seeding Frenzy'.
Tronten the Subnexus is aware of breakdowns in gem production occurring in the Grotto, which he finds unaccpetable. The gems must flow! Whatever is threatening the safety of the Repository enough that Bammor would send Tronten a message of distress must be dealt with at once!
_To zone in:_
You say, 'Ready to go!'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Practice caution in all you do. These are difficult times for the Underfoot.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Pellucid Grotto - Seeding Frenzy.

---

_The first five steps take place in the Magnite temple (western part of the zone; same place as Kronk in non-instance Pellucid)._
Speak with Bammor the Magnite 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
You say, 'Hail, Bammor the Magnite'
Bammor the Magnite says 'So, Tronten has at last sent us agents who will help rectify our labor shortages and defend this repository. With all the problems recently -- the crystalkin rebelling and the cliknar attacking -- we could use the help. Please begin your work by conferring with Oduz and Geltior. They will explain how the magnites are afflicted by the schism among the crystalkin and the schemes of the cliknar.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
Speak with Oduz the Magnite 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
You say, 'Hail, Oduz the Magnite'
Oduz the Magnite says 'We magnites labor endlessly to fill the worlds of all creation with the gems needed to keep their mana flowing and economies rich. We rely on the labors of the crystalkin to provide us with finished gems to seed into the worlds. Without their help, we quickly become useless. Nearly half the crystalkin now fail to heed the words of Brell. Without the gems they are supposed to bring us, your world, Norrath, will soon falter and wither. They must be encouraged to assist us. I fear there is no workable solution as long as they insist upon disagreement and resistance to the way of Brell.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
Speak with Geltior the Gembringer 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
You say, 'Hail, Geltior the Gembringer'
Geltior the Gembringer says 'I have nothing but complaints regarding the way things have been run in the Repository since Brell disappeared. I almost think some of the autarchian crystalkin might have been right about the breakdowns that have been occurring. They think we should reason with the cliknar, give them a percentage of gems in exchance for peace. It would be better than the constant attacks we have been facing. Here, take this bag of complaint forms to Bammor. Maybe after reading these he'll reconsider his positions and put things in working order.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
Deliver Big Grey Bag of Complaints to Bammor 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
Bammor the Magnite says 'It appears the other gembringers and magnites are allowing their fears and doubts to subvert their better judgment. Let it be known that Geltior's lengthy list of complaints will not return Brell Serilis to the Underfoot from wherever it is he has gone, nor will it add progress to the duties set before us by Brell himself. We must prevail upon the Autarchians to return to their senses and once again aid our efforts in the Repository. And we must never try to reason with the insidious hive mind of the cliknar.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
Speak with Bammor 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
You say, 'Hail, Bammor the Magnite'
Bammor the Magnite says 'We can only solve the dilemma one step at a time. Before facing the cliknar, our front should be united. You will take my order to Cheon the Workmaster, who huddles with his autarchian cronies near the Black Plinth to the east of Geodech, in defiance of Brell's will. Cheon must submit at once and resume his duties. If he does not comply, be prepared to convince him to do so by force. Regardless of his choice, I will need the gems his workers are carrying. The work must continue; the seeders must have the gems.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.

---

Speak with Cheon 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
_Through a tunnel to the immediate east of the port-in point, you will come out into an opening full of rebellious crystalkin and a named, Cheon the Workmaster. Mobs become aggro as you hail Cheon, it's generally best for one person to do the hail while the rest wait in the tunnel and use that as a pull point._
You say, 'Hail, Cheon the Workmaster'
Cheon the Workmaster says 'Of course we will not submit to Bammor's order. We serve our own will now. We have awakened to the truth of the Way of Self, and will aspire to serve our Autarchy in the pursuit of goals that are more meaningful than doing Brell's will. It is past time for Bammor to accept that the old ways are done. Brell has forsaken us. As well, we forsake those who strive to keep us to a fruitless path. You are an agent of Bammor; you are to be eliminated.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
Cheon the Workmaster says 'I will grind you into dust!'
Destroy the rebellious crystalkin 0/18 (Pellucid Grotto)
Destroy Cheon 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
_Task Window Says:_ Negotiations have broken down. Kill the rebellious crystalkin warriors and eliminate Cheon; his irredeemable stance is only going to get more crystalkin killed.
_Cheon hits for a max ~8,000; AE rampages; and single-target rampages while the rebellious crystalkin (which can each be pulled single or mezzed) hit for a max ~6,000._
_Be sure to loot the Refined Seeding Gems from the crystalkin. You need 20 of them._
_After the above kill steps and looted items are done, everyone should move to the magnite temple before completing the next series of hails, after which 15 cliknar + 1 named spawn on the bridge._

---

Deliver the rebels' gems to the crustal seeder 0/20 (Pellucid Grotto)
_The crustal seeder is in the same room with Bammor, Geltior, and other quest NPCs._
Speak with Bammor 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
You say, 'Hail, Bammor the Magnite'
Bammor the Magnite says 'Unfortunate that the rebellious autarchians made resistance. Their deaths are regrettable, but at least the gems they carried have been given to the crustal seeders. I will send word to Tronten to organize a new work team. Now, to deal with the cliknar threat. They have been attacking the workers and stealing our gems. Geltior knows more about the threat. Speak with him.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
Speak with Geltior about the Cliknar threat 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
You say, 'Hail, Geltior the Gembringer'
Geltior the Gembringer says 'Bammor is still worried about the cliknar attacks? Yes, they have been bothering us, but I think I found a solution. I spoke to one of them, Voskatik, and I think I have secured the peace. He will stay away from the Repository as long as...wait, I hear clicking coming from outside...'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
_16 cliknar (including a named) spawn outside the room you're in, on the bridge over the lava. They can be single-pulled. The non-named cliknar hit for a max ~6,500 and summon. They are stunnable and mezzable. The 85th level cliknar are rootable/snareable while the higher level ones are not._
_The General hits for a max ~8,000 and summons. He's immune to mez, stun, and root._
Destroy the invading Cliknar 0/15 (Pellucid Grotto)
Destroy Voskatik and salvage any information jelly he is carrying 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
_NOTE: The task locks upon the General's death. Loot the "Voskatik's Message Jelly" from his corpse._
Deliver the jelly to Geltior 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
Geltior the Gembringer says 'I see you have Voskatik's memory jelly. This is proof I intended no wrong! Thank you for giving me the chance to explain my conversation with the cliknar leader. I did not know he would try to attack. I told him he could have a certain percentage of our gems, in exchange for peace. I gave him ten whole sacks of gems, but I suppose that wasn't enough. I cannot believe they tried to kill us and take all of the gems! Do they not understand that creatures of Brell must not destroy each other here in the Underfoot?'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
Tell Bammor that the thread has passed, for now 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
You say, 'Hail, Bammor the Magnite'
Bammor the Magnite says 'I thank you for your assistance. I will have to have a conversation with Geltior about his folly. It almost cost us our lives -- and the lives of countless other creatures. Cliknar violating the sanctity of this place could only mean they are interested in destroying other worlds. Perhaps they would overseed worlds like Norrath below, with enough gems to cause a mana disjunction or something even worse. Stopping them was a good thing; Brell would not have been pleased to see our Repository duties ended. [Leave] me now and speak with Tronten.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
You say, 'I wish to leave'
Bammor the Magnite says 'Tell Tronten we can always use more adventurers to protect the Repository.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Pellucid Grotto.
Speak with Tronten 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
Return to Tronten the Subnexus and report your success in restoring the flow of gems to Norrath.
You say, 'Hail, Tronten the Subnexus'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Very well. Mortals did as I asked. Depredations of autarchian crystalkin thwarted; cliknar assault has been repulsed. There is much damage to repair, but I am pleased. May Brell return to us before more destructive events take place.'
Your task 'The Seeding Frenzy' has been updated.
You have crushed the crystalkin rebellion and successfully prevented a cliknar takeover of the Magnite Repository! Had this institution fallen into cliknar hands, it would have given the cliknar the power to disrupt the manaflows of Norrath.

This solo task begins with Clontik Beardburner who is located in the zone-in area of the Underquarry (location -15, 150, -193).
You say, 'Hail, Clontik Beardburner'
Clontik Beardburner says 'Eh? Who are you? You don't belong here! Well, seeing as how there are so few of us left still devoted to carrying out the will of Brell Serilis, it would only harm our cause to waste effort having you [moved] from this place.'
You say, 'Moved?!'
Clontik Beardburner says 'This is the Underquarry; we mine and quarry material here, endlessly. Stone, precious metals, marble, you name it. At the moment, however, things are in a disastrous state. My stone-mining crews are being insubordinate, the Burynai are claiming the copper mine for themselves, there are Cliknar invading the silver mine, and everyone I've sent to the gold mines has yet to return. I [fear] the entire Underquarry may spin completely out of control soon, and if our production isn't corrected, there will be terrible ramifications for the entire Underfoot and all the worlds that connect to it.'
You say, 'Why do you fear this?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'So much depends on me, but ever since the influence of this Church of Self began to be a problem, more and more of my Underquarry crew have failed to follow my orders! Being assigned to the Underquarry is the highest of honors a Dwarf or even a Gnome could ever hope to achieve!  Why would one risk losing the favor of Brell by abandoning one's sacred duties? Yet that is precisely what has happened. I, and these few who remain here, are all that are left who remain dedicated in service to Brell. Yet it is strange, Brell no longer appears, or has not been seen or heard from in a very long time. I may need your [help] if we are to restore the Underquarry to its proper functioning.'
You say, 'How could I help?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'Much to do, much to do. Your help would genuinely be appreciated. I will need help with restoring order in the [Stonequarry] as well as in the [Metal Mines]. When you have completed these tasks I have [other work] I need completed.'
You say, 'The metal mines?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'I have lost so many guildsmen of late in the Metal Mines alone it is enough to make me thirst for a soothing ale again. I can send no more of my own dwarves and gnomes down there; so few are left, we need them to endure if the Underquarry is to be [restored].'
You say, 'I could see about having them restored'
Clontik Beardburner says 'You will go in their stead? What a noble creature you are. When Brell returns, I will be sure to mention your name to him in a most favorable light. Go into each of the metal mines in the Underquarry. You will need to seek out the leader of each of the groups occupying each of the mines, and eliminate them.'
You have been assigned the task 'Unrest in the Metal Mines'.
Brell's absence has turned the Underquarry upside down. The golems and burynai are in revolt, and the cliknar have been persuaded to tear the silver mines from Brell's grasp. Go to each of the three metal mines of the Underquarry and put an end to any insubordination taking place there.
The copper mines have become a nest of Burynai rebels. Go there and stop their leader from turning the Burynai even further against the Way of Brell. Cliknar have burrowed into the silver mine and are definitely up to no good. Go there and eliminate their Protauris so that their activities will be slowed. Most of the remaining golems have found their way into the Underquarry's gold mines, and they too are refusing to follow the tasks which gave them purpose. Destroy the leader of the golems and stop them from abandoning their purpose.
- 1. Investigate the rebel presence in the Underquarry Copper Mine 0/1 (The Underquarry)
- 2. Kill the rebellious Burynai leader, Lord Ragarrh 0/1 (The Underquarry)
- 3. Go to the Silver Mine and locate the rebel leader there 0/1 (The Underquarry)
- 4. Destroy the cliknar leader, known as the 'Protauris' 0/1 (The Underquarry)
- 5. Explore the Underquarry Gold Mine and learn which golem is leading the insurrection there 0/1 (The Underquarry)
- 6. Eliminate the rebellious golem known as Keolyx 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Locations for map file:
P 765.0728, -187.5706, -199.1053,  0, 0, 0,  1,  copper_mine
P 605.1702, 445.2426, -431.2084,  0, 0, 0,  1,  silver_mine
P 176.7785, 711.2474, -368.3794,  0, 0, 0,  1,  gold_mine
P 1373.1821, 248.6674, -313.4822,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Lord_Ragarrh
P 672.2256, 658.0920, -541.6625,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Protauris
P 779.4237, 590.4119, -634.2004,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Keolyx
Copper mines are due east from the zone-in area (the burynai caves). Lord Ragarrh is in the southeasternmost cave.
To get to the silver mines, go to the large room with the giant stone genari and head into the tunnels from the eastern wall of this room. These are the cliknar caves. Towards the bottom/back of these tunnels are three identical looking open caves. Protauris is in the northwestern one.
To get to the gold mines, go to the large room with the giant stone genari and head into the tunnels from the southern wall of this room. These are the golem caves. All the way at the very bottom is Keolyx.
- 7. Report back to Clontik and tell him the rebel leaders have been dealt with 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Clontik Beardburner'
Clontik Beardburner stares at his boots. "There are better things I'd like to be doing than sending people to kill the creatures of Brell in the metal mines, but I must do what I can to preserve this sacred place, this eternal Underquarry."
175 platinum
Experience (about 1.5 AAs at Level 85)
~17 Brellium TokensRewards:
- Brellium Token [item=88944]

You say, 'How could I help?'

You say, 'What about the Stonequarry?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'I do not know how long I've been here - eons, it seems - and always the Underquarry has performed flawlessly. Then the miserable Emissaries came. I can't tell you how much I loathe them, for they have turned most of my mining crew against me, against the Way of Brell, and spread chaos to the Underquarry. You have been assigned the task 'Chaos in the Stonequarry'.
Go to the main Stonequarry chamber and eliminate the disobedient engineers and foremen.
Clontik said his subordinates in the first Stonequarry room must be dealt with first; go there and begin searching for the engineers and foremen.

# An Alternative Explanation

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task begins with Clontik Beardburner who is located in the zone-in area of the Underquarry (location -15, 150, -193).
In order to request this task, you must first have completed two of Clontik's other solo tasks: "Chaos in the Stonequarry" and "Unrest in the Metal Mines".
You say, 'Hail, Clontik Beardburner'
Clontik Beardburner says 'Much to do. Much to do. Your help would genuinely be appreciated. I will need help with restoring order in the [Stonequarry] as well as in the [Metal Mines]. When you have completed these tasks I have [other work] I need completed.'
You say, 'Other work?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'There are some follow-up tasks to be performed now that I have been getting feedback from the rest of the Underquarry. My plan for restoring order has [failed]. The Underquarry is producing quantities of stone and metal that just aren't needed, and we will have to find a [solution] somehow.'
You say, 'Failed?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'Yes, the elimination of the corrupted managers and workers in the Underquarry has done nothing to restore harmony with Brell's vision among the workforce. We must dig [deeper] if I am to know what to do.'
You say, 'Deeper?'
You have been assigned the task 'An Alternative Explanation'.
Look for Reggund Tilecracker in the northern stonequarry room, deep in the Underquarry 0/1 (The Underquarry)
This step updates at -31, -66, -363 (In the large quarry room -- entered through a tunnel leading out of the genari/Goralf room with the pit. He is in the northern part of this room.)
Reggund Tilecracker supposedly has a different approach to the situation in the Underquarry 0/1 (The Underquarry)
There are many see invis genati around here. Hail Reggund for the update (+417, +40, -334).
You say, 'Hail, Reggund Tilecracker'
Reggund Tilecracker says 'Ahh. Clontik finally seeks out my assistance, when all else fails. Killing won't suffice to put the Underquarry back on track, my friend. Not now. Perhaps you'd like the key to resolving this breakdown of order?'
Hear more of Reggund's ideas 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Reggund Tilecracker'
Reggund Tilecracker says 'The creatures here in the Underquarry aren't concerned with their work any longer. They still do their work, but at their own pace and on their own schedule, which no doubt infuriates Clontik to no end. For thousands of years he has been set by Brell to manage the Underquarry and never had a problem until now. Further, this problem cannot be solved the way he is now going about it. For him to understand how to get what he wants, he will need to understand what now motivates his workers. To do that, you will have to help him acquire copies of their recent communications.'
Memorize the details of Reggund's plan for the Clontik's benefit 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Reggund Tilecracker'
Reggund Tilecracker says 'Unfortunately, though we all abhor killing in the Underfoot, the creatures here guard their possessions most jealously. You will no doubt have to slay the creatures to get their secret communications. Every group of creatures I've mentioned has a unique method of relaying information. The burynai plotters use a drumlike cylinder called an Archtone Wheelstick; the cliknar sergeants receive Command-Flavor Hivejelly which tells them what is expected of them; and the Iron Golems have been seen accepting some sort of golden crystals from the golden golems of late. Bring one of each of these items to Clontik, those may give him a better idea of how to solve his dilemma.'
This opens up the next three steps:
A Golemite Sagecrystal of Emergent Awareness may be of use to Clontik 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Go and find a vial of Cliknar command jelly... The Cliknar commander might be an obvious source 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Collect several Archtone wheelstick message devices from the Burynai 0/1 (The Underquarry)
A Golemite Sagecrystal of Emergent Awareness is dropped by an iron golem (usually only found deep in the southern tunnels, located around /way -482, -410, -607 and /way -526, -387, -589).
The Vial of Protaurian Command-Flavor Hivejelly is dropped by a cliknar sergeant (found among the cliknar in the east tunnels).
The Archtone Wheelstick is dropped by burynai plotters and mineblades.
These three items have been reported to be pre-lootable (hand to any NPC and hit cancel).
Deliver the Golemite Sagecrystal of Emergent Awareness to Clontik 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Deliver the vial of Cliknar command jelly to Clontik 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Deliver the Archtone wheelstick message device to Clontik 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Speak with Clontik Beardburner 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Clontik Beardburner'
Clontik Beardburner shakes his head. "So it's more than just obstinacy and unwillingness to follow my orders in Brell's absence. Examining the information these items contain, it's clear that these creatures have all been convinced that their lives have special meaning, that their purpose requires their own input and acquiescence. Individuality has been given to them. Once given, it cannot be taken away. I fear the Underquarry may have been changed forever; Brell may never get it back the way it once was."
210 platinum
Experience (2.2 AAs at Level 85)
20 Brellium Tokens
The task "Overheated Production" opens up after completing this task.Rewards:
- Brellium Token [item=88944]

# Overheated Production

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task begins with Clontik Beardburner who is located in the zone-in area of the Underquarry (location -15, 150, -193).
There are pre-requisites for requesting this task: "Chaos in the Stonequarry" + "Unrest in the Metal Mines", followed by "An Alternative Explanation".
You say, 'Hail, Clontik Beardburner'
Clontik Beardburner says 'Eh? Who are you? You don't belong here! Well, seeing as how there are so few of us left still devoted to carrying out the will of Brell Serilis, it would only harm our cause to waste effort having you [moved] from this place.'
You say, 'Moved?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'This is the Underquarry; we mine and quarry material here, endlessly. Stone, precious metals, marble, you name it. At the moment, however, things are in a disastrous state. My stone-mining crews are being insubordinate, the Burynai are claiming the copper mine for themselves, there are Cliknar invading the silver mine, and everyone I've sent to the gold mines has yet to return. I [fear] the entire Underquarry may spin completely out of control soon, and if our production isn't corrected, there will be terrible ramifications for the entire Underfoot and all the worlds that connect to it.'
You say, 'Fear?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'So much depends on me, but ever since the influence of this Church of Self began to be a problem, more and more of my Underquarry crew have failed to follow my orders! Being assigned to the Underquarry is the highest of honors a Dwarf or even a Gnome could ever hope to achieve! Why would one risk losing the favor of Brell by abandoning one's sacred duties? Yet that is precisely what has happened. I, and these few who remain here, are all that are left who remain dedicated in service to Brell. Yet it is strange, Brell no longer appears, or has not been seen or heard from in a very long time. I may need your [help] if we are to restore the Underquarry to its proper functioning.'
You say, 'My help?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'Much to do, much to do. Your help would genuinely be appreciated. I will need help with restoring order in the [Stonequarry] as well as in the [Metal Mines]. When you have completed these tasks I have [other work] I need completed.'
You say, 'What other work?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'There are some follow-up tasks to be performed now that I've been getting feedback from the rest of the Underquarry. My plan for restoring order has [failed]. The Underquarry is overproducing quantities of stone and metal that just aren't needed, and we will have to find a [solution] somehow.'
You say, 'A solution?'
Clontik Beardburner says 'I must abandon my plan to impose my will upon the denizens of the Underquarry, though it is within my rights to do so. I will instead ask you to negotiate with the two senior foremen. Kronidar Stoutaxe supervises the metal mines, and Drenkar Darkhammer is in charge of the stone quarry. Both are very proud and tireless Dwarves, who don't believe a word of what my previous messengers have been telling them. Someone with the initiative to think creatively will need to go to them and work out an [agreement].'
You say, 'I'll see about this agreement'
Clontik Beardburner says 'Speak with Kronidar and Drenkar and find out what it will take to get them to shut down the Underquarry's production for awhile.'
You have been assigned the task 'Overheated Production'.
Hail Kronidar Stoutaxe 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Kronidar is at approximate location 130 -455 -197.
You say, 'Hail, Kronidar Stoutaxe'
Kronidar Stoutaxe says 'Kronidar Stoutaxe at your service, chief foreman of metal mining in Brell's Underquarry I am. Clontik's story of a disaster in the making with our production level is amusing. We have never had a problem in the Underquarry, ever. While the reports are disturbing, as is Brell's disappearance, you'll have to bring me solid evidence that there is a problem before I can take such a dramatic step as shutting down our metal production. Only if you can find evidence that the Burynai have become hostile, or that the golems have become unreliable, would I agree to make so bold a move.'
Kill 10 rebel burynai 0/10 (The Underquarry)
Collect 5 undertone wheelsticks 0/5 (The Underquarry)
These are known to drop from burynai tunnelblades, caveblades, and tricksters.
Collect 5 golemite sagecrystals of iterave progress 0/5 (The Underquarry)
These are known to drop from brass and silver golems.
"Collect" items in this task may be pre-looted (if task doesn't update, attempt trading with Kronidar and hit cancel to update the task).
Deliver undertone wheelsticks to Kronidar 0/5 (The Underquarry)
Kronidar Stoutaxe says 'I am shocked that the Burynai could have become involved in this trouble. Yet their messages you have brought me indicate that not only have deep and disturbing changes taken place in our fellow creatures, but that they are acting autonomously and digging up metal ore to their hearts' content, defiant of our pre-determined production schedule. At the rate they are digging, and with Brell no longer regulating things, I wouldn't be surprised if the entire Underfoot ends up choked in unwanted ore in a matter of days. Perhaps they might even dig deep enough to open a breach into...somewhere else.'
Deliver sagecrystals of iterative progress to Kronidar 0/5 (The Underquarry)
Kronidar Stoutaxe says 'It is indeed difficult to understand how the golems could have become unreliable. If the other information you have been asked to present is in order, then we must shut down the metal mines at once. It will take time, but I think I can get at least the dwarves and gnomes to stop mining metal. But there is something I hadn't thought of: where will they put their pickaxes when they stop work? We'll have to build pickaxe racks, I suppose. Find me some pieces of wood, and I'll try and locate a carpenter somewhere.'
Collect a wooden pickaxe slat 0/4 (The Underquarry)
These are in the wood boxes spread around the zone.
Deliver a wooden pickaxe slat to Kronidar 0/4 (The Underquarry)
Kronidar Stoutaxe says 'Thank you, these will give us enough wood for our first pickaxe rack. Imagine, we of the Underquarry putting aside our pickaxes for once. What will we do with our time?'
Hail Kronidar Stoutaxe 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Kronidar Stoutaxe'
Kronidar Stoutaxe says 'I believe you and Clontik; I will shut down the metal mines at once, as soon as Drenkar agrees to shut down his stone quarry. I wouldn't want him to remain in production, and make me look like the worrisome old fool; he would never let me forget that if we live for all eternity!'
Hail Drenkar Darkhammer 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Drenkar is found at -592 -176 -260.
You say, 'Hail, Drenkar Darkhammer'
Your task 'Overheated Production' has been updated.
Drenkar Darkhammer says 'So you say Clontik has a problem with us doing what Brell brought us here to do? Maybe he should come down here himself so I can laugh in his face. But I have seen some mildly disturbing changes in the routine lately, including your presence here. If you can bring me solid proof that all is not well with my stone quarry, for example, that our genati aren't performing their tasks as they are supposed to, then I shall revise my thinking and take Clontik's news more seriously.'
Kill 3 surly genati 0/3 (The Underquarry)
Collect genati ore-stash maps 0/3 (The Underquarry)
Collect genati satchels 0/3 (The Underquarry)
As with the previous items, these are pre-lootable.
Deliver genati ore-stash maps to Drenkar 0/3 (The Underquarry)
Drenkar Darkhammer says 'Let me have a look at these genati maps. Why would the genati be stashing ore in the tunnels... no, no, NO, this is all wrong! Why are they behaving this way? Where did they even get time to make such maps...? All of this is... is... why, it's against Brell's Way!! We must have order here. I am loathe to admit it, but perhaps Clontik is right, and the Underquarry is in serious trouble. If I am not even getting good information on what is happening right under my very nose, then something has indeed gone very, very wrong down here.'
Deliver genati satchels to Drenkar 0/3 (The Underquarry)
Drenkar Darkhammer says 'There are bits of metal ore in these bags! Whichever genati were using these bags, were supposed to be moving STONE! Not METAL! Bah, I am losing my patience with these creatures, they have simple jobs to do and they simply stop doing them? Very well, Clontik, you worry-prone pile of calluses, you'll have your way today. Before I can shut down the stone quarries, I must have some metal pegs of some sort strong enough for my crews to hang their stone-cutting saws.'
Collect stonesaw pegs for Drenkar 0/4 (The Underquarry)
These are in the parts boxes spread around the zone.
Deliver stonesaw pegs to Drenkar 0/4 (The Underquarry)
Drenkar Darkhammer says 'These are good pegs, they'll support our saw blades. We've never had to retire or put down our stone-saws before, not once.'
Hail Drenkar Darkhammer 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Drenkar Darkhammer'
Drenkar Darkhammer says 'I shall shut down the stone quarry now. Go and tell Kronidar what I'm doing, I'm sure that old knuckle-wart is waiting to see if I'm actually going along with this plan.'
Hail Kronidar Stoutaxe 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Kronidar Stoutaxe'
Kronidar Stoutaxe says 'Very well, if Drenkar is shutting down production, then so shall I. Report to Clontik that the Underquarry's production has been halted, for now.'
Hail Clontik Beardrunner 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Clontik Beardburner'
As much effort as it took to get their cooperation in shutting down production, something which has never happened before in the Underquarry, I fear the delay may only be temporary until they run out of patience and start digging again. What else would they do with their time? They were made to dig. For now, hopefully the golems and genata will start removing the piles of unwanted ore from wherever they have been dumping them.
Clontik Beardburner says 'You must be a master negotiator to get those two to agree to anything, much less respond to an emergency that I warned them about.'
Reward:
315 platinum
Experience (3.3 AAs at Level 85)Rewards:
