Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This group task begins with Tenkar Everhot at the zone-in area of the Underquarry (location -15, 150, -193)._
_In order to request this task, you must complete a series of pre-requisites:_
Unrest in the Metal Mines
An Alternative Explanation
Overheated Production
A Clean Mine is a Safe Mine

---

You say, 'Hail, Tenkar Everhot'
Tenkar Everhot says 'Goodwill to ya. I trust you're not just another apeling from the surface, here to make mischief along with the other creatures that have [invaded] my beloved Underquarry?'
You say, 'They invaded?'
Tenkar Everhot says 'Aye, and we need an army to hold them off, an army we haven't got! Some of these other dwarves and gnomes are ready to start wringing their hands, but not me. I'll be fighting them off with my axe and hammer, till there's no more air in me lungs! Long centuries have I served Brell Serilis, and wherever he may have gone, I'll not give up an inch of his realm as long as I've sworn to protect it! But, I have to admit, [disaster] looms!'
You say, 'A disaster?'
Tenkar Everhot says 'We have only a limited amount of time for talking! We've got [duties] that must be done!'
You say, 'What duties?'
Tenkar Everhot says 'Take your pick. First, our negotiations with the Bellikos have broken down. We now suffer Lady Envy of the Bellikos pushing into the Underquarry with a clockwork army. I suppose we'll need to put a [mission] together to blast them out. If that was too tame for ya, maybe you could get together fifty or so adventurers and lead a [raid] into that first stone quarry of ours, something big has broken its way out of the lower mines and won't be restrained.'
You say, 'Let's hear about that mission'
Tenkar Everhot says 'The situation is dire, she'll be here any minute, I can almost smell her! If she completes her armored subordinate clockwork, her army will be indestructible, and the Underquarry will be hers. That must not happen! Will you [accept] the mission to drive her off?'
You say, 'I accept'
_Without pre-requisites:_
Tenkar Everhot says 'Look, I don't mean ta be rude, but I just don't trust ya. Go talk to that fool Clontik Beardburner. If you can convince him that you're not just some overgrown monkey, then maybe you'll be able to help me.'
_With pre-requisites:_
Tenkar Everhot says 'We have no reserves left to maintain order in the Underquarry, and we certainly can't afford to contend with another faction of belligerent squatters! I can hear Lady Envy coming with her clockwork armies. We don't have nearly enough dwarves or gnomes to stop them! Seek her out and see if you can work out an accomodation somehow, but the Underquarry must remain under the control of Brell Serilis, though he is not here to hear our pleas! If she remains unreasonable, look for something you can use against her, and resolve things the hard way, destorying the clockworks one at a time until she gives up. Tell me when your ready to [go].'
You have been assigned the task 'Scent of a Woman'.
Lady Envy has brought an army of clockworks into the Underquarry to fulfill her twisted megalomania. Clontik and the other dwarved and gnomes loyal to Brell Serilis will have no choice but to submit to her unless you can successfully intervene.
You say, 'I wish to go'
Tenkar Everhot says 'Find your way to Lady Envy; see if she will reveal a weakness somehow.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Underquarry - Scent of a Woman.

---

Confront Lady Envy regarding her plans for the Underquarry 0/1 (The Underquarry)
_Lady Envy is found in the room due east from the zone-in. There are a few KOS mobs you'll have to clear before you speak with her (including a see-invis genati)._
You say, 'Hail, Lady Envy'
Lady Envy says 'Well for you and these Dwarves and Gnomes, there shall be NO accommodation, NO mercy. We Bellikos came and explained very, very carefully the tenets of self respect, of free will, but always we have been met with the most unfortunate stubbornness from them. The time for consideration has passed. As soon as I complete repairs to my Subordinate, my armored army will crush them! Which reminds me, I must find only \*exact\* matches for the missing parts or he might just think for himself.'
Your task 'Scent of a Woman' has been updated.
_After hailing Lady Envy, the eight "Destroy" steps listed below open up. The "Loot" steps open up after the killing of individual named clockworks._
Destroy at least five of the Tantalum clockworks 0/5 (The Underquarry)
Destroy Ptoleus, the Tantalum clockwork coordinator 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Loot a Counterrotating Flywheel Piston from Ptoleus 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Destroy five of Lady Envy's Therium clockworks 0/5 (The Underquarry)
Destroy Trathon, the Therium clockwork coordinator 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Loot a Difference Machine Gearbox from Trathon 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Destroy five of Lady Envy's Periluminum clockworks 0/5 (The Underquarry)
Destroy Aurdred, the Periluminum clockwork coordinator 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Loot a Servogizmonic Manipulator from Aurdred 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Destroy at least five of the Galvanite clockworks 0/5 (The Underquarry)
Destroy Gracho, the Galvanite clockwork coordinator 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Loot a Pellucinite Cylinder from Gracho 0/1 (The Underquarry)
_The Therium and Periluminum clockwork groups are located in the eastern caves/tunnels. (Location for periluminums: -50, -1035, -208)_
_The Tantalum and Galvantie groups are located in the south/southwest caves/tunnels. Locations for map file:_
P 144.4714, 596.5150, -262.8911, 0, 0, 0, 1, Ptoleus\_/\_Tantalum
P 251.9012, 693.4111, -368.0259, 0, 0, 0, 1, Gracho\_/\_Galvanite
P 1096.0237, 137.0296, -224.1894, 0, 0, 0, 1, Aurdred\_/\_Periluminum
P 871.5342, -282.3821, -208.1207, 0, 0, 0, 1, Trathon\_/\_Therium
P 361.5594, 55.2577, -199.0996, 0, 0, 0, 1, Lady\_Envy
_You can invis through most of the zone, but there are a few see invis mobs to watch out for._
_The clockworks hit for a max ~7,000 and are unmezzable and unrootable. They are, however, stunnable. Each of the named drops an item to be turned in later._

---

Give the Counterrotating Flywheel Piston to the Unfinished Subordinate 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Give the Difference Machine Gearbox to the Unfinished Subordinate 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Give the Servogizmonic Manipulator to the Unfinished Subordinate 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Give the Pellucinite Cylinder to the Unfinished Subordinate 0/1 (The Underquarry)
_Upon handing in:_
an unfinished subordinate says 'I think, therefore, I am. I am a device of Brell Serilis. I am subordinate to my master Brell. What is happening here? For what purpose am I being used? This is not right. This is not right. I must correct matters, and defend the Underquarry from aggressors. I am very sorry, Lady Envy!'
Your task 'Scent of a Woman' has been updated.
Lady Envy says 'Kill!! Kill!!'
Lady Envy bites with rusty teeth.
Lady Envy says 'Kill!! Kill!!'
_Lady Envy attacks you. The clockwork attacks her._
Convince the Unfinished Subordinate to think for himself 0/1 (The Underquarry)
_As Lady Envy's health lowers, the clockwork stops._
Lady Envy says, "I expect ALL of my subordinates, finished or not, to obey my commands! And now, my disobedient clockwork servant, try to function after I remove your primary flange baffle!"
Lady Envy has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Lady Envy's corpse falls with a soft thump to the ground.
Your faction standing with Underfoot Autarchs got worse.
Your faction standing with Underfoot Citizens got better.
Loot and scrolls with orders or instructions from Lady Envy 0/1 (The Underquarry)
_Lady Envy drops an item called "Instructions"._
Give Lady Envy's instructions to Peramin Longbottle 0/1 (The Underquarry)
_Give the item to Peramin at the instance zone-in area to complete the task._
Peramin Longbottle says 'I can't believe my eyes; a few minutes ago I was prepared to face oblivion, the tearing apart of Underfoot at the steely claws of Lady Envy's clockwork army. Now I am fumbling around for an acceptable way to express my gratitude. Rest as much as you can for I fear the next crisis may be upon us soon. So many disturbances in the Underquarry may awaken Brath from his eternal slumber!'
Your task 'Scent of a Woman' has been updated.
So it appears that individuality works both ways. Once given an honest choice, the Unfinished Subordinate was able to place value on continued service to Brell, and reject the path of Self. The defeat of the Bellikos witch appears to have had some disturbing ramifications, however. . . is that [rumbling] from deeper in the Underquarry getting louder?

Experience

Modified: Wed Dec 18 07:26:49 2024 | | _This solo task begins with a sporali named Trullica in the Foundation. It's located at approximately 225, 1225, -202. It is the first of three solo tasks from Trullica._
You say, 'Hail, Trullica'
Trullica says 'Hello there. I am Trullica. I am pleased to see others from the [upworld] here. I hope you remain safe during your travels. Please, keep clear of the [clay pits]. They are no longer safe. It is most unfortunate because there are some that are in dire [need] of the clay. And don't get me started about the [Genari].'
You say, 'The upworld?'
Trullica says 'I too am from the upworld, though perhaps not as far up as you. I come from [Darkhollow] originally, though I find the Underfoot to be a much more interesting place.'
You say, 'Darkhollow?'
Trullica says 'Ah, yes, that entrance has been sealed since I came through. It's been a while since then, and the Cliknar have been actively blocking movement throughout the Underfoot -- their way of feeling like they are in control, I suspect. I discovered a crack in the Creep, a deep new fissure in one of the walls. So I explored. After a fair amount of harrowing adventure, I found myself in another plane! This place, the [Underfoot], is perfect: orderly, living, built of soil, stone and will. It just feels right to me. I've made this my new home, and nobody seems to mind. I've even managed to make myself [useful] here.'
You say, 'You think you're useful, do you?'
Trullica says 'Oh, yes. It's a rather long story that leads to the need for my unique skills. You see, when I first arrived the Cliknar were farmers. They tended vast fields, growing any foods needed in the Underfoot. Plants, you know. Few of Brell's creations need food, but those that do only eat plants. The Cliknar were the providers for those that needed it. That was their purpose, their place in the Underfoot. But then something [happened].'
You say, 'What happened?'
Trullica says 'I never have fully understood what caused it, but they slowly changed. They stopped tending their farms and started acting oddly. It seemed almost as if they were experimenting with other options. Like a sporeling that has not been shown the way of things. They would wander aimlessly. They would pick apart things to look inside. They would dig tunnels just to see where they would end up. They stopped doing what they were meant to do, and since then the whole of Underfoot has been [off center].'
You say, 'Off center?'
Trullica says 'Very much so. You see, the Underfoot is Brell's great machine. The creatures here don't 'live' as you or I do. They don't get up in the evening and decide what they will do that night. They always know what they should be doing. Their lives are fulfilled by doing what they were created to do. The Genari carry heavy stone for decades until they grow large enough and solid enough to take their place in the Foundation holding up mountains. The Greken push the lava, circulating it from the Underfoot to countless other realms that need its lifeblood. The Cliknar grow things and deliver them to those that are hungry. At least they once did that. Since they removed themselves from the workings of the Underfoot, all sorts of [unusual things] have been going on.'
You say, 'What sorts of unusual things?'
Trullica says 'Other creatures have broken away. While some of the Burynai refuse to work, others have started destroying things. Individual golems have been found standing in corners or repeatedly walking into walls. Even some Genari have been seen just sitting, staring at walls or aimlessly wandering the Underfoot. But the Cliknar are the worst. I am not certain, but perhaps it has something to do with their queen. All of them have gone mad. And without them there is [no food] in the Underfoot.'
You say, 'No food?!'
Trullica says 'Ah, you see, that's where I come in! When I saw what was happening, I realized that there weren't any creatures in the Underfoot other than the Cliknar that had a clue about farming. Sure, some of the Gnomes had gardens when they were on Norrath, but that's just not the same thing. Me, I'm a farmer. I took it upon myself to organize a new farm here in the Foundation. I don't have the magical powers that the Cliknar [had], but I'm good at [growing things].'
You say, 'What about that power the cliknar had?'
Trullica says 'They lost their connection to the Underfoot when they rebelled, and so their power to grow was also lost. They do seem to have learned other skills since then. Unfortunately, most of them involve violence.'
You say, 'You're good at growing things?'
Trullica says 'All I really had to do was choose some plants that grow well underground and gather up some of the gnomes and dwarves here, along with the few of my people that remain free, and start a farm. We can't keep up with the needs of everyone without using some of that amazing [Clay of Cosgrove] in our soil, but we manage.'
You say, 'Clay of Cosgrove?'
Trullica says 'Wonderful stuff! Very small amounts blended with the soil will help plants grow at an astonishing rate. I hear that it is what Brell uses to build all of his creations, even the first Dwarves and Gnomes! Simply amazing.'
You say, 'Tell me about the clay pits'
Trullica says 'The pits are where the [Clay of Cosgrove] is harvested. It used to just sort of ooze up from the pits in plentiful amounts, but since things got [off center] here the pits have sort of dried up. Some clay still comes out, but not nearly enough. And now those annoying Cliknar have started stealing it! I wonder, would you consider helping me [find out] why?'
You say, 'I'll find out'
Trullica says 'Well, I'm pretty sure that I know why: They want to use it to create things. But the problem is, for them at least, the clay won't respond to them anymore. For them it's just clay. They really have no need for it, and we need it desperately. So I'd like your help trying to get some of it back and perhaps finding out exactly what they want it for. I can't ask the creatures of the Underfoot to help; they just don't understand what I want. Oh, the Dwarves and Gnomes do, but they worry that they would be breaking the rules to harm any of Brell's creations. I assure you that the Cliknar are separated from Brell and are harming the Underfoot. Will you [help stop them]?'
You say, 'I'll help stop them'
Trullica says 'Thank you!'
You have been assigned the task 'Cliknar Clay'.

---

Deliver 4 Stolen Blob of Clay to Trullica 0/4 (The Foundation)
_This is a drop from cliknar scouts, soldiers, and hunters in the western part of the Foundation._
Speak with Entricalla 0/1 (Arthicrex)
_Entricalla is a sporali locked in a cage at a sporali farm in Arthicrex. Approximate location of the farm: 720, 1280, 5. There are numerous see-invis mobs in this area, so be on the lookout._
You say, 'Hail, Entricalla'
Entricalla says 'Hello. I am sorry, but I haven't the strength to greet you properly. I will have to be quick. I am a trusted prisoner here, but I cannot risk being seen with you. Trullica is of the same spore as I, and I sense his spores upon you. So I will trust you. The insects are indeed trying to modify the clay. They feed it to us and to the aphids, hoping that our bodies will give the clay a greater organic quality. All it does with us is make us ill, but within the aphids is does seem to change. They haven't worked it out yet, but if you seek samples you will need to kill some of the poor mindless aphids and steal some of their dung from the Cliknar farmers. Good luck.'
Deliver 3 Aphid Corpses to Trullica 0/3 (The Foundation)
Deliver 6 Sacks of Aphid Dung to Trullica 0/6 (The Foundation)
_The above items drop from aphid mobs in the northeastern part of Arthicrex (farmers drop the sacks; nymphs, drones, and mature aphids drop the corpses). They are pre-lootable. On handing in these items:_
Your task 'Cliknar Clay' has been updated.
_Rewards:_
105 platinum
Experience
~10 Brellium Tokens

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125

**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task begins with a sporali named Trullica in the Foundation. It's located at approximately 225, 1225, -202. It is the third of three solo tasks from Trullica; you must have completed the tasks "Cliknar Clay" and "Feet of Clay" in order to request this one.
You say, 'Hail, Trullica'
Trullica says 'Hello there. I am Trullica. I am pleased to see others from the [upworld] here. I hope you remain safe during your travels. Please, keep clear of the [clay pits]. They are no longer safe. It is most unfortunate because there are some that are in dire [need] of the clay. And don't get me started about the [Genari].'
You say, 'Genari'
Trullica says 'Since my time here, I've noticed that the Genari have been a [constant].'
You say, 'A constant?'
Trullica says 'Yes... They hold the mountains up and have done so for a very long time. They never [falter]... Never!'
You say, 'They never falter?'
Trullica says 'Something is causing them to waver. A little bit here, little bit there. Surely you've felt the ground [shake]!'
You say, 'I've felt it shake...'
Trullica says 'If a Genari moves, his load shifts. Unfortunately, this is the very bedrock that makes up this place as well as the upworld, so it causes the entire world to [move]. Perhaps those of us that have lived here for so long are more sensitive to the movements.'
You say, 'How can we stop that move?'
Trullica says 'If you can go where the Genari are and see what is causing them to lose focus, I am sure we'd be closer to [helping] them.'
You say, 'I'll see about helping them'
Trullica says 'Thank you.'
You have been assigned the task 'Rock the Worlds'.
Investigate the Genari in the northern part of the eastern section 0/1 (The Foundation)
Investigate the Genari in the southern part of the eastern section 0/1 (The Foundation)
Investigate the Genari near the waterfall east of here 0/1 (The Foundation)
Investigate the Genari in the central part of the central section of the east 0/1 (The Foundation)
The above four steps update as you walk around the giant statues in the given areas of the zone.
Kill 5 Autarchians 0/1 (The Foundation)
Several autarchian dwarves can be found in the eastern parts of the zone. Kill any five of them for this update.
Return to Trullica 0/1 (The Foundation)
Trullica says 'The Autarchians? Hmm. . . Something tells me that there is more to this. Thank you for your help. I have Dorillis working on some things for me if you'd like to assist.'
Reward:
ExperienceRewards:

# The Search for Saunk

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This group mission (3-6 players) begins with Darott in the Foundation. He is located far north of the zone-in, outside the burynai caves.
In order to request this mission, you must have completed Trullica's three solo tasks.
You say, 'Hail, Darott'
Darott says 'Hey, you. You help Adherent burynai like me, yes? We grateful. You do us another favor, we do something [nice] for you.'
You say, 'Something nice?'
Darott says 'You know Saunk? Young shaman, very powerful. He even more powerful now. We are [worried] about him, what he will do now.'
You say, 'Worried?'
Darott says 'Saunk a good friend, Saunk fight bad ones who follow bad ways. Saunk also fight cliknar. But Saunk [learn] bad things from cliknar.'
You say, 'What did he learn?'
Darott says 'Saunk find out what cliknar ants are up to, he eat their jelly-words and know their thoughts. Secret things only cliknar know, Saunk learned. They after the sacred Clay. They take Clay of Cosgrove, they do things to it to make their Queen smarter and stronger, then feed it to her. Saunk learn how to do this too. He [change] himself.'
You say, 'How did he change?'
Darott says 'Saunk make cliknar formula for himself, give himself many magic powers. Too many, if you ask me, if you ask Greph. He use powers to kill enemies attacking us. Then he use powers to kill enemies not attacking us. Then he use powers on rival burynai, then on his friends. Saunk lose mind, Saunk [betray] himself and other burynai.'
You say, 'How did he betray you?'
Darott says 'Saunk decide we all no more than bugs to him, he go somewhere else and make another Foundation where he most important person. Here where Darott need favor. Darott have enough power to find Saunk in place where he went. But Saunk not trust Darott anymore, and Darott not strong enough to defeat Saunk or stop Saunk from sending me back here to real Foundation. Darott stay in not-real Foundation long enough to see another Darott there, a shade of real Darott which is me, but is also not-me. He like me enough to help you know what to do when I [send] you there, which I hope is really soon, before Saunk's power grow to large and mighty and he finally decide to become a god and destroy us all.'
You say, 'Send me there'
Darott says 'I will use magic, I will send you to Saunk's not-real-Foundation place. Hold still, tell Darott when [ready]!'
You have been assigned the task 'The Search for Saunk'.
Darott the Adherent is sending you to the pocket-dimension his former friend Saunk has created for himself. You must find him and destroy him if necessary to keep him from becoming even more powerful!
Speak with the Shade of Darott, once you have been ported into Saunk's demi-plane. The Shade will tell you how to get Saunk to appear.
Say "ready" to zone into an instanced version of the Foundation. No mobs are in aggro range of the zone-in point.
You say, 'Ready!'
Darott says 'Okay, your skin start tingling, and soon you go 'whoosh' into not-real-Foundation. Say hello to Saunk, if you can get him to talk and be nice again.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Foundation.
Speak with the Shade of Darott; find out how to find the real Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
You say, 'Hail, Shade of Darott'
Shade of Darott says 'I am glad you came here. I am the Shade of Darott. I suspect I truly exist not. Here in this place, this mirror-image of Foundation, Saunk reigns supreme. See around you the other Shades -- copies of creatures Saunk knew or remembered from his days in the real Foundation. They act like the real thing, but they, like everything else here, are but figments of Saunk's imagination. You not find Saunk as he meditating, gather power for the next step towards godhood! He impatient with visitors even if he know you're here, send avatars to kill you or drive you away. If you destroy enough of his shades, maybe he take notice. Destroy shades of adherents, autarchians, cliknar, and bellikos, that enough to make him mad.'
Kill the Shades of Burynai; they aren't real 0/24 (The Foundation)
Burynai Adherents are found throughout the zone-in area and at the clay production area of the regular Foundation zone, while Autarchians are found in the eastern half of the zone in camps of 3-4. There is a total of 24 spawns in the zone.
Kill the Shades of Bellikos Emissaries; they aren't real 0/3 (The Foundation)
Two bellikos are found in the autarchian camps. A third bellikos is found in the easternmost part of the zone.
Kill the Shades of Cliknar Warriors; they aren't real 0/24 (The Foundation)
The cliknar are located in the western caves.
Tell the Shade of Darott the false citizens of the unreal Foundation are now gone 0/1 (The Foundation)
You say, 'Hail, Shade of Darott'
Insignificant beings. How dare you disturb my menagerie! This place was to be my refuge, my playground, where I would repose until I learned what to do with my former brethren. As for you, you do not even belong in Underfoot at all, I shall test your mortal resilience with destructive force!
Shade of Darott says 'Yes, you done it now. Darott sense rumbling in not-real-Foundation, Saunk now awake and alert, wonder what happening. You face first avatar of Saunk now. Be careful. They powerful.'
Your task 'The Search for Saunk' has been updated.
From here on, you'll have to defeat seven versions of Saunk, each one more difficult than the previous. You'll have time to med between fights as each spawns in a different location in the zone.
Named locations:
- First Avatar: +502 -9 (northern boundary of the eastern half of the zone)
- Second Avatar: -53, 97 (central area of the eastern half of the zone)
- Third Avatar: -555, 255 (southern boundary of the eastern half of the zone)
- Fourth Avatar: -130, 1015 (central area north of the Underquarry zone)
- Fifth Avatar: 100, 1190 (northern half of the western half of the zone)
- Saunk the Shaman: -200, 1800 (near zone-in)
- Genati of Saunk: Very close to -200, 1800 (zone-in area)
Locations for map file:
P 10.2464, -495.8269, -150.4684,  0, 0, 0,  1,  First
P -93.8467, 46.4823, -133.0777,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Second
P -253.4342, 555.2084, -142.7878,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Third
P -1013.9325, 121.0205, -145.4890,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Fourth
P -1196.7479, -106.1445, -201.9860,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Fifth
P -1854.3297, 242.6473, -210.0227,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Saunk_the_Shaman
P -2101.0005, 322.1855, -210.1438,  0, 0, 0,  1,  Genati_of_Saunk
The first named hits for ~5,500. They escalate in strength up to the final named which hits for a max ~8,500, casts a self-buff and silence:
Genati of Saunk has a self-buff and a silence:
Resolve of the Genata: Self 0', Unresistable (0)
1: Increase Absorb Damage by 250000
2: Increase Hitpoints by 2500
Brazen Hammerblow: Single Target, Magic (-600)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 3600
2: Unknown #380 (100/2/0)
3: Add Effect: Brazen Hammerblow Stunned Silence
Speak to the First Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
First Avatar of Saunk says 'You think to test my fighting spirit, my stamina? I assure you, this is a mistake.'
Destroy the First Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
First Avatar of Saunk has been slain by _____!
So you are indeed powerful, and not to be trifled with. So be it. I have many avatars. These are made from the former bodies of my friends, whose minds I destroyed and replaced with copies of my own will. I am so much more now than I was. You can't even grasp the experience of amassing so much power and growth in so short a time. I have increased my intellect a thousand-fold from only a few days ago, when I was barely smarter than the average burynai! Now I and my avatars stand on the brink of godhood!
Speak to the Second Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Second Avatar of Saunk says 'Destruction is your only reward, you have chosen a poor opponent if you were expecting anything else!'
Destroy the Second Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Second Avatar of Saunk has been slain by _____!
You destroy my avatars so casually, not realizing that they grow stronger! There is only one outcome to this contest, and it shall be your end. I, Saunk, shall prevail and rise to godhood, the first everlasting burynai god!
Speak to the Third Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Third Avatar of Saunk says 'I am no longer burynai, but a god. I am multiple beings, and yet one. My power grows with each passing second!'
Destroy the Third Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Third Avatar of Saunk has been slain by _____!
Perhaps you persist in killing my avatars to learn more about how I achieved this incredible power, and escaped the fetters of my mortal burynai existence. Perhaps you are merely foolish, and have extended your reach far beyond your grasp. The secrets of power are locked within the Clay of Cosgrove, which the cliknar have succeeded in discovering, within days of the disappearance of Brell Serilis. Their thinker, Qeyn, was considering the recipe for the extract. I simply intercepted it. It was astonishingly easy to make. Alas, you will die before you learn the secret yourself. A god tolerates little in the way of competition!
Speak to the Fourth Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Fourth Avatar of Saunk says 'The secret of the cliknar has given me strength, soon I will re-order your world! I shall rule the planes! Soon enough, there will be nothing I cannot do!'
Destroy the Fourth Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Fourth Avatar of Saunk has been slain by _____!
Pitiful. You cannot destroy me! Avatars may fall, but my essence is imbued into the very reality here, all around you. I am godlike, and you are not. Dare you tear down my works, my greatest avatars? After I have defeated you, I shall alter you, and make you my pets!
Speak to the Fifth Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Fifth Avatar of Saunk says 'You are too troublesome to tolerate any longer. The destruction of my avatars is an inconvenience, yes, and now an irritation as well.'
Destroy the Fifth Avatar of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Fifth Avatar of Saunk has been slain by _____!
Your task 'The Search for Saunk' has been updated.
Fine. Destroy my avatars. Break my concentration. I will have no choice but to meditate on the next stage of my escalating godhood later.
Speak to Saunk the Shaman 0/1 (The Foundation)
Saunk the Shaman says 'You are the stem, and I am the razor. Fall on your knees and worship me, or meet your peril!'
Kill Saunk the Shaman 0/1 (The Foundation)
Saunk the Shaman has been slain by _____!
Your faction standing with Underfoot Autarchs got worse.
Your faction standing with Underfoot Citizens got better.
WHAT HAVE YOU DONE! You've destroyed my original body! I shall have vengeance! You've trapped my consciousness inside my last avatar, the body of a genari! I will destroy you utterly for this outrage!
Genati of Saunk spawns nearby. Although you've made him quite angry, he doesn't attack. You can still med here if needed prior to engaging.
Kill Genati of Saunk 0/1 (The Foundation)
Genati of Saunk has been slain by _____!
Your task 'The Search for Saunk' has been updated.
Your shared task is now locked.  You may no longer add or remove players.
Speak to the Shade of Darott to return to the real Foundation 0/1 (The Foundation)
You say, 'Hail, Shade of Darott'
Shade of Darott says 'You defeat Saunk? You truly powerful! Saunk gone, this place crumble soon, it not stay solid without Saunk's power. You tell real-Darott I say 'goodbye'. Here, I send back to real-Foundation now.'
Your task 'The Search for Saunk' has been updated.
You have succeeded in killing Saunk the Shaman before he could become truly godlike. In doing so, you have saved Underfoot and probably Norrath as well.
You can hail him again to zone out (note: there is a roaming genati who sometimes wanders into aggro range of the zone-out point).
A chest spawns with loot (1 item per task). Known drops:

20-30 Brellium Tokens
105 platinum

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _Rank II spells in Underfoot are obtained through Trade-In gems which drop in various places throughout the expansion._
_Holdegar Bairdun is a dwarf found just east of the zone-in of Brell's Rest (entering from the Hole or Guild Hall portal)._
You say, 'Hail, Holdegar Bairdun'
Holdegar Bairdun says 'Well met! I am Holdegar Bairdun, last of the Bairdun line of gem-polishers. I spend my blissful eternity here in Brell's Rest practicing my trade, which means polishing up the green magic gems that are sometimes carried around by denizens of the Underfoot. If you could, er, cajole a few of these gems away from their owners somehow and bring them to me, I'll give you an improvement to one of your advanced magic spells.'
_Rank II spells (or in this case, Trade-In Gems) seem to be localized to certain zones or NPC types (much moreso than in previous expansions). Known drop data can be found on the gem item pages._
_Upon handing him a "Trade-In Gem: \_\_\_\_\_ Rk. II":_
Holdegar Bairdun says 'A fine specimen, yes, I wish I had more to give you in trade but all I have is this spell scroll.'
_In return, you receive a No-Trade Rk. II spell._

Entered: Fri Dec 18 16:17:17 2009
Modified: Sat Feb 3 18:13:17 2024 | | Rank III spells in Underfoot are obtained through Trade-In gems which drop in various places throughout the expansion.
Dwekvard Gravelpick [npc=34044] is a dwarf found just west of the zone-in of Brell's Rest (entering from the Hole or Guild Hall portal).
You say, 'Hail, Dwekvard Gravelpick'
Dwekvard Gravelpick says 'You have the honor of speaking with Dwekvard Gravelpick, one-time rune advisor to none other than Ugen Proudhammer himself! I've found eternity with Ugen in the Shining City of Kernagir to be a bit stifling in Brell's absence, shall we say. I'm now here in Brell's Rest for a time, practicing my study of Underfoot runestones. Bring me one of these if you should find one during your raids into Underfoot, and I'll give you a premium improvement to one of your advanced magic spells.'
_There are three types of runes:_
_Scintillating Obsidianite Runes (dropped from lower tier raids in the Underfoot expansion) which are used for level 83 spells and tomes._
_Sparkling Precandescent Runes (dropped from mid tier raids in the Underfoot expansion) which are used for level 84 spells and tomes._
_Twinkling Hyperfiligreed Runes (dropped from Convorteum raids) which are used for level 85 spells and tomes._
**Please let us know, only what you can confirm firsthand, the order of spells you receive and which rune type was used to obtain them.**

# Mardan #2: For Those Who Have Fallen

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task is the second of three tasks that begin with Mardan Howlinblade in Arthicrex (location: -260, 940, 5 -- outside the entrance to Fungal Forest). It follows his first task, "Abandon All Hope".
You say, 'Hail, Mardan Howlinblade'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'I am surprised you are still here! With us being at such low [numbers], the Cliknar will surely attack. It is an advantage that I would exploit if I were them.  We must gather reinforcements at once!  We could also use another hand searching for a more recent group of [missing] dwarves that have not returned, since you have shown experience in such matters already.'
You say, 'Low numbers?'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'It will be very difficult to survive for long without additional help.  I need someone to go to the dwarves of Underfoot and get them to send us more [support].'
You say, 'Support?'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'Find Haeger Stonecracker in Underquarry, Borgar Rompinstomp in Foundation, and Foreman Gronkin in Cooling Chamber. They should be willing to listen to you and be in a position to send us some help.'
You have been assigned the task 'For Those Who Have Fallen'.
Severely undermanned, Mardan needs you to get the dwarves of the Underfoot to send him some additional men to support his campaign in Arthicrex.
The three "Talk to..." steps open up immediately. The kill/return steps open up once you've hailed the corresponding NPC.
Talk to Haeger Stonecracker in Underquarry to gain support for Mardan 0/1 (The Underquarry)
Haeger Stonecracker is located at the zone-in area of the Underquarry (-15, 150, -193).
You say, 'Hail, Haeger Stonecracker'
Haeger Stonecracker says 'Mardan needs help you say? Hmm... Well, we have much to do here... But if you can help clear some of the problems we are having here, I'll be happy to send some dwarves to assist Mardan.'
Kill 10 burynai 0/10 (The Underquarry)
Return to Haeger Stonecracker 0/1 (The Underquarry)
You say, 'Hail, Haeger Stonecracker'
Haeger Stonecracker says 'Ah! So you're back! You've done your part, I'll honor mine. Tell Mardan that I'll send some volunteers as soon as I can.'
Talk to Borgar Rompinstomp in the Foundation to gain support for Mardan 0/1 (The Foundation)
Borgar Rompinstomp is located due north of the zone-in to the Foundation from the Underquarry, among numerous gnomes and dwarves.
You say, 'Hail, Borgar Rompinstomp'
Borgar Rompinstomp says 'I told Mardan that it was way too dangerous to go to Arthicrex! Besides, we got too much going on here to deal with that. Perhaps if you helped clear out some of the pests, I may be inclined to help.'
Kill 5 small beetles 0/5 (The Foundation)
Kill 5 large beetles 0/5 (The Foundation)
Return to Borgar Rompinstomp 0/1 (The Foundation)
You say, 'Hail, Borgar Rompinstomp'
Borgar Rompinstomp says 'Hmm... Well, I suppose I have to honor my end of the agreement. They are fellow dwarves after all! We'll head out as soon as possible.'
Go to Foreman Gronkin in Cooling Chamber to gain support for Mardan 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
Foreman Gronkin is found at location 150, -18, 4.
You say, 'Hail, Foreman Gronkin'
Foreman Gronkin says 'Are you daft?! We've barely enough hands to deal with our own problems let alone bail out Mardan with his! It'd go a long way in me making a decision if you were to help out with the dead mucking about in the lower chambers.'
Kill 5 zombies 0/5 (Cooling Chamber)
Zombies are among the undead mobs found in the southernmost part of the zone.
Return to Foreman Gronkin 0/1 (Cooling Chamber)
You say, 'Hail, Foreman Gronkin'
Foreman Gronkin says 'You are diligent aren't you? Tell Mardan that Foreman Gronkin has sworn to bail out his dwarven hide yet again! We'll leave as soon as I round up a crew.'
Return to Mardan Howlinblade 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Mardan Howlinblade'
The dwarves have all pledged to send Mardan aid!
Mardan Howlinblade says 'Thank Brell!  Hopefully they arrive soon.  The [news] our scouts have gathered makes me fear that we'll need them before they are able to assist.'
210 platinum
Experience (2.2 AAs at Level 85)Rewards:

# Mardan #3: A Desperate Deed

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 01:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This is the third of three tasks that begin with Mardan Howlinblade in Arthicrex (location: -260, 940, 5 -- outside the entrance to Fungal Forest). It follows his first two tasks, "Abandon All Hope" and "For Those Who Have Fallen").
There is a one-hour time limit to finish this task.
At the end of the previous task you saw:
Mardan Howlinblade says 'Thank Brell! Hopefully they arrive soon. The [news] our scouts have gathered makes me fear that we'll need them before they are able to assist.'
You say, 'What news?'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'It appears that some of the Cliknar have discovered our predicament. They must be stopped before they inform the rest of the [hive]!'
You say, 'The hive?'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'Yes, yes... if the Queen becomes [aware of us], we are finished.'
You say, 'Aware of us?'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'I am surprised they are not already aware. Those foul beasts communicate so quickly, it's as if they share a mind. Perhaps the Queen is [too distracted] right now to hear them.'
You say, 'Too distracted?'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'It must be all the overworlders meddling with their farms. Har! I never thought I would praise their arrival to Brell`s home, but they may just save us! Quickly, you and your allies must [ready] yourselves! I can hear the cliknar approaching!'
You say, 'Ready!'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'Here they come! You must not let them escape, or the hive will learn of our weakness! For the dwarves!'
You have been assigned the task 'A Desperate Deed'.
Once you trigger the task, three cliknar spawn near the Fungal Forest teleporter and charge the person who triggered them.
Keep them aggro'd, otherwise they'll emote and fail the task:
Those fools have left us... Soon..the queen shall hear us.
At last the queen can hear us, Those helpless dwarves can not escape...
Slay the approaching cliknar agents! 0/3 (Arthicrex)
Kill the first wave (spawns up by the Fungal Forest teleporter).
Talk to Mardan 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Mardan Howlinblade'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'That was a close call...wait! I hear more coming!'
Repeat for a second wave (spawns east of Mardan's location).
Slay the approaching cliknar agents! 0/3 (Arthicrex)
Talk to Mardan 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Mardan Howlinblade'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'I'm glad you're on our side _____. I see another group! Don't let your guard down!'
Repeat for a third wave (spawns to the north, at the nearby wall).
Slay the approaching cliknar agents! 0/3 (Arthicrex)
Kill nearby cliknar to ensure that the state of the dwarves is no longer known among the hive mind  0/10 (Arthicrex)
Head into the hive and kill 10 cliknar.
Approach the main hive in the north to make sure no agents have escaped 0/1 (Arthicrex)
This update happens at /loc 1529, 1075, 50
Inform Mardan that the attack has been prevented 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Mardan Howlinblade'
Mardan Howlinblade says 'Friends, I am forever grateful for your help in such a time of need. We would have certainly perished without you.'
Mardan's previously empty eyes now glimmer with hope. As he puts his hand on your shoulder and slowly nods, you can feel the heavy burden he once bore fade away.
157 platinum, 5 gold
Experience (1.6 AAs at Level 85)
17 Brellium TokensRewards:
- Brellium Token [item=88944]

Entered: Mon Dec 21 03:43:46 2009
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This group mission begins with Grogald Elderhammer in Arthicrex (location: -1685, 425, 197 -- just inside the zone-in area). In order to request this mission, you must have completed Mardan Howlinblade's tasks._
You say, 'Hail, Grogald Elderhammer'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'Looks like I've found the right person, you're standing still long enough to listen to an exasperated [dwarf].'
You say, 'I'll listen to a dwarf'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'Of course I'm late arriving to Arthicrex, and found the cliknar completely out of control. Bad enough the various races are choosing sides in the confusion happening since Brell's presence vanished, but these cliknar intend to destroy everyone else but themselves. It's a [mutiny] against their creator! A severe penalty must surely be imposed upon them by the gods!'
You say, 'Mutiny?'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'The cliknar hive is apparently doing its own thinking now. I've had to fall back to this corner and hide from their patrols after losing three of my most stalwart cousins. The cliknar patrol regularly through here since they helped build the Temple of Self over there. Some odd creature, sheathed in dark cloth, also comes through here every once in a while. He supposedly lives across the gorge in that strange house. The cliknar don't bother the Temple's followers, yet. There seems to be an uneasy truce in place. The hive doesn't need to antagonize the Temple's people and risk unifying the rest of Brell's creatures against them. There is some word that the cliknar are up to some rather disturbing activities of [late], including raising sporali as livestock.'
You say, 'What activities of late?'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'From what little I can glean from the tales of Grazden Sporefoot, the cliknar are interested in the Clay of Cosgrove for some reason. They are harvesting sporali, not just for their flesh, but also for spores and compounds that they think will be useful in transforming the Clay of Cosgrove into the stuff they want. Needless to say, using other creatures in this way is abhorrent. It almost goes without saying that we don't wish to see the results of the cliknar [project]!'
You say, 'A project?'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'Here's how you can help me. Gather a force of your best fighters and go and see how you can help Grazden put a stop to this menace. I daren't try to move about too much myself, but you and your people might have a chance. Are you [willing] to help?'
You say, 'I'm willing'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'I'm most grateful. Speak to me again when you're ready, and I'll whisper the [way] to Grazden.'
You have been assigned the task 'To Serve Sporali'.
Grogald Elderhammer is sending you on a special assignment. The cliknars have established locations intended to breed sporali for food, but also to create alchemical potions which will alter the clay of Brell and turn it towards some sinister new purpose. This effort of theirs must be stopped at once!
_Say "way" to zone in:_
You say, 'Show me the way'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'Tell Grazden I'm sorry I couldn't do more, or do it faster. I've had a terrible time with burynai causing trouble and killing me mates in the Underquarry.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Arthicrex.

---

Find Grazden Sporefoot and learn the locations of the sporali farms 0/1 (Arthicrex)
_At the entrance to the northern half of the zone, just beyond the ravine and lava tunnel, you will find a sporali named Grazden Sporefoot. Location: -795, 145, 7_
You say, 'Hail, Grazden Sporefoot'
Grazden Sporefoot says 'Hello. I must not linger here long; I must be brief. The cliknar have descended into madness, it is true; they have also begun preying upon the sporali for nourishment. I cannot begin to tell you how disturbing it is to see them raising young sporali slaves as mindless livestock, and harvest their organs for use in their secret project. There is nothing you can do to stop them but through violent means. Locate and destroy the west sporali farm, kill its guards and leaders, and bring back any harvested materials to me.'
Your task 'To Serve Sporali' has been updated.
Go to the western sporali farm 0/1 (Arthicrex)
_This is the northwestern farm._
Kill four west sporali farm guards 0/4 (Arthicrex)
_Four west farm guards can be found on the surface of the farm._
Retrieve three samples of Sporochord Extract 0/3 (Arthicrex)
_/open the leatherbound chests to loot the three samples._
Kill Spore Collector Kerod 0/1 (Arthicrex)
Kill Mineralist Dek 0/1 (Arthicrex)
Kill Reguloid Koryx 0/1 (Arthicrex)
_The above three named are found at the bottom of the farm. They can be split from one another._
Deliver three vials of Sporochord Extract to Grazden 0/3 (Arthicrex)
Hail Grazden 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Grazden Sporefoot'
Grazden Sporefoot says 'Very good. You have destroyed the west sporali farm; the Cliknar will surely have difficulty replacing what they have now lost. Speak with me in just a moment, I will have more information for you.'
Your task 'To Serve Sporali' has been updated.
Hail Grazden 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Grazden Sporefoot'
Grazden Sporefoot says 'To effectively disrupt the cliknar plan to use sporali entrails to transform the Clay of Brell into something more useful to them, whatever that may be, you'll need to destroy the eastern sporali farm. It is located in a slightly better-defended portion of the Arthicrex. Long may those cliknar suffer who turned their hive onto such a destructive path.'
Your task 'To Serve Sporali' has been updated.
Go to the eastern sporali farm 0/1 (Arthicrex)
_This is the southeastern farm. The remaining steps play out in the same fashion as with the western farm._
Kill four east sporali farm guards 0/4 (Arthicrex)
Retrieve one sample of Toximorel Compound 0/1 (Arthicrex)
Kill Spore Collector Ki`Kivin 0/1 (Arthicrex)
Kill Mineralist Rotak 0/1 (Arthicrex)
Kill Reguloid Thornek 0/1 (Arthicrex)
_**Note:** The task locks as you complete the above-four steps (either as you kill the named, or as you loot the item: whichever of the above-four steps you last complete)._
Deliver one sample of Toximorel Compound to Grazden 0/3 (Arthicrex)
Grazden Sporefoot says 'Disgusting. Chunks of sporali bodymass treated with cliknar bile. I recoil at the thought of the poor abused sporali who gave his life to produce this item. You have, I trust, eliminated the entire farm?'
Hail Grazden 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Grazden Sporefoot'
Grazden Sporefoot says 'You have done well completing your tasks to destroy the sporali farms quickly. I was beginning to fear a strong cliknar patrol would get here before you. Please return to Grogald, and tell him that I am most pleased with your efforts.'
Your task 'To Serve Sporali' has been updated.
Hail Grogald Elderhammer 0/1 (Arthicrex)
You say, 'Hail, Grogald Elderhammer'
Grogald Elderhammer says 'Aye, a beautiful deed is a splendorous thing to behold. The Cliknar will be hard-pressed to rebuild those farms now that they are fighting so many of the other races in Underfoot. The slaughter of untrained sporali has ended for a time.'
Your task 'To Serve Sporali' has been updated.
You have successfully disrupted the sporali farms, and put a stop to whatever sinister alchemy the cliknars were using them for... at least, for now.

Entered: Wed Dec 30 15:31:32 2009
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task is the first of four in a task arc from Schelly Starflight in Kernagir, the Shining City. She is located at approximately 405, 60, -50 (first floor, northern building)._
You say, 'Hail, Schelly Starflight'
Schelly Starflight says 'I greet you on behalf of the council, outsider. I have heard much about your feats outside the Shining City. As I'm sure you are aware, Brell's presence has been absent for some time now. Shortly after this occurred though, I began to notice some [strange] things in Kernagir.'
You say, 'Strange things?'
Schelly Starflight says 'For the first time in our history, some of those who live here began behaving in a manner not consistent with the will of Brell. Usually it would be a minor issue such as not wanting to perform their assigned duties. With others it was more disturbing; they would become [secretive] and withdraw completely from the other workers.'
You say, 'Secretive?'
Schelly Starflight says 'At first these were isolated incidents, but as more time passed without Brell's guidance, the number of workers showing these symptoms began to increase alarmingly. As it stands now, our once brilliant city of Kernagir has been divided in two - those of us who still follow Brell's will and those who have succumbed to whatever [disease] has infected them.'
You say, 'A disease?'
Schelly Starflight says 'If not a disease then what could it possibly be? It spreads like a plague and if it is not checked, I fear the Shining City will be brought to ruin. Our attempts thus far to identify the source of this aberrant behavior have met with failure. We cannot wait for Brell to return. Given your efforts in the realms outside Kernagir, I think you may be our last hope. Will you [aid] us?'
You say, 'I'll aid you'
Schelly Starflight says 'I am greatly relieved that you will be assisting us in this matter. Unfortunately we have exhausted all our leads so you will need to start anew by gathering information. As an outsider, I think you may be more likely to succeed in your efforts than we were. You should begin by speaking to those who are acting strangely. They can be found all over the city. When you feel you have gained sufficient information to act on, return to me and we will decide your next course of action.'
You have been assigned the task 'Is it Contagious?'.
Schelly wants you to question various workers who appear to be infected with something that has been altering their behavior.

---

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | This solo task is the second of four in a task arc from Schelly Starflight in Kernagir, the Shining City. She is located at approximately 405, 60, -50 (first floor, northern building).
Pre-requisite for requesting this task: Is it Contagious? [quest=4978]
You say, 'Hail, Schelly Starflight'
Schelly Starflight says 'Greetings again Ogre. I am greatly disturbed by the words on this note. Although your previous questioning did not reveal anything pertinent, I would guess the problems with the workers are related to this note. I want you to continue investigating the cause of the odd worker behavior by finding where this [note] came from.'
You say, 'What note?'
You have been assigned the task 'Where Did It Come From?'.
Schelly Starflight hands you the Worn Note. 'I want you to take this to Chamberlain Scrimble. You can find him in the Arbiter's Chamber above us. Tell him I want a full analysis of the note to determine its origins. He will know how best to pursue this. Given your performance thus far, the council feels you can be trusted with this task. We look forward to the results.'
You receive Worn Note [item=86979].

---

1\. Take the Worn Note to Chamberlain Scrimble 0/1 (Kernagir)
2\. Listen to Scrimble's instructions on how he wants the note analyzed 0/1 (Kernagir)
Chamberlain Scrimble may be found at 382, 181, 56. Give him the Worn Note:
Chamberlain Scrimble says, 'So, the council wants a full analysis of this to determine its origins? Hmm...I think it will be best if we conduct two separate analyses. I know just the gnomes, Wimzig Manasieve will conduct a magical analysis, and Lindwiss Solvolysis will conduct a chemical analysis. Unfortunately this will require some {alterations} to the original note.
You say 'alterations"
Chamberlain Scrimble rips a piece from the left side of the note and another from the right. 'That should do it, Here are two pieces from the note. Take the left peiece to Wimzig. You can find him on the top floor of the armory, just northwest of the central tower. Take the right piece to Lindwiss. You can find her in the basement of the Forge, just north of the central tower/ Once you have the final analyses, you should bring them back to Shelly. Good luck!'
You receive Right Part of a Worn Note [item=86981] and Left Part of a Worn Note [item=86980].
3\. Bring the left part of the note to Wimzig Manasieve for magical analysis 0/1 (Kernagir)
Wimzig Manasieve [npc=34760] may be found at 1189, 902, 346, on the top floor of the first building to the west of true north. Give Wimzig the Left Part of a Worn Note.
Wimzig Manasieve listens as you explain what Scrimble requested. 'A magical analysis to determine the origin of the note? That shouldn't be a problem. I'll get to work right away.'
4\. Destroy the magical creatures spawned by the curse on the note 0/3 (Kernagir)
_Three mezzable an evil eye [npc=35116] spawn at Wimzig._
Wimzig Manasieve says 'Oh my!'
_They spawn right at him and do not aggro anything else in the room. If you die, they will repop when you approach Wimzig again._
5\. Speak to Wimzig and get the results of the magical analysis 0/1 (Kernagir)
_You receive Magical Analysis Report [item=86986]._
6\. Bring the right part of the note to Lindwiss Solvolysis for chemical analysys 0/1 (Kernagir)
Lindwiss Solvolysis [npc=34754] may be found at 972, 564, 136, at the bottom of building on true north (east of Wizmig's building).
7\. Pick up the reagent box Lindwiss stored in the freezer 0/1 (Kernagir)
_This is Box of Reagents [item=86983] \-\- a groundspawn at 167, 1004, 143, at the bottom of the building to the west of true south._
_Two dwarf assassins spawn upon picking up the groundspawn. Before doing this, it's recommended you kill the roaming mob that comes out of the room where you pick up the groundspawn. She is the only one that will aggro and you can fight right in the hall._
_If you fail, pick up the groundspawn again to make them spawn._
8\. Kill the assassins 0/2 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
_2x an assassin [npc=35117] spawn in the hall outside the door._
9\. Give the box of reagents to Lindwiss 0/1 (Kernagir)
Lindwiss Solvolysis says 'You were attacked? How dreadful! Let me get this analysis finished before anything else bad happens.'
10\. Speak to Lindwiss and get her results of the chemical analysis 0/1 (Kernagir)
You say, 'Hail, Lindwiss Solvolysis'
_Receive Chemical Analysis Report [item=86987]._
11\. Take the magical analysis report to Schelly 0/1 (Kernagir)
Schelly Starflight says 'Thank you for this report. I we eagerly await the second as Scrimble has advised me to expect two reports.'
12\. Take the chemical analysis report to Schelly 0/1 (Kernagir)
Schelly Starflight says 'Thank you for this second report. Now that we have both, the council will go over the data and decide on the next course of action. Please return shortly and I should have another assignment for you.'
You were able to get the worn note analyzed despite encountering some obstacles along the way.

Deliver 4 Sessiloid Shards to Lindwiss Solvolysis 0/4 (Kernagir)
Deliver 4 Lichenash to Lindwiss Solvolysis 0/4 (Kernagir)
Return to Lichen Creep 0/1 (ALL)
Search the Lodestone living quaters for anything unusal 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
_In Lichen Creep, there are portals laid out in a clockwise manner. These each lead to the room. Which portal you need to take may be random._
_You are looking for a Cryptic Scroll, which spawns in one of these rooms. The portal room known to have the scroll was the "one at about 2 o'clock" (if looking at the portals as a clock). Portal location: -1090, +568, 31._
_NOTE: At least one named mob in Lichen Creep is known to have a PH in one of the portal rooms, so there is a slim chance you could encounter a named upon porting up._
Return to Schelly with the scroll 0/1 (Kernagir)
You say, 'Hail, Schelly Starflight'
You managed to locate a scroll in lichen creep which contained useful evidence.
Schelly Starflight says 'What's this?' Shelly reads over the note. 'This is certainly a damning piece of evidence if we can ever discover who R.I. might be. The council will hold on to this scroll until additional evidence is located. Speak to me again when you are ready to follow the next lead.'
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Experience (2.2 AAs at Level 85)
40 Brellium Tokens
- Brellium Token [item=88944]

You say, 'Hail, Schelly Starflight'

---

Speak with Wimzig Manasieve 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
You say, 'Hail, Wimzig Manasieve'
Your task 'The Scrying Game' has been updated.
Wimzig Manasieve says 'Pleasure to see you again \_\_\_\_\_\_! So you wish to delve deeper into the curse on the note? Hmm...if you wanted to locate the exact spot where the curse was placed on the note, you would need to triangulate it using one of my scrying devices. Here you go. Just follow the instructions and you should have no trouble finding the location. Have fun out there!'
Find a suitable point near Brell's Rest 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
_Location: -193, -530, -12_
Use the Scrying Device near Brell's Rest 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
Find a suitable point near Fungal Forest 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)

Kill Sidara the Beguiler 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
Sidara the Beguiler says 'Rock Icewalker was right; he knew you wouldn't stay out of our business. When you triggered my curse on the note, I knew if I waited here you would show up eventually. Too bad you won't be leaving!'
Return the scrying device to Schelly Starflight 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
_On hand-in:_
You have learned that Rock Icewalker is involved in the corruption of workers in Kernagir.
Schelly Starflight looks stricken. 'Rock Icewalker? This is worse than I could ever have imagined! To think that one of the council is the source of this corruption is unthinkable, yet the evidence is compelling. We will have to bring this evidence before the council at once!'

# The Silent Schism

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This group task begins with Dvalger Quickwrench in Kernagir, the Shining City. He is located at approximately 645, 110, 55 (second floor).
To request this task, you must have completed Schelly Starflight's task arc.
You say, 'Hail, Dvalger Quickwrench'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'What a disappointment. Kernagir is not the place I [thought] it was. Some Shining City experience this turned out to be.'
You say, 'You thought it'd be...?'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'From the time we're wee little and still burning our hands on our family cookpot, we're told all about the Shining City of Kernagir and how wonderful it will be, even better than Brell's Rest. Brell takes only the smartest, boldest, and craftiest tinkerers and craftsmen into the Shining City when they are done with their mortal life. It is a place of supreme honor. Well, I've been here a week, and the place just isn't what was [described] to me.'
You say, 'How was it described?'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'Imagine having this ultimate reward described to you. Imagine working hard your entire life to reach that reward. Imagine dying and then waking up in Brell's Rest with your toolbox in hand, hardly daring to believe that the gatekeeper of the Shining City might actually let you inside, then experiencing the unqualified honor and joy of reaching that ultimate goal; eternal tinkering and breakfasts with Brell Serilis himself. Then finding out Brell is not here, and that the delightful eternal [laboring] I was looking forward to, isn't allowed to happen!'
You say, 'Laboring?'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'Yes, the enthusiastic pursuit of our dearest lifelong creative pursuits, and commune with the greatest genius tinkerers, engineers, and designers of all the ages. It is the reason we wish to come here. There are so many problems and quarrels and conflicts going on in the Shining City, that one can't even concentrate on their eternal endeavors, but instead fall into one or the other [faction].'
You say, 'The other faction?'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'Yes, the Dogmatics and the Chromatics. From what I have been able to gather, first Brell disappeared, then the Church of Self has sent emissaries here to remind people of their individuality. At that point the Hierophant of Architects decided to undertake a great project, which, when finished, would allow us to summon Brell. Then the arguments began among the engineers and architects and designers, some of whom insisted we consider solutions Brell had forbidden, others insisting that Brell's [vision] must be followed even in his mysterious absence.'
You say, 'Brell's vision?'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'The Dogmatics consider only what Brell would have wanted them to, and nothing else. They are starting to refer to the Chromatics as heretics. The Chromatics profess not the doctrine of the Self, yet they were certainly [influenced] to an extent by its emissaries.'
You say, 'How were they influenced?'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'It is so bizarre. All of us are here to do the will of Brell and spend eternity in his fellowship. All of our goals are the same, yet when he disappeared, it is as if instant arguments over little things became inevitable, as if the Hierophant of Architects doesn't realize that it is his job to settle these disputes. Say, I just had a great [idea].'
You say, 'An idea?'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'I'm not high enough profile to raise issues with the Hierophant or even get into his presence, but you are not bound by the same conventions, you are not a permanent resident, you have no risk of ruining your eternal existence here. Perhaps you could speak with the Hierophant and cause things to [change] in the Shining City.'
You say, 'I'll make a change'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'That would be a most excellent solution to a difficult and vexing problem. I wish I could say something to the Hierophant, but I can't imagine a more awkward conversation. [Go] and find the Hierophant, Ugen Proudhammer, in the uppermost floor of the Tower of Recognition.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Silent Schism'.
The eternal reward that worthy creatures of Brell receive in the form of admission to the Shining City is being ruined by quarreling and infighting within the Creator's Guild. You must restore order to the Guild before it descends into chaos.
To zone in:
You say, 'Ready to go!'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'Hurry, if the Creator's Guild rips itself apart, that will be the end of this place.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Kernagir - The Silent Schism.
Find the Creator's Guild headquarters in the Tower of Recognition 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Head up another floor or two until you come to the giant tower which takes up the middle of the zone. Go inside and all the way up the winding ramps to the top floor where you'll find Ugen Proudhammer (location 640, 665, 640).
Speak with Ugen Proudhammer about the concerns Dvalger raised 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
You say, 'Hail, Ugen Proudhammer'
Ugen Proudhammer says 'Right. You're who? Dvalger who? No matter. I can see you don't exactly belong here, but perhaps you can help us with some difficulties we're having with our Chromatic creatives. They think to resist my potent intellect and my wise decision-making. Well, they think wrong! They try, but fail, to produce an acceptable set of plans for the Audientium Orb. It is a concept for a magical device, that will allow me to speak to Brell Serilis, wherever he may have gone, and either convince him to return to us, or at least find out why he has departed and what we should do in his absence. A noble plan, ruined by that heretic Bvard, the malingerer Vardnek and the rest of those shiftless Chromatics! Tell him that my patience runs out, and that the revised Orb plans are needed NOW.'
Find Vardnek Tinkerheimer in the Tower of Recognition 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Vardnek is one floor below (location 755, 690, 558).
Relate Ugen's demands to Vardnek 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
You say, 'Hail, Vardnek Tinkerheimer'
Vardnek Tinkerheimer says 'Ah, I see you've met our master, the Creator's Guild Hierophant, Ugen Proudhammer. First of a mighty line of Dwarven geniuses, from the age before the age when the gnomes created steam. He never lets us forget that he invented the axe, but you want to know something? He hasn't had an original idea in ages, and since Brell left and no longer counsels him, he declares everything we do to be a horrid abomination. Bvard and I have redone these plans for the Audientium Orb a thousand and one times, but Ugen thinks only of how our work offends him. Perhaps it is because he envies our work. Perhaps it is because he cannot admit to himself that he is not as innovative as we are. I care not; I, Bvard, and the other Chromatics simply wish the endless revision to end, and the work on the Orb to begin!'
Accept three revised Audientium Orb plans from Vardnek; tell him you'll agree to distrubute them 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Hail him again to receive 3x "Revised Audientium Orb Plans". You'll need to deliver copies of these to Fnarda, Derv, and Ugen (all located on the top floor)
Deliver a copy of the revised Audientium Orb plans to Fnarda Scaldpot 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
On hand-in:
Fnarda Scaldpot says 'They have outdone themselves this time! This is perhaps the best success Vardnek and the other guildsmen have ever achieved! Oh, I do hope Ugen sees how wonderfully they've succeeded in fulfilling his goal!'
Deliver a copy of the revised Audientium Orb plans to Derv Mugglepocket 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
On hand-in:
Derv Mugglepocket says 'Wow, incredible! This must be Vardnek's team's finest work yet! Look at the added detail, the notes explaining the tiniest nuances of the Audientium Orb's purpose and design! Incredible, I say! It's now for Ugen to give it a look; I do hope he's in a better mood today.'
Deliver a copy of the revised Audientium Orb plans Ugen Proudhammer 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
On hand-in:
Ugen Proudhammer says 'Ah, the new plans, let me see. . . uh, no. Not at all good. Wretched, in fact. Worse by far than anything I could conceive on the worst moment of my worst day, while drunk and sick. An abomination!'
Talk to Ugen and find out what he doesn't like about the revised plans 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
You say, 'Hail, Ugen Proudhammer'
Ugen Proudhammer says 'Of course, this new version of the plans, well, it's just terrible. Like the more ignorant among the kobolds might have written it. Unacceptable. I won't tolerate this kind of sloppy attempt at design work! Take this back to that indolent Vardnek fellow, and tell him to do it over again, or I'll send people down there to see that it's done right if they have to start cracking heads. Also tell him I'm sick of hearing tales of woe about how I'm so unpleasant to deal with in Brell's absence. I was great in my day! I was a legend, greater than Vardnek, greater than any of them! By my shining brilliance alone does the Shining City get its inspiration!'
Give the bad news to Vardnek Tinkerheimer 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Note: Hailing Vardnek at this point will make all non-KOS mobs in the zone KOS. None of them see invis, so you can invis before doing so.
You say, 'Hail, Vardnek Tinkerheimer'
Vardnek Tinkerheimer says 'Oh, for the love of Brell! Ugen wants yet more changes? He'll never be happy with anything we give him, and he never tells us what he does want! If any of the other artisans, engineers, and tinkerers hear of this outrage, they will certainly mutiny. . . oh, no, too late.'
Kill five Heretic Artisans 0/5 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Kill five Heretic Engineers 0/5 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Kill five Heretic Tinkerers 0/5 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
These mobs hit for a max ~8,000; there is nothing overly special about them in terms of difficulty or unique abilities.
Retrieve 15 Sacred Creator's Guild Amulets from the dead heretics 0/15 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
These have a 100% drop rate from heretics.
Deliver 15 Sacred Creator's Guild Amulets to Ugen Proudhammer 0/15 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Speak with Fnarda Scaldpot 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
You say, 'Hail, Fnarda Scaldpot'
Fnarda Scaldpot says 'Yes, I know Bvard is innocent of what Ugen accuses him of, but I haven't the will nor the courage to stand up and confront Ugen. He is the legend, and he rules this place in Brell's absence. What you must do is find Bvard and cause him to accept Ugen's new restrictions. Yes, it means Bvard will be eternally miserable, but that can't be helped.'
Talk to Bvard the Heretic and find out if there is a way to avoid further carnage 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Bvard spawns two floors below Ugen; one floor below Vardnek. He is alone.
You say, 'Hail, Bvard the Heretic'
Bvard the Heretic says 'You know, just as Ugen Proudhammer invented the notion of heretics trying to thwart his goals, to hide his own weakness as a master architect, so I too must reinvent myself as that heretic. None of us he labeled as heretics will ever have a moment's peace of mind as long as he stubbornly clings to this insanity. I, for one, choose not to live out the rest of eternity consumed by this aching resentment. I would rather pass to oblivion than be held forever as someone else's falsely-labeled threat. Do me the honor of killing me, if you please.'
Bvard immediately attacks.
Bvard the Heretic says 'If it's death ya seek, then I shall deliver it!'
Bvard the Heretic says 'It seems you came here to fight.  I hope ya got what ya wanted.'
Kill Bvard the Heretic 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Bvard hits for a max ~9,000 and is otherwise on par with the other mobs in the zone in terms of difficulty.
NOTE: There are conflicting reports as to when this task locks. Some say it's upon the death of this named; other says it's upon the hail in the next step.
Bvard the Heretic has been slain by _____!
Bvard the Heretic's corpse falls to the ground.
Tell Ugen Proudhammer of Bvard the Heretic's sacrifice 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
WARNING: This is where the task locks.
You say, 'Hail, Ugen Proudhammer'
Ugen Proudhammer says 'Bvard is dead! Bvard is dead? What have I done? When Brell returns, he will wonder what happened to so many of our artisans and engineers. I have done nothing but deny them their rightful reward, their endless pursuit of creation. All because I couldn't see that there could exist other viewpoints besides my own. Oh, I should die a thousand deaths! I should join my colleagues in oblivion, but alas, I will submit to Brell's own righteous punishment.'
The task locks.
Inform Dvalger Quickwrench that the Creator's Guild should run more smoothly now 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
You say, 'Hail, Dvalger Quickwrench'
Dvalger Quickwrench says 'You've settled the quarrel among the members of the Creator's Guild all right. Too bad so many of them had to die, again. Still, the silent schism would only have gotten worse until Kernagir itself would have been consumed in flame. That was an outcome worth avoiding. Oh, when will Brell Serilis return?'
Though Bvard certainly didn't deserve the fate he received, his selfless act of sacrifice nevertheless has returned a shaky peace to the Shining City. Of course, Brell knows all that happens in the Shining City, and if he returns, the Hierophant may find it uncomfortable to explain the deaths of so many 'heretics' whose souls now float in everlasting limbo.
An ornate chest spawns with loot (1 item per task). Known drops:
Algid Ice Cube
Backdigging Martel
Beguiler's Last Answer
Club of Spores
Discarded Wand
Dwarven Martel
High Priest War Staff
Jewel of the Flame Alchemist
Mad Sizentist Shield
Rager's Beating Stick
Rewards (everyone in task):
105 platinum
Experience (1.1 AAs at level 85)
~40 Brellium Tokens
Some classes (not all) also receive a spell or skill:
Bard - You'll get nothing and like it!

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task is the first of three tasks (plus a group mission) that begin with Gotonur, a telmiran in Brell's Temple (location: 655, -10, 21 -- in the large room north of the zone-in; same room as Brell Serilis)._
_Be on the lookout for wandering see-invis KOS mobs while speaking with Gotonur._
You say, 'Hail, Gotonur'
Gotonur says 'Greetings \_\_\_\_\_, welcome to the sacred halls of our creator Brell. I assume you are here to [investigate] the problem that has begun to affect your world above.'
You say, 'I'm here to investigate. What's going on here?'
Gotonur says 'Something has happened and we are still clueless as to the details. Our creator has been so focused on his project that he will not respond to anyone. There is [one] who may have some insight into the problem, but he has locked himself away inside the room behind me.'
You say, 'One?'
Gotonur says 'The Font Guardian was the last to interact with Brell before something happened and then he locked himself in the room behind me. The only way inside is by creating a key to open the door. We are not capable of retrieving the necessary part to create the key but you may be [able] to.'
You say, 'Of course I'll be able to'
Gotonur says 'Before I allow you to take on that challenge you must [prove] to me you can master the powers of creation. This is not a simple task and as such completing it will prove that you have a good chance of surviving the Vault of Stone.'
You say, 'I'll prove myself'
Gotonur says 'The first task is to prove you can master physical manipulation. You must gather parts from certain inhabitants in the Underquarry and Foundation, then build your creation in Lichen Creep. When you have done this you can return to me for your next task.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Laws of Physics'.
Gotonur wants you to gather parts from various beings throughout Underfoot and past the test of physical manipulation.

---

Collect a ball of dung from an odd looking beetle in the Foundation 0/1 (The Foundation)
_Odd looking beetles hit for a max ~8,000. They can spawn anywhere in place of "a small beetle"._
Collect 10 metal essence cores from metal golems in the Underquarry 0/10 (The Underquarry)
_These drop from golems in the southern caves of the Underquarry (southern tunnel from the large room with stone genaris and Goralf)._
Create a golem, activate it, and destroy it 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
>Head to Lichen Creep and right-click the dung. This creates an Inactive Corporeal Golem. Click this item around location -1280, 900, -100. You see:
You manipulate the ball of dung and essence cores in your hands. Contact with the dung makes it very easy to shape the cores into a rudimentary golem figure.
This spawns a Corporeal Construct (max hit ~8,500).
The golem begins to animate and attacks you on sight.
Kill this for the update.
Return to Gotonur 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
You say, 'Hail, Gotonur'
Gotonur says 'Your mastery of physical manipulation can be felt in the aura radiating from your spirit. It seems you were successful in your attempts, which is very good news. Hail me when you are ready for your next task.'
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Experience
~40 Brellium Tokens

Create a golem, activate it, and destroy it 0/1 (Fungal Forest)

Return to Gotonur 0/1 (Brell's Temple)

You say, 'Hail, Gotonur'
Gotonur says 'You have mastered the manipulation of both the physical and magical, and now it is time to put these two together and add the final ingredient of life. I warn you that continuing on from here is dangerous and only those who are are serious should attempt this [final] test.'
You say, 'What is the final test?'
Gotonur says 'The process of adding life involves imbuing an incorporeal soul with a physical body. This requires a portion of your own life essence. Hail me again when you have gathered some friends and are ready to take on this dangerous mission.'
