Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This group mission begins with Gotonur, a telmiran in Brell's Temple (location: 655, -10, 21 -- in the large room north of the zone-in; same room as Brell Serilis)._
_Be on the lookout for wandering see-invis KOS mobs while speaking with Gotonur._
_In order to request this task, you must first have completed Gotonur's three solo tasks._
You say, 'Hail, Gotonur'
Gotonur says 'Once again you have shown your desire to help find out what has been happening. But before everything can be revealed to you there is one more thing you must [do].'
You say, 'What must I do?'
Gotonur says 'At the center of this temple lie the Fonts of Creation. It is here where the process of life starts. Within the locked chamber lies the font guardian. In order to open the door one must make a keystone. The font guardian is the only one who knows why our creator has stopped talking to us but we cannot create a keystone as we have a problem retrieving the mold needed to create it. Can you [help] us?'
You say, 'I can help.'
Gotonur says 'When we built this temple we built another just like it deeper beneath the earth. It is here where we keep the keystone mold and some of the older more powerful creations to protect it. Unfortunately being kept locked away has weakened their connection to our creator and they have become [aggressive] to everything.'
You say, 'Aggressive?'
Gotonur says 'They will attack anything on sight which includes those of us who reside here. The only way inside the chamber is to retrieve the keystone mold and the only way to retrieve the keystone mold is through the halls that protect it. You have proven that you grasp the basic understanding of creation and it is this knowledge that is needed in order to make the keystone once you have retrieved the keystone mold. Do you think you can do it?'
You say, 'Yes.'
Gotonur says 'Thank you, \_\_\_\_\_\_. When you are ready, please say you are [ready] and I will open the gateway. I await your return with the keystone so we can finally get to the bottom of this dilemma.'
You have been assigned the task 'Fire In The Hole'.
You have agreed to assist Gotonur in making a keystone. In order to do this, you must brave the halls of the Vault of Stone, retrieve the keystone from the vault guardian, and bring it back to Gotonur so that he can make a keystone.
_To zone into the instance:_
You say, 'Ready!'
Gotonur says 'Be careful, \_\_\_\_\_. We are counting on your success.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered The Vault of Stone.

---

Retrieve a jagged shard from ancient colossi 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Retrieve a pulsing sphere from timeworn channelers 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Retrieve a chipped claw from cunning fiends 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_The above three items drop from said mobs. Trash in this zone hits for a max ~8,500. Everything sees invis and is tightly packed together._
_Only mischievous constructs and cunning fiends are mezzable. Everything else is immune to mez._
Unlock the door to the inner vault chamber 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_Whoever has the three items in their possession needs to click on the locked door to the north of the zone-in:_
The three pieces click into place and the sounds of several clicks emanate from the walls around it. There is a loud click and the door slides up revealing the next chamber.
Retrieve a Vial of Purified Water from the Protector of Purity 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Retrieve an Unstable Glob of Clay from the Protector of Clay 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_The above two mobs can be found in the rooms leading away from either side of the room you just opened. They hit for a max ~12,000._
Mix the purified water with the unstable glob of clay to create an unstable mass 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_Right-click the glob of clay to produce an unstable mass of clay._
The water and clay mix together seamlessly to create an unstable mass that looks like it could stick to the door very easily.
Use the unstable mass of clay on the door to gain access to the worshipping area 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_Whoever has the unstable mass in their possession needs to open either door leading deeper into the zone._
Retrieve a crumbling head from a crumbling construct 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Retrieve a broken torso from a broken construct 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Retrieve a cracking legs from a cracking construct 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Retrieve a worn arms from a worn construct 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Retrieve a pair of eroding feet from an eroding construct 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_The above five golem constructs can be found throughout the newly opened areas of the zone. They each hit for a max ~14,000._
Assemble the golem by attaching everything to the torso 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_Right-click on the torso to create a destructive golem._
Activate the destructive golem in the garden hall or pool hall leading to the keymaster chambers 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_Whoever has the destructive golem in their possession needs to head down to one of these halls and right-click the item. This spawns a non-KOS Destructive Golem which will then open the doors to the end room._
The destructive golem stands before the door and destroys the barrier before crumbling to dust. The path to the keymaster is now open.
Retrieve the vault key from the Key Master 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_The Keymaster hits for a max ~14,000. Whoever loots the key from its corpse can open the door to the Vault Guardian (due north from the zone-in)._
Defeat the Vault Guardian 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
_The Vault Guardian is perma-rooted, so you'll have to fight it in the room. It hits for a max ~14,000._
_**Note:** The task locks upon the Vault Guardian's death._
Retrieve the Keystone Mold from the Vault Guardian 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Return to keystone mold to Gotonur 0/1 (Brell's Temple)
Gotonur is stunned when you hand the keystone over. Recovering from the shock he pulls out a vial of brown liquid and begins to pour it into the mold. The mold begins to bubble and smoke and melts away revealing an odd looking rune. Gotonur hands the item to you and bows his head in acknowledgement. Gotonur says 'Take this and open the font chamber.'
_Everyone in the task receives a Keystone._
_"a stone chest" spawns with loot (1 item per task). Known drops:_
Battlestaff of Symbols
Beguiler's Last Answer
Brute's War Spear
Eyepiece of the Fancy Tinker
Naglebrak's Cleansing Tool
Orb of Suspension
Orb of Timeless Stone
Slime-Etched Breastplate
Slime-Etched Leggings
Tarnished Stone
Telmiran War Shield
_Rewards (everyone in task):_
210 platinum
Experience (2.2 AAs at Level 85)
~80 Brellium Tokens

Modified: Tue Nov 11 09:32:35 2025 | | This solo task begins with Adeena [npc=34813] in the Fungal Forest. She is a telmira located at -905, -850, 95 (southeastern area of the zone). Keep a lookout while speaking with her as KoS mobs wander the area.
You say, 'Hail, Adeenas'
Adeenas says 'Hello! I am Adeenas of the telmira. I see you must be lost. Perhaps you should get your bearings here before you further travel the Fungal Forest. Like most of the Underfoot, this place is dangerous to outsiders. This is more true if you venture further to the east, or if you choose to trifle with the sporali or cliknar. Perhaps you came by to [chat] with me instead?'
You say, 'Let's have a chat'
Adeenas says 'I love talking to new people! I get so lonely these days, since the sporali became violent and the cliknar invaded the forest. The other telmira have scattered to the winds, it seems, and I don't know what to do next. No matter. I have you to talk to now. You can remain here and talk with me [forever], yes?'
You say, 'Forever...'
Adeenas says 'Yes! You can remain here forever--if you'll let me transform you into a telmira like me with my special spell, of course; and whatever fate comes to the telmira, you will share. Does that sound [fun]? We can fly to the tops of mushrooms, skim the gullies of rivers, sing songs honoring the life of the forest, and eat fruit beneath the weeping spore caps.'
You say, 'No, that doesn't sound like fun...'
Adeenas says 'You mortals are all the same. What sounds like fun to a telmira only seems to scare you to death, yet you all seem to embrace the most terrifying and random type of existence. Well, you don't know what you're missing, that's all I can say! Since you're going to stick with your mortal experience of death and danger, how about helping me with a [favor]? Or did you already do that for me? If so, perhaps it's time to do something about [Rakkirin].'
You say, 'A favor?'
Adeenas says 'As I mentioned before, the other telmira kin have gotten separated by the recent disturbances in the forest. Brell's thoughts have gone away somewhere and not returned. While we long for his presence to come back and set these matters right, I fear I cannot wait any longer. I must know what has become of my kin before something else unforeseen and terrible happens. I cannot go looking for them myself, with the [sporali] having gone mad. Would you be so [kind] as to find them all for me, or at least what happened to them?'
You say, 'I would be so kind'
Adeenas says 'Oh, thank you! You are so kind. I will certainly write an epic poem in your honor once you finish my dangerous and life-threatening errand!'
You have been assigned the task 'Fate of the Telmira'.
Adeenas hasn't heard from any fellow telmira kin since Rakkiren Shrivstem took control of the sporali. Help Adeenas locate the missing telmira, or if that is not possible, at least find out what happened to them.

---

1\. Explore the northwestern corner of the Forest for signs of Autoros 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
This step updates at /way +1425,+2315,+430 where Autoros [npc=34826] is.
2\. Hail Autoros 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Autoros'
Autoros says 'Adeenas sent you? I am Autoros of the telmira. Though I was nearly killed by the invading cliknar and by the warring sporali factions, I have been fortunate enough to remain hidden in this refuge, at least for now. Tell Adeenas not to worry about me. We must, however, make every effort to call out to Brell Serilis, for his absence is allowing havoc to be unleashed upon the Fungal Forest. If things continue to spin out of control, the fertility of this place will cease, and entire worlds will start to die.'
3\. Explore the southwestern corner of the Forest for signs of Eulyris 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
This step updates at /way -410,+2020,+430 where Eulyris [npc=34829] is.
4\. Hail Eulyris 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Eulyris'
Eulyris says 'You may call me Eulyris. I have sequestered myself in this area in the hopes that the mad sporali will avoid me. Barbarous things they do in the name self-enlightenment. The balance has been upset drastically and grows worse all the time. Please let Adeenas know that I am all right, and when the time comes I will be ready to join our power to call Brell Serilis, if indeed he still exists.'
5\. Explore the southern-central corner of the Forest for signs of Vaelani 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
This step updates at /way -790,+1165,+230 where Excrug Shrivstem [npc=34820] is.
6\. Hail Excrug Shrivstem 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
Your task 'Fate of the Telmira' has been updated.
Excrug Shrivstem says 'I am Excrug. I found Vaelani fleeing from us and aided with the capture. Vaelani has been reduced to a primal essence and is now in the possession of my master, Rakkirin. I trust Vaelani can be restored to normal without harm. I admit that some of Rakkirin's orders cause me anguish and suffering. I do not wish to harm the creatures of the Forest or do things that Brell Serilis might find upsetting.'
7\. Explore the northern-central edge of the Forest for signs of Lisaryn 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
This step updates at /way -778,+1108,+213 near where Deagus the Fungrotect [npc=34844] is.
8\. Hail Deagus the Fungrotect 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Deagus the Fungrotect'
Deagus the Fungrotect says 'Bah, found Deagus you have. These creatures are destroying themselves. It's no worse than anything else happening in the Underfoot, but certainly no better. I came this way a few days ago, aware that the cliknar appeared to be burrowing into the Forest; but then the sporali seemed to go crazy, and they started killing each other and chasing the telmira around. I saw several of them kill one of the telmira here. It was the saddest and most despicable thing I ever saw. I was too late to come to its aid. Here are the remains, in case one of the other telmira should be interested.'
You receive Lisaryn's Remains [item=86865].
9\. Deliver Lisaryn's Remains to Adeena 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
Your task 'Fate of the Telmira' has been updated.
Adeenas says 'Oh, no! Lisaryn is dead! It will take years for Lisaryn's spirit to regather essence and be reborn! Oh, if only Brell Serilis were here, awful things like this would never happen!'
10\. Hail Adeenas 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Adeenas'
You have helped Adeenas determine where the other telmira have gone, or what might have happened to them. This may help Adeenas come up with an idea for dealing with the sporali and cliknar menaces later.
Adeenas says 'You have helped me immensely! I must think about what has happened, and perhaps with the help of the remaining telmira sages I can figure out how to rescue poor Vaelani from Rakkirin's clutches. Now, for you, I shall uphold my promise and get started on my epic poem that will glorify your name!'
_Rewards:_
210 platinum
Experience
40 Brellium Tokens [item=88944]

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task begins with Orgus Shrivstem at approximate location 255, 2675, 340 in the Fungal Forest (just outside Pellucid Grotto)._
You say, 'Hail, Orgus Shrivstem'
Orgus Shrivstem says 'Hello. If you would not harm me, and help me gather some additional [materials], I will agree to do you a service.'
You say, 'Tell me about these materials'
Orgus Shrivstem says 'Yes, sadly I was expelled from my tribe, and that is a difficult situation to be in. We sporali cling to the most amenable areas of the Fungal Forest, and when I was driven away, I had little choice but to move to a less hospitable area. There is much that I need to survive. It is much drier here; I will need a sealant like cliknar brainwax to survive for long. I will also need to create a pouch from a gelatinous osmopump so that I can brew the yeast I need to season the spider palps for my dinner. I can probably catch my own [spiders] later, if I had a set of beetle jaws that would be suitable for a deadfall trap. There is much I can tell you in exchange for this [help]. In addition, if you could contact Adeenas, and tell him of my plight, I would be very grateful.'
You say, 'What'll you give me for my help?'
Orgus Shrivstem says 'Help me with this effort, and I will do what I can to reward you with knowledge.'
You have been assigned the task 'Gathering of Essentials'.
Orgus Shrivstem has left his tribe of sporali, fearing that they are acting against the will of Brell. He can be a good source of background information for the Fungal Forest, but first he will need some help gathering supplies he needs to survive.
Loot some masses of rancid spider palp from spiders in the Fungal Forest. Orgus will feed on these. Get a lump of cliknar brainwax. Orgus needs this to keep his outer body from drying out. Harvest of gelatinous osmopump from the body of a goo. Orgus can fashion this into a yeast-growing sac. Bring Orgus a double-jointed beetle jaw. He needs this element to construct a small animal trap. Bring Orgus' plea for assistance to Adeenas of the Telmira.

---

Collect 2 Rancid Spider Palps from spiders 0/2 (Fungal Forest)
_These can drop from all spiders in Fungal Forest._

Deliver 2 Rancid Spider Palps to Orgus 0/2 (Fungal Forest)

Speak with Adeenas 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
_Adeenas is located in the southeastern area of the zone at approximately -905, -850, 95. Watch out for roaming KOS mobs at her location._
You say, 'Hail, Adeenas'
Your task 'Gathering of Essentials' has been updated.
Adeenas says 'You are quite an interesting creature! Too interesting to be ignored, of course. I am Adeenas of the telmira. I understand you bring greetings and supplications from Orgus Shrivstem. He worries much, but he is in a safe place and will endure for a long time, as long as he uses hit wits and invites no additional attention. Come back later, and I may have thought of a thing or two you can do for me, if you want. I have favors of my own that need asking.'
Return to Orgus 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Orgus Shrivstem'
You have successfully aided Orgus Shrivstem in his effort to stay alive in his state of exile. You have also learned more about the situation in the Fungal Forest, and what it might take to restore order here.
Orgus Shrivstem says 'You are very kind to assist me. Not many would go out of their way for a talking sporali--certainly not many of the two-legged mammals. I made a promise to tell you about what has happened to the Fungal Forest. Proselytizers of the Church of Self came not long after we stopped feeling the magic presence of Brell Serilis. They convinced many among the sporali to change their ways and serve their own needs rather than those of the Underfoot. This seemed to me to be at odds with Brell's vision for a well-functioning Forest. Not long after that, Rakkirin took over as the leader of the tribe and began organizing us to destroy the others. Most wanted little to do with him, and nothing at all to do with any rebellion. I argued at first, but I was threatened with heresy and burning. The other tribes were decimated, and I saw that I must flee. Here I have been for several days, watching as the cliknar came. If chaos reigns here, all the worlds connected to this place will become infertile.'
_Rewards:_
105 platinum
Experience (1.1 AAs at Level 85)
20 Brellium Tokens

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This shared task begins with Excrug Shrivstem at approximate location -790, 1165, 230 in the southern-central part of Fungal Forest._
You say, 'Hail, Excrug Shrivstem'
Excrug Shrivstem says 'I grieve in silence for those who have suffered for my past actions. How could things have gotten this far out of control? I trusted Rakkirin to lead us well. Then came the cliknar invasion, and Rakkirin showed us that we must be strong and protect ourselves with a devastating offense. We said nothing when Rakkirin demanded the destruction of the other sporali who would not bow to him; we thought he was right. It is for these [acts] that my thoughts remain disturbed.'
You say, 'What acts?'
Excrug Shrivstem says 'Brell taught us that each Sporali, indeed every creature of the Fungal Forest is a wondrous creation, deserving of care and respect. When we began destroying the Telmira and the other groups of Sporali, I began having doubts that Rakkirin was right. At first I thought we were acting to save the Forest. Now I see that we were deceived, and that our efforts of late amount to serious [crimes].'
You say, 'Crimes?'
Excrug Shrivstem says 'Among the things I am deeply ashamed of are our efforts to secure only the purest water for ourselves. I know not why he needs it, but Rakkirin was directed to harvest as much of the purest water as possible from this, the wettest region in the Underfoot. Unfortunately, the water here is not strictly pure by Brell's exacting standards, so he demanded we purify the water somehow. Rakkirin chose to use the [heartseeds] of our fallen sporali enemies for this purpose; one heartseed can filter many units of water to absolute purity.'
You say, 'Heartseeds?'
Excrug Shrivstem says 'The undamaged, fresh heartseeds of a sporali who has recently been killed have become a commodity, it seems. We are being asked to slaughter our brethren in the name of a higher objective. May Brell have mercy on me for letting things go so far astray. Is there any way I can have your assistance to [redeem] my actions?'
You say, 'I'll redeem those actions'
Excrug Shrivstem says 'I know I don't deserve this consideration, but the spirits of the fallen sporali demand it. Rakkirin has captured several of our surviving enemies from other tribes, and he certainly intends to slaughter them in order to harvest their heartseeds. You must not allow them to do this. You must rescue them before another infamous blot is placed on our reputation. Bring them to me, and I will guide them to safety.'
You have been assigned the task 'Seeds for the Waterstalkers'.
Excrug Shrivstem is ashamed of the things he has done for his kinsman, Rakkirin, and asks for your help in thwarting Rakkirin's evil plan to use the heartseeds of fallen enemy Sporali to purify water.
Locate the Shrivstem tribe's holding pen that Excrug spoke of. It is in the southeast part of the forest.

---

_The three sporali run off. You don't need to follow them._

Excrug Shrivstem says 'Thank you for liberating the captives, but I fear Rakkirin may have found other ways of obtaining heartseeds. I have received word that he has sent his scouts to kill Olgar Grubcap and Spornus Cloverfoot, and I fear that they are already dead. Please, find them and prevent Rakkirin's scouts from stealing their heartseeds!'
Find Olgar Grubcap's heartseed before teh Shrivstem scouts do 0/1 (Fungal Forest)

Speak with Excrug Shrivstem 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Excrug Shrivstem'
Excrug Shrivstem says 'We have done well today. Rakkirin's plan has been delayed for a time. He is, however, not a sporali to give up at the first setback. He will try again, so you must take more direct action soon.'

_In order to request this task, you must have completed "Fate of the Telmira", "Gathering of Essentials", and "Seeds For the Waterstalkers"._
You say, 'Hail, Deagus the Fungrotect'
Deagus the Fungrotect says 'Hello! Perhaps you can help me. The entire blasted forest has gone [mad]!'
You say, 'Mad?'
Deagus the Fungrotect says 'Cliknar are invading thinking they own the place, and the [Shrivstem] sporali are on the warpath. The emissaries of the Faith of Self came and influenced them to conquer the Fungal Forest. It's bad enough that I have to deal with these blasted rhino [beetles] keeping me up at night.'
You say, 'What about these Shrivstem?'
Deagus the Fungrotect says 'In sporali culture, the Shrivstems were the most beloved of Brell. Now they have descended into darkness, killing and hunting the other creatures of the Forest, performing strange rituals, and staining their own reputation. The Faith of Self influenced them with some sort of false belief that their wants are greater than the needs of the community; now they are up to no good. Their leader, Rakkirin, seems to have gone insane. He has taken Vaelani hostage, using a magic bottle to reduce her to a stored glowing essence. We must guarantee her [safety].'
You say, 'How do we guarantee safety?'
Deagus the Fungrotect says 'You must go and talk with Rakkirin, negotiate, and find out what it is that he will want in exchange for freeing Vaelani. Will you [do] this?'
You say, 'I will do this'
_Without pre-requisites:_
Deagus the Fungrotect says 'There are a lot of complicated issues to deal with these days. I don't fully understand what's happening, and I don't think you do either. So perhaps you can help clear things up. Speak with Orgus Shrivstem, Adeenas and Excrug Shrivstem, if you haven't already. They can use your help, and what we learn from them could make sense of things. If you can do that, then I might have a task for you.'
_With pre-requsites:_
Deagus the Fungrotect says 'Excellent. My job just got a lot easier. With your help, we can put this place back together. Speak with Rakkirin, find out what he wants, then high-tail it back here and we'll put our heads together to see if there's a way we can deceive him and get Vaelani back at the same time.'
You have been assigned the task 'Salvation of Spores'

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This group task begins with Adeenas in the Fungal Forest. She is a telmira located at -905, -850, 95 (southeastern area of the zone). Keep a lookout while speaking with her as KOS mobs wander the area._
_In order to request this task, you must have completed Adeenas's three prior tasks:_
Fate of the Telmira
Gathering of Essentials
Salvation of Spores

---

You say, 'Hail, Adeenas'
Adeenas says 'Hello! I am Adeenas of the telmira. I see you must be lost. Perhaps you should get your bearings here before you further travel the Fungal Forest. Like most of the Underfoot, this place is dangerous to outsiders. This is more true if you venture further to the east, or if you choose to trifle with the sporali or cliknar. Perhaps you came by to [chat] with me instead?'
You say, 'Let's have a chat'
Adeenas says 'I love talking to new people! I get so lonely these days, since the sporali became violent and the cliknar invaded the forest. The other telmira have scattered to the winds, it seems, and I don't know what to do next. No matter. I have you to talk to now. You can remain here and talk with me [forever], yes?'
You say, 'Forever?'
Adeenas says 'Yes! You can remain here forever--if you'll let me transform you into a telmira like me with my special spell, of course; and whatever fate comes to the telmira, you will share. Does that sound [fun]? We can fly to the tops of mushrooms, skim the gullies of rivers, sing songs honoring the life of the forest, and eat fruit beneath the weeping spore caps.'
You say, 'No fun at all...'
Adeenas says 'You mortals are all the same. What sounds like fun to a telmira only seems to scare you to death, yet you all seem to embrace the most terrifying and random type of existence. Well, you don't know what you're missing, that's all I can say! Since you're going to stick with your mortal experience of death and danger, how about helping me with a [favor]? Or did you already do that for me? If so, perhaps it's time to do something about [Rakkirin].'
You say, 'Time to do something about that Rakkirin'
Adeenas says 'Rakkirin Shrivstem has become wicked. Like all of us telmira, he is especially blessed of Brell Serilis and knows better than to be going around inflicting violence and terror on the other creatures of the forest! Shameful, if you ask me. What's worse, he is growing an army of super-sporali that is supposed to help him do something terrible. You know, it would be a grand adventure to [intervene] in his evil plan, wouldn't it?'
You have been assigned the task 'The Fungal Bloom'.
_To zone in:_
You say, 'Let's intervene'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Fungal Forest - The Fungal Bloom.
The Telmira sages and Gigyn have gathered together to help put a stop to Rakkirin Shrivstem's plan to grow a new army of Bloatspore warriors to serve him. You must help them with this task. As you arrive in Shrivstem Glade, you will need to speak with your new allies and make sure they are ready for battle.

---

_Adeenas and five other NPCs are found at the zone-in. The first five steps are to hail them._
Find out if Autoros is ready for combat 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Sage Autoros'
Sage Autoros says 'I am looking forward to showing you how well we fight, mortal.'
Confirm with Eulyris that he is ready for battle 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Sage Eulyris'
Sage Eulyris says 'I am relishing the chance to set the sporali back on the path of Brell by force. The time for waiting on Rakkirin of the Shrivstems to come to his senses naturally has ended!'
Talk with Vaelani, and see if he is ready to fight 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Sage Vaelani'
Sage Vaelani says 'I am ready! Let us fight for what is right, and restore the natural order of Brell Serilis.'
See that Knave Toltan knows what he is supposed to do when the fighting starts 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Knave Toltan'
Knave Toltan says 'I am ready for what must happen next. Sad that Rakkirin cannot hear any but the voice of his own darkened conscience. Many will die.'
Have a word with Knave Garusk before the battle begins 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Knave Garusk'
Knave Garusk says 'I will say the Words of Brell until Adeenas gives the word. In the Words of Brell I find solace and peace. May Brell's words return to this forest soon.'

---

Inform Adeenas that the battle against the blooming Shrivstem army may now begin 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
_When you hail Adeenas, the battle begins. The five NPCs charge the sporali down below and the task locks. Helping them is not necessary (although there is an achievement for keeping them all alive). If you leave them unaided, they'll all die a quick death. From there, you can pull kill targets like normal._
You say, 'Hail, Adeenas'
Adeenas says 'Very good, very very good! Now we must bring down that big brute before we can do anything else! I'll stay up here and watch if you don't mind. I'm not that good at fighting like my friends are. But I'll be cheering you on as all of you face the perilous bloom of Shrivstems! Oh yes, I almost forgot, if you find The Gorgax's summoning whistle that brings Sigorus the Veilcaster out of the Shrivstems' Veil, that will be very helpful. If we slay Sigorus, the Shrivstems will not be able to keep the magical energy flowing to their Veil, and we will be able to defeat all of them.'
Knave Garusk swings its massive fists and attacks.
Knave Toltan swings its massive fists and attacks.
Sage Vaelani says 'Feel the fires of Lord Brell's forge!'
Sage Eulyris says 'Feel the fires of Lord Brell's forge!'
Sage Autoros says 'Feel the fires of Lord Brell's forge!'
Your task 'The Fungal Bloom' has been updated.
_All five charge down the hill towards the sporali._
Destroy some Shrivstem lysospores 0/12 (Fungal Forest)
Destroy some Shrivstem blightspores 0/6 (Fungal Forest)
Destroy some Shrivstem mossbacks 0/4 (Fungal Forest)
Collect The Gorgax' summoning whistle. Adeenas can use this to summon the Shrivstem veilcaster 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
_The Gorgax is on par with the other sporali in terms of difficulty, but has a lot more hitpoints._
Give The Gorgax' summoning whistle to Adeenas 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
_Note: A named will spawn immediately upon completing this turn-in._
Adeenas says 'Yes! That's the whistle. Let me study it and see how it works. I will be the one to blow the whistle, so I must make sure to blow it the right way the first time, or else the wrong sporali might come out of the Veil. Tell me when you're ready for me to use the whistle, like, when you're all done fighting the Shrivstems who are outside the Veil.'
Adeenas says 'Here goes! Cover your ears, there might be a piercing tone, or you might not hear anything at all. This should magically summon Sigorus the Veilcaster from inside the bubble. You'll be able to slay him, and then the Veil should weaken to the point to allow you inside.'
Sigorus the Veilcaster says 'The spirit of the spore will take you down.'
Kill Sigorous the Veilcaster 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
Sigorus the Veilcaster has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Sigorus the Veilcaster's corpse falls, its spores trying one last time to heal it.

---

_You're not done yet. Another batch of sporali appears in the area._
Destroy some bloatspore sprouts. Depriving Rakkirin of these sprouts will be half the battle 0/10 (Fungal Forest)
Destroy some Shrivstem cataspores 0/5 (Fungal Forest)
Destroy some Shrivstem fungrolurks 0/5 (Fungal Forest)
Destroy some Shrivstem glowspores 0/5 (Fungal Forest)
Destroy some Shrivstem shadespores 0/5 (Fungal Forest)
_These will respawn as you fill your kill quota. Once you collect all 30 kills, complete these last two steps:_
Take Rakkirin Shrivstem's surrender 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Rakkirin Shrivstem'
Rakkirin Shrivstem says 'You have ruined my plan to gather an army as I have been commanded. I expect punishment from him now. Brell Serilis has forsaken us, left us without hope. The Temple of Self gave us hope. You took that hope away. Now all of the Underfoot plunges into chaos and fear. What have you done? What can I do now, but surrender?'
Bring the news of the victory to Adeenas 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
You say, 'Hail, Adeenas'
Adeenas says 'Rakkirin has surrendered? Hooray! Well, he didn't need to be so despondent about it. We just want him to live by Brell's rule, as he is supposed to, and not attack, imprison and slay the rest of us just because he thinks he should. Well, you're a hero now, so I guess this means you'll help destroy this Church of Self? We should probably go in a minute, the Shrivstems will need some time to put their camp back in order.'
See what reward you have been offered 0/1 (Fungal Forest)
_Opening the fungus-encrusted chest completes the task._
You have destroyed the nascent army of Shrivstem sporalis, and prevented Rakkirin's plans from being carried out. This may not stop the problem, but at least there will no longer be an army of giant Sporali warriors.
_In the chest is one item of loot. Known drops:_

Crystalline Sledge
Darkearth Scimitar
Deathspore Cap Shield
Dwarven War Staff
Enforcing Hatchet
Flexible Chitin Burgonet
Gold-Edged Saber
Stone of the Sovereign
Telmira Mask of Warfare

Platinum
Experience (1.1 AAs at Level 85)

You say, 'Hail, Inventor Biddlebokk'
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'Stay back! Oh...hello. Sorry about that. I'm a little jumpy, but I think I've found a safe spot for now. Oh, heavens, why did I come here? With all that's going on, why didn't I just send an assistant? What am I going to do? And I still need my forms, and...well, you can see I am very [busy]!'
You say, 'Busy?'
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'I have been charged by Brell himself with researching the creation of a new race of humanoid. I just don't think I can do it! Not after the failure of project X-5. I am still under investigation for 300 test subjects that are missing. Not to mention the accidental polymorphs. It wasn't my fault! Enough about that, though. I need help! Your [help]!'
You say, 'I can help'
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'You'll really help me? Oh, this is such good news! There is much to be done, my friend. I forgot to secure the proper research forms before I came here. You just don't go about researching how to create a new race without the proper forms. All of the forms can be obtained from the Shining City. Getting them [won't be so easy] though.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Grand Experiment'.
You say, 'It won't be so easy?'
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'Well, let's just say the clerks that take care of such papers can be "difficult" to work with at times. I'm sure you will do just fine though. You should talk to Secretary Kilkin first. She can be found in the Council Chamber of the Shining City. She should have the right form for you so we can start our work.'
Inventor Biddlebokk has been tasked by Brell to research the creation of a new race of humanoid. Before the experiment can start, however, you need to secure the proper paperwork from officials in the Shining City.
You will need to turn in the Royal Proclamation Form of Creation to Secretary Kilkin. She can be found in the Council Chamber in the Shining City.

Speak with Bub 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)

Deliver 1 Research Notes to Inventor Biddlebokk 0/1 (Lichen Creep)

Well, it seems the experiment can continue now. In Lichen Creep, plants known as Lava Tubes grow. Every so often, the will sprout spores which contain a magical, fire-based energy. # Biddlebokk #3: What Could Go Wrong?

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This shared task (1-6 players) begins with Inventor Biddlebokk in Lichen Creep. He is located at -395, 915, -16.
To request this task, you must first have completed his two prior tasks: "The Grand Experiment" and "The Experiment Begins".
You say, 'Hail, Inventor Biddlebokk'
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'Did you think I wouldn't be [busy]? With [Bub] drinking on the job, I have my hands full. Do you think you could bring me some [lichen] samples so I can finish my experiment?'
You say, 'Lichen samples?'
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'I will need a few samples of the lichen that the cliknar eat. They must be using some special enzymes to break it down. Could you [harvest] some of it for me?'
You say, 'I'll harvest some'
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'Thank you, _____!'
You have been assigned the task 'What Could Go Wrong?'.
Deliver 10 Lichen Sample to Inventor Biddlebokk 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
These drop from cliknar scouts and gatherers in Lichen Creep.
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'These samples are perfect! Now, to analyze all the components you have brought to me. Thereâ€™s one problem, though: My tools are missing. I was doing some research when a lava mephit cornered me and stole them. If you can, please find my tools so I can finish the experiment!'
Deliver 1 Biddlebokk's Tools To Inventor Biddlebokk 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
The tools drop from lava mephit workers and guards.
Inventor Biddlebokk says 'Ok, now that I have my tools, I should be able to create a mixture for the base of our new humanoid. Oh, this is so exciting! I hope nothing goes wrong. . . So far so good. . . Ok, the mixture has been created. Whew! Now you need to take the mixture to Falrorn Borlakio in the Shining City. I think heâ€™s in the same workshop as Halgin. He can create a mold of the new humanoidâ€™s form from the mixture.'
Deliver 1 Undulating Mixture to Falrorn Borlakio 0/1 (Kernagir)
The locations of Falrorn and the other turn-in NPCs will be marked on your compass.
Falrorn is at 115, 700, 54.
Falrorn Borlakio says 'Oh, great. Another one of Biddlebokk's experiments? This should be good. You might want to stand back in case something happens. Let me just put this in the mold here. Here you go. I don't know what it is, but it's probably not what Biddlebokk envisioned. In fact, it appears to be highly toxic. I would give this to the Cleaner Bot over there to dispose of.'
Deliver 1 Failed Experiment to Cleaner Bot 0/1 (Kernagir)
The Cleaner Bot is next to Falrorn (135, 715, 54).
Cleaner Bot says 'Highly toxic substance identified. Please stand back. Destroy mode initiated. Item has been destroyed. Here is your empty mold. Report to Bookkeeper Blanch for experiment failure protocol 8A processing. Thank you for using Cleaner Bot. Your efforts help keep the Shining City shiny and clean.'
Deliver 1 Empty Mold to Bookkeeper Blanch 0/1 (Kernagir)
Bookkeeper Blanch says 'So Biddlebokk's creation experiment failed? Wow, what a surprise. That substance you had in the mold could have made the entire city sick. I guess I shouldn't have expected anything less from that daft inventor. Take this reprimand to Biddlebokk. I will let you off the hook, but don't go helping him with anymore of his so-called experiments.'
Deliver 1 A Reprimand to Inventor Biddlebokk 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
I knew I couldn't do it! And now I have a reprimand! Oh, well, it's not the first I have received, and I am sure it won't be the last. It's too bad things didn't turn out as I had hoped. I was going to name the creation after you! Ah, well, maybe another time, eh? Thanks for all of your help. I should get ready to see Brell and receive my punishment. Maybe I will see you again, when I get out of prison. Take care, my friend!
315 platinum
Experience (3.3 AAs at Level 85)
~60 Brellium Tokens
Completion of this task arc allows you to request the Lichen Creep group mission, "Bonfire of the Adherents".Rewards:
- Brellium Token [item=88944]

# Bonfire of the Adherents

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

This group mission begins with Gweddon Tinkertasker in Lichen Creep. He is located at -320, 145, -65 (northeastern part of the zone).
In order to request this task, you must first have completed Inventor Biddlebokk's three tasks.
You say, 'Hail, Gweddon Tinkertasker'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Greetings, friend. Are you a friend? Or are you in league with those who are behind the recent unsavory [events] taking place in Underfoot?'
You say, 'Events?'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'The autarchians, they call themselves. Traitors to Brell Serilis they are! I and several of the others have held out against their strange teachings, but I fear we are no match for their sheer numbers here. Nearly all of the gnomes now subscribe to the Faith of Self and will wait no longer for Brell's voice to return to their ears. The autarchians have grown a great [cult], and strong it is among the mephits and lava creatures who watch over the sources of lodestone.'
You say, 'A great cult?'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'There is no telling where they will stop. They spread their insidious teachings to even the lava golems, the most trusting of creatures, and persecute those of us adherents who desire only to wait for Brell awhile longer. Then, some of our number were called by the mephit Zwurek deep within Lichen Creep, where mutiny has been simmering for many days. I have not heard from Fiddy, Duerg, or Neerda since! Will you please [help] me?'
You say, 'I can help'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'You must go deeper into Lichen Creep, and release each of the three missing Adherents by teleporting them to safety with a magic wand, when all of their captors have been eliminated. Only then will they be free of any magic wards that may restrain them. I do hope you can help; your presence here must be a [sign].'
You say, 'A sign of what?'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'A sign of Brell's return, of course. I despair we shall ever find my fellow Adherents alive, but if we do it will be thanks to the blessing of Brell Serilis, who shall return to us and reward us for our unshakable faith. Here, take the Metacrystalline Teleportation Wand, and head first to the Searing Prominence, where Fiddy is held. It is located just beyond where this stream meets the lava. Let me know when you are ready to [go].'
You have been assigned the task 'Bonfire of the Adherents'.
Anyone in the zone with the task requester receives a Wand of Metacrystalline Teleportation. (Another Wand can be requested by saying "sign" again.)
To zone in:
You say, 'Ready to go!'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Are you ready to depart?'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Lichen Creep - Bonfire of the Adherents.
You hear Gweddon shout in the distance, "Don't forget, the wand's only tuned for rescuing my friends!"
Explore the disturbance at the Searing Prominence 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Kill Norket the Disgruntled and his minions 0/11 (Lichen Creep)
Norket hits for a max ~8,000 while his minions are slightly less difficult.
Norket the Disgruntled says 'Invaders have been spotted! Attack! For the Underfoot!!!'
Norket the Disgruntled has been slain by _____!
Norket the Disgruntled's corpse falls to the ground. "Forgive me Brell, I have failed you!"
Task Window Says: Gweddon Tinkertasker, a loyal servant of Brell, is troubled that the mephits of Lichen Creep have been summoning the adherents of Brell to other parts of the Creep for reasons unknown. You must locate the adherents and ensure their safety.
Look for Fiddy near the Searing Prominence, which is north of the Boiling Waterfall. You will need to kill the gnomish autarchian minions and their leader, Norket the Disgruntled. Their magic is preventing Fiddy from being rescued.
Use the wand to rescue Fiddy 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Task Window Says: Fiddy looks a little out of it. You'll have to rescue her with the teleporter wand.
Speak to Gweddon 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
You say, 'Hail, Gweddon Tinkertasker'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Yes! Fiddy magically appeared right next to me, no problem! Now, if you wouldn't mind locating Duerg Rockpiker. I think he might be held by the lava golems by the Peninfernal Lava Pool. Don't worry about its exact location; it should be west of the Boiling Waterfall, below the cliknar camp. Look for the overly-zealous lava golems.'
Explore the Peninfernal Lava Pool 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Kill the lava golem zealots 0/10 (Lichen Creep)
Task Window Says: Press on westward into the Lichen Creep and try to locate another adherent, Duerg, who is being held hostage by the magic of the zealous lava golems. The golems will need to be eliminated before you can free Duerg.
Use the wand to rescue Duerg 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Speak to Gweddon 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Note: The task locks upon hail.
You say, 'Hail, Gweddon Tinkertasker'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Duerg also arrived unharmed! He and I both thank you very much! The last bit of rescuing you must do is to find Neerda Stoneguard. Duerg says that the lava mephits, led by Zwurek, plan to push him off a cliff into a lava pool if he doesn't join the autarchians! Zwurek and his troublemakers must be defeated; we can't allow them to cause any more trouble here in Lichen Creep. Save Neerda and bring me proof that you have killed his captors. They are camped at the Serrated Cliff, the edge of a basin where lava cascades into the lower pools of the Creep. Hurry!'
Explore Zwurek's mephit camp along the Serrated Cliff 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Kill the lava mephit executioners 0/6 (Lichen Creep)
Kill Zwurek 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
The lava mephit executioners hit for a max ~7,500 while Zwuerk hits for a max ~8,500 (he has ~1.4m hp). He also casts the spell "Zwurek's Sputum" with fair frequency:
Zwurek's Sputum: Targeted AE 35', Fire (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 6500
Loot six ceremonial blades from the executioners 0/6 (Lichen Creep)
Autarchian Execution Blades are dropped by the mephit executioners surrounding Zwurek.
Use the wand to rescue Neerda 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Task Window Says: Poor Neerda is ready to pass out from the heat, it will never be a better time to use the wand!
Speak to Gweddon 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
You say, 'Hail, Gweddon Tinkertasker'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Goodness! You have saved all three of them from...well, whatever it is that happens after a person becomes an autarchian and falls from the grace of Brell Serilis!'
Give the ceremonial blades to Gweddon 0/6 (Lichen Creep)
Return the metacrystalline teleportation wand to Gweddon 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Thank you for returning the wand; I know you've put it to good use!'
Speak to Gweddon for further instructions 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
You say, 'Hail, Gweddon Tinkertasker'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'I must now ask you to leave Lichen Creep for a while, my friend, and take this message to [Tronten] the Subnexus, our adherent ally in Pellucid Grotto. I need to inform him of what we have seen. And while you're doing that, I'll arrange for the safe passage of my friends to the Underquarry, where we have a few remaining allies! Please, go quickly!'
You receive "Gweddon's Warning".
Say "Tronten" for a teleport straight to Tronten's location in Pellucid Grotto.
Deliver Gweddon's warning message to Tronten the Subnexus 0/1 (Pellucid Grotto)
Tronten the Subnexus looks at you with his simmering crystal eyes. 'I thought these autarchians were mostly confined to the Pellucid Grotto and portions of the Underfoot under cliknar control. Now they have virtually conquered Lichen Creep! I can do little to repair the damage they have already done, but I can certainly aid [Gweddon] with this powerful orb. Take it to him; he can protect himself with it while the adherents regain their strength. Tell him he can count on our assistance soon!'
Your task 'Bonfire of the Adherents' has been updated.
You say, 'Send me to Gweddon'
Tronten the Subnexus says 'Give my regards to Gweddon.'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Lichen Creep.
You receive "Tronten's Protocrystalline Orb".
Deliver Tronten's orb to Gweddon 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Oh, very good! I knew Tronten would be a good enough ally to send me something of assistance! This orb looks as though I could use it to hide indefinitely from my persecutors!'
Speak to Gweddon 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
You say, 'Hail, Gweddon Tinkertasker'
Gweddon Tinkertasker says 'Thank you for all you have done. You have prevented our enemies from wiping us all out! Thanks to you, we will be able to wait until Brell returns and tell him what we have seen!'
Your task 'Bonfire of the Adherents' has been updated.
You have prevented the Autarchians from completely wiping out the Adherents in Lichen Creep. Brell Serilis would be most pleased!

You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Greetings overlander! I have something most urgent that I can use your [help] with. I'd do it myself if it weren't for these blasted [pests]!'
You say, 'My help?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'We've been having many problems with these so called Autarchians, but we are [concerned] about why they seem to be congregating here.'
You say, 'Concerned?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'This many Autarchians in one place? Surely that cannot be a good thing! No, they must be [stopped]! '
You say, 'They must be stopped!'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'I am completely out of my element here. This heat is almost unbearable! Will you help us with the [Autarchians]?'
You say, 'I'll help you with those Autarchians'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Thank you! For now, all I need from you is to gauge their strength and locations. I need to know what we are dealing with.'
You have been assigned the task 'Into The Fire'.
Gihbra needs to find out the overall strength of the Autarchians that have come to the husk. He will need you to recon the area and assess the races and their numbers.

---

The fungi colony is the east-central tunnel system. The Autarchian update is deep in the southwestern tunnels. The lava pool is at the end of the "husk" - the southeast part of the zone._

Fungi colony: -487, -481, -147
Autarchian den: -902, 274, -38
Lava pool: -1006, -171, -56
Return to Gihbra Artinguard 0/1 (Volska's Husk)

Recon of the Autarchians is complete
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Interesting. . . I wonder why the fungi are separated from the others. It seems there is much more to do before I can leave.'

Entered: Mon Feb 22 17:27:23 2010
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task is the third in a series of five that begins with Gihbra Artinguard in Volska's Husk. He is located at approximately -140, -165, -47. Be careful in this zone as there are no especially safe points, including the locations of task givers._

You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'I cannot believe everything that has gone on lately. It is only a matter of time before the Autarchians try to take over the whole of the underfoot. Perhaps we can [slow] them down a bit.'
You say, 'Slow them down?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'The mephits appear to be indigenous to this area. The Bellikos must be controlling them so they may harvest the Greken eggs without the mephits' [interference].'
You say, 'Interfence?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'So will the mephits fight the Autarchians if the Bellikos did not have a hold on them? I guess there is no way to know for sure. But it is worth a [shot].'
You say, 'I'll give it a shot'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'So then it is settled. You must go to the mephits' lair and [kill] the Bellikos warden who is influencing them.'
You say, 'I'll kill her!'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Thank you. I am sure the mephits will make it difficult to get to the warden. You should go speak to the Mephit Shaman in Lichen Creep first. If you convince him, I am sure it will go a long way to help here.'
You have been assigned the task 'Autochthonous Support'.

---

Speak to the Mephit Shaman in Lichen Creep 0/1 (Lichen Creep)
_The Mephit Shaman is found at location -1035, 920, 40. Two golems that are sometimes see-invis are at either of its sides._
You say, 'Hail, Mephit Shaman'
Mephit Shaman says 'Dis Bellikos do not be mess wif da mephits in da husk. I do not allow it. Da mephit in husk dos know better den to be messin wif da Bellikos. More mephits I will send to da husk when I can. For now, da mephits who help da Bellikos should die.'
Kill the Bellikos warden in the mephit's lair 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_The Bellikos warden spawns at -161, -625, -133 (in the mephit caves of Volska's Husk -- the southeast corner of the northeast caves in the zone)._
Return to Gihbra Artinguard 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
The mephits are free of Bellikos influence. They should no longer aid the Autarchians, when the shaman sends more mephits to the area.
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Now maybe the mephits will not take too kindly to the Autarchian's presence'

Entered: Mon Feb 22 17:48:39 2010
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This solo task is the fourth in a series of five that begins with Gihbra Artinguard in Volska's Husk. He is located at approximately -140, -165, -47. Be careful in this zone as there are no especially safe points, including the locations of task givers._
_In order to request this task, you must first have completed "Into the Fire", "Uncover the Truth", and "Autochthonous Support"._
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Thank you for eliminating the Bellikos overseeing the Mephits. I'm beginning to see how everything works down here, and I think there is a way to [destabilize] the social system.'
You say, 'Destabilize it?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Yes, I know you've done much for me, \_\_\_\_\_\_, and I thank you -- Brell thanks you. But there is more to do. If what you've told me already is accurate, each race in here must have a leader. It is silly to think that each creature can do whatever they want, with no one to tell them what rocks to mine, or what food to cultivate, or when to sleep, or what to fight. Order is natural. Brell commands, and we obey; the foreman tells the worker where to wield his pickaxe; the council governs, and the citizens comply. The [wise and strong] will always be the leaders of the foolish and weak.'
You say, 'Wise and strong?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'I'm sure it is no different here. There is no creature as foolish as the one who thinks he is completely free to do as he pleases. Above him always, just out of his vision, is a fist, a sword, or an edict waiting to crush him if he strays from the will of those better than he. And above all is Brell. His will is greater than that of these heretics. They have strayed from the path: It is not freedom; it is rebellion. And Brell will [crush] them.'
You say, 'Crush?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'In here, \_\_\_\_\_\_, we are the arms of Brell. I waited to tell you this until I knew more about the situation here in the husk, and until I knew I could trust you: I came here with Brell's blessing. The Coldain sent me to investigate, but I know nothing of war or espionage. I petitioned the council of the Shining City. They heard my case, recognized its gravity, and sent me with Brell's knowledge and aid to thwart the plans of the First. They then gave me knowledge of a powerful enchantment: the Spell of Brell's Compulsion. I can cast it on you, and those who speak to you while its effect holds cannot help but divulge their secrets -- perfect for our [plans]. You see, even here Brell asserts his will!'
You say, 'Plans?'
You have been assigned the task 'The Arms of Brell'.
Gihbra Artinguard says 'I have cast the spell on you. Now, speak to the leaders of the races. Find their weaknesses and exploit them. If we can unseat them from their positions of power, there will be confusion among the races. The fragile order established in the husk will become unbalanced. The Dwarves will be unable to mine for minerals; the Gnomes will stop building their clockworks; the Gnolls and Kobolds will stop hunting for Sporali; and the Sporali will flee the caverns. The Bellikos will have no choice but to expose themselves and try to restore order. That will be our chance to strike.'

---

Hail Fibros Geripon, leader of the Sporali 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ With the spell of Brell's Persuasion upon you, the leaders of the husk will be compelled to speak with you.
_Fibros is found in the sporali caves at location -525, -640, -143._
You say, 'Hail, Fibros Geripon'
Fibros Geripon looks shaken as Brell's blessing surrounds you and you gaze into his mind. 'What do you seek? Who are you? You want to know of me? I am Fibros Geripon...I lived as a captive in the Cliknar camps...for so long...I cannot remember how long...and then I escaped and ended up here...and I...' Fibros Geripon shudders, and a new voice seems to emanate from his mouth. 'Who are you, and how do you know I [speak] for Fibros?'
You say, 'I know you speak for Fibros...'
Fibros Geripon says 'The old ones, shriveled and dried as they may be, speak, and the Sporali listen. The words of the old are foolish and fearful. They would have us flee or hide. But I, Omnus Vigilcap, am unafraid. For every Gnoll or Kobold who raids our cavern, a hundred Sporali will pay retribution... and I will [command] them.'
You say, 'Command them?'
Fibros Geripon says 'The Sporali respect this shell, this Fibros Geripon. He exhorts them to defend, and they defend. He asks them to sacrifice themselves in battle, and they obey. The Sporali are naturally weak, but I make them strong. If it were not for my [control] of the old one, they would not listen to the exhortations of a Sporali as bold as I.'
You say, 'Your control?'
Fibros Geripon says 'When I escaped from the Cliknar farms, I took pity on Fibros. He was an old Sporali, who had been a captive for an untold amount of time. His mind was mostly gone, but I could not leave him. I took him with me when I made my way to the husk. When the beasts began attacking us, I could not convince my brethren to defend themselves. The fearful lot would follow Fibros when he would bumble about and wedge himself into dark crevices and tremble. The Gnolls and Kobolds would sniff out the hiding Sporali and drag them off without resistance. But my [plan] would not let the beasts dine so easily.'
You say, 'What plan?'
Fibros Geripon says 'If his mind were stronger, it would not have worked. I infected Fibros with my own spores. They burrowed into his mind, and now I speak for him. The more spores I feed him, the greater control I exert over his will. But I am afraid; as I fight with the Gnolls and Kobolds, my body becomes scored with wounds. My spores bleed from me and drip behind me as I move throughout these caverns. The more I do battle, the fewer spores I have to influence Fibros. The spores are control: control of Fibros, control of the Sporali. If my voice were no longer heard, the Sporali would flee for their lives, and our grand experiment in self-hood would end.'
Collect 5 of Omnus' Spore Globules 0/5 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ Fibros Geripon is being controlled by Omnus Vigilcap. If you can get some of Omnus' spores, you might be able to take control of Fibros.
_These are groundspawns throughout the sporali caverns._
Ingest 1 Omnus' Spore Globule 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ Your mind is strong enough to resist any control Omnus might try to impose on you.
_Once you've collected five spores, right-click one of them to ingest it (note this does not break invisibility)._
You inhale a handful of Omnus' spores and feel a tingling spread behind your eyes. You ignore a voice like Omnus' that begins to whisper inside your mind...
Give 4 Omnus' Spore Globules to Fibros Geripon 0/4 (Volska's Husk))
_Task Window Says:_ Now that you've tapped into Omnus' means of control, commandeer the link by feeding more spores to Fibros.
Fibros Geripon ingests Omnus' spores docilely. In your mind, you can see yourself through his eyes...
Hail Fibros Geripon 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ Now is your chance to speak through Fibros and influence the Sporali.
You say, 'Hail, Fibros Geripon'
Fibros Geripon's eyes stare distantly. He opens his mouth, and you hear your words spoken in his voice. 'Listen, Sporali! Our ways have been unwise! If we stand our ground, the Gnolls and the Kobolds will certainly destroy us. Our only chance to live is to flee these caverns and rejoin our brethren in Corathus. I have been led astray by Omnus Vigilcap! He is a fool and will surely cause our deaths if we follow his insane ideas. Listen to yourselves, not Omnus. Let us protect ourselves and leave this place!' Fibros Geripon falls silent. The nearby Sporali look at each other and nod in agreement.

---

Hail Adins Tarbrew, leader of the Dwarves 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ The Sporali have listened to Fibros' words. You should speak to Adins Tarbrew and try to exploit the Dwarves' situation.
_Adins is located in the main Autarchian den, approximate location -1190, 39, -130._
You say, 'Hail, Adins Tarbrew'
Adins Tarbrew's eyes widen, and he sways a bit as he talks. 'I know what you want from me: beer! Everybody does. Back in Brell's rest, they'd all say, 'He brews better than Brell himself!' And it was true! After a day of battles, warriors would crowd the mess halls, and I'd serve those braggarts a batch of brew so stiff that Brell himself would have to revive them to fight again the next day! But that got [boring].
You say, 'Boring?'
Adins Tarbrew says 'Yes, my brew became famous enough that Brell let me into his Shining City. Yes, I served suds to the entire council. But beer is beer. It gets you drunk. You never SEE anything! I wanted room to experiment, so I tried different ingredients: roots and extracts, minerals and powders, pulverized bones of creatures you've never heard of, beard clippings of the boldest of Dwarves. Then everyone [saw] plenty.'
You say, 'Saw plenty?'
Adins Tarbrew looks past you, as if he sees something at the back of the cavern. 'Ice dragons -- thousands of them -- swooping down on Shining City, scooping up Dwarves and Gnomes in their claws and dashing them against stone spires. A hundred Dwarves rallied against the attack: Overturning benches, breaking doors off hinges, ripping stones from monuments, they built battlements in the city square and held the dragons at bay. Until the effects of my brew wore off. For hours they had battled imaginary dragons, but the city looked like a real battlefield. Brell had me kicked out, and I was [forbidden] from brewing.
You say, 'Forbidden?'
Adins Tarbrew says 'Can you forbid a Kobold from fighting? A Gnoll from digging? Brewing is in my nature, and not even Brell himself can keep me from it. The First was right: satisfy yourself. Do whatever you were made to do. So, I settled here, where I can brew whatever I want and my [recipes] are appreciated.'
You say, 'What recipes?'
Adins Tarbrew says 'My fellow Dwarves love it. They gripe when they have to mine for minerals, or when the Gnomes steal our supplies, but they perk right up when they drink my brew. They come to me with hands shaking and eyes darting, and they leave with a healthy swagger and broad smile. I generally keep things going around here -- I tell them to get to work and bring me supplies, and then I reward them with [beer].'
You say, 'Beer?'
Adins Tarbrew says 'I brew it right here in the husk, and the recipe is really quite simple: two drops of Sporali blood, two handfuls of husk minerals, one mug of hot spring water, and one sprig of bitter lava root. At first I tried Sessiloid dung, but that made the workers too sleepy. I just leave it out now. Now, don't go telling the other Dwarves my recipe! If they knew how to make it themselves, they wouldn't bother listening to me anymore...'
Brew some Somnolent Beer with special ingredients 0/1 (ALL)
_Task Window Says:_ The Dwarves are addicted to Adins' brew. You should make a special batch using 2 drops of Sporali Blood, 2 handfuls of Husk Minerals, 1 Mug of Hot Spring Water, 1 sprig of Bitter Lava Root, and 4 slugs of Sessiloid Dung.
_In a brew barrel, combine (no-fail):_
4x Sessiloid Dung _(drops from sessiloids in the nest area)_
2x Drop of Sporali Blood _(drops from sporalis)_
2x Husk Minerals _(groundspawn in the main part of Volska's Husk: -950, -55 and -893, 43)_
1x Bitter Lava Root _(groundspawn in the heart, 630, -70, -150)_
1x Hot Spring Water _(click on the bubble at the pool of water at -655, -361)_
_This produces 5x "Somnolent Beer."_
_NOTE: There is a brew barrel in the southernmost part of the southwest cave system: -1425, 200, -95._
Give Somnolent Beer to 5 Autarchian workers 0/5 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ Share the soporific beer and Adins' secret recipe with the Autarchian Dwarf workers.
_Non-KOS Autarchians workers are found in the back of the Autarchian den. Give the beer to five of them:_
an Autarchian worker says 'That's some tasty beer! Really, the recipe is that easy? I'll have to make more of this! I do feel a bit sleepy now, though...'
Hail Adins Tarbrew 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ See what Adins thinks of your meddling.
You say, 'Hail, Adins Tarbrew'
Adins Tarbrew looks at you and laughs. He hiccups. 'You told them my recipe, didn't you? I knew you would. I knew better, but for some reason I told you anyway. I'm stupid. And why do all the workers look like they're sleepwalking? Looks like I'll have to concoct a newer, better brew, because they're through listening to me now. But how can I be mad at you? Who can be angry at a giant Gnome with a Froglok head?'

---

Speak with Leathereyes, leader of the Gnolls 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ The Dwarves won't be able to mine for minerals when they're sleeping all day. Talk to Leathereyes, leader of the Gnolls, and see what you can do about them.
_Leathereyes is found in the main Autarchian den at approximately -1125, 445, -62._
You say, 'Hail, Leathereyes'
Leathereyes growls at you, his heavily scarred eyelids showing two slivers of contempt. 'Come to fight Leathereyes? He choke you and rip your throat and bury your skull while you live! He leads pack; you slink with tail between legs! Bark! Why does he [tell] you his secrets?
You say, 'Tell me'
Leathereyes says 'Horrible \_\_\_\_\_, don't talk to Leathereyes! Whine...Leathereyes is fierce, best Gnoll in Rest of Brell! Every night sleep with blood of hundred Kobolds on lips and teeth. But always they come back! Same face of skin Leathereyes tore! So he tears again...and wants real [death]!'
You say, 'Death?'
Leathereyes says 'Bark! Leathereyes wants crunch of Kobold in teeth, wants to bury bones deep...no rising again! Human female says snake tunnels have many Kobolds and much death. Leathereyes can kill all he wants, kill Kobolds and eat mushrooms. He tells Gnolls to come fight for him -- [fight and hunt] for fierce Leathereyes!'
You say, 'Fight and hunt?'
Leathereyes says 'But Leathereyes must bite and shake them. Tails in legs and full of fear...Leathereyes don't trust them. They yap and bark together; he sees them lapping at same pools. All want fight with Leathereyes, and bury him like many bones. But Leathereyes look strong! Gnolls too much in fear to challenge him -- no rivals to split the pack! Yap! Bark!'
Hail Gihbra Artinguard and form a plan 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ Leathereyes is afraid of losing respect of his fellow Gnolls. Gihbra might know how to exploit this fear.
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'It sounds as though Leathereyes rules by strength alone. If a Gnoll were to challenge him and win, the pack would turn on him. I'm sure you could tailor a passable Gnoll disguise with a few of their skins. I recall seeing a loom when I was in the Shining City...'
Tailor a Gnoll Disguise 0/1 (ALL)
_Task Window Says:_ Maybe you can fool Leathereyes into thinking you're a Gnoll by tailoring a Gnoll disguise out of 5 Autarchian gnoll skins.
_In a loom or sewing kit, combine five Autarchian Gnoll Skins (dropped by gnolls in Volska's Husk)._
Wear the disguise and humiliate Leathereyes in battle 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ Wear the disguise, hail Leathereyes, and humiliate him in battle. Don't kill him though.
_Cast the illusion on yourself and speak to Leathereyes, at which point he'll attack you. Beat him down to low health for the update. (When this happens, your illusion fades and the item disappears.)_
You say, 'Hail, Leathereyes'
Leathereyes says 'Bark! You kill Gnolls? You challenge Leathereyes? His eyes scarred, but yours he gnashes in teeth!'
Leathereyes says 'Leathereyes has no rivals! He rips meat from bones while you live!'
_Once he reaches 20% health, he goes unaggro._
Hail Leathereyes 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ Leathereyes looks humiliated.
You say, 'Hail, Leathereyes'
The garment begins to fall apart.
Leathereyes whimpers, tail between his legs. He looks up as you approach and barks. 'You saw Leathereyes lose? Whole pack saw! They follow rival. Leathereyes will tear them all, bleed their blood and drown the fire pools!
_You will want to re-invis and do "/pet back off" soon after this, as he eventually walks back to his spawn point and becomes attackable again. Pets will still have him in their attack queue, so they will re-attack him unless you stop them first._

---

Return to Gihbra Artinguard 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_Task Window Says:_ The Gnolls will no longer respect Leathereyes, having seen him beaten by a single Gnoll. Tell Gihbra of all the success you've had.
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Your task 'The Arms of Brell' has been updated.
The first part of the plan is complete, but there is still another leader whose secrets you must exploit.
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Wonderful, \_\_\_\_\_. Our plan is in motion. Already you have cast the husk into turmoil, now that several races have lost faith in their leaders. They will fight among themselves, the leaders and their followers, and we will be that much closer to seeing the will of Brell established once again. Brell's spell still has power left; let us talk further, as there is still more work to be done...'
_Rewards:_
525 platinum
Experience (how much exp?)
~200 Brellium Tokens
_Completion of this task leads into the final one in the series, "Brell's Will Be Done"._
- Brellium Token [item=88944]
