# Gihbra #5: Brell's Will Be Done

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This solo task is the fifth in a series of five that begins with Gihbra Artinguard [npc=35069] in Volska's Husk. He is located at approximately -140, -165, -47. Be careful in this zone as there are no especially safe points, including the locations of task givers.
In order to request this task, you must first have completed Into the Fire [quest=5023], Uncover the Truth [quest=5024], Autochthonous Support [quest=5036], and The Arms of Brell [quest=5037].
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Our plan to destabilize the husk is now fully in motion. Let us go [further].'
You say, 'Further?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Let's finish what we've started. Order in here is starting to break down, but we must still contend with one more leader. Interix Tanglefuze, by my estimation, is the chief Gnome in this husk. I'm sure if you [speak] with him you'll find a way to discredit him in the eyes of his followers.'
You say, 'I'll speak to him'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Go with Brell's blessing, _____. Soon the Autarchians and the Bellikos will be crushed under Brell's fist, and his will shall reign once again in the Underfoot.'
You have been assigned the task 'Brell's Will Be Done'.
1. Interrogate Interix Tanglefuze 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: Perhaps Interix Tanglefuze, leader of the Gnomes, has a weakness that can be exploited.
Interix Tanglefuze [npc=35087] is found in the main Autarchian den at approximately -1115, 250, -95. Keyword for update is "supplies".
You say, 'Hail, Interix Tanglefuze'
Interix Tanglefuze  looks you up and down and laughs. 'You're some kind of enchanter, aren't you? No, by the looks of you I'd say it's not your own enchantment. We normally wouldn't be talking; my clockworks would have beaten in your silly _____ face by now. But it's as if I feel the presence of Brell himself -- some sort of powerful persuasion spell. You think you're clever? You think you would even begin to comprehend the things I could reveal? Go ahead and ask me [questions]. Trying to understand my answers will be like Sessiloids studying the charts and motions of stars.'
You say, 'I have questions for you...'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'Yes, of course I am in charge. I'm the smartest. I know how the chain of supplies works in the husk; I know the fastest way to build the strongest clockworks; I know what everyone is doing and why they're doing it. I can see how everything fits together, from the bolts and gears in my clockworks, to the living cogs and widgets grinding away in these caverns. They think they're free, but someone else is always [turning] their crank.'
You say, 'Turning their crank?'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'The Bellikos aren't vacationing in the husk, admiring the sights of golems and Mephits and lava. There's only one reason why they're here: This is their labor camp. Don't you get it? The Sporali feed on the minerals in the hot springs; the Kobolds and the Gnolls bring us Sporali meat and serve as sentient -- or at least semi-sentient -- guards; the Dwarves mine the metals and minerals in the caverns; and the Gnomes craft war devices with those supplies. We're a Greken and clockwork factory here, building and growing the [weapons] with which to oppose Brell himself.'
You say, 'What weapons?'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'That's why I came here in the first place. Do you think a Gnome as smart as I would leave Kernagir for any other reason than crazy, wild, dangerous ambition? I want to build the most deadly clockworks I can, large or small -- it doesn't matter. Do you think Brell would allow me to build a clockwork that could raze the foundation of Kernagir in mere hours? Of course not! And you can only keep plans for such things secret for so long, so I [came] here.'
You say, 'Came here?'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'I didn't need to listen to any Bellikos silliness about choosing your own path or following your inner self. That sort of nonsense is for the cretins in these caverns or those dumb beasts that bumble about near the lava pits. Small minds never have large egos. And if you need to be told to [think] for yourself, you shouldn't.'
You say, 'Think for yourself?'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'Even before the explosion that sent me to the Underfoot, I lived according to my own will, and I never stopped doing so. I don't care what tricks Brell's First Creation uses to get people to follow him. I follow myself, and I can't be tricked. I get what I want: a place to experiment that is far from Brell's eyes. He gets what he wants: clockwork warriors. But I'm chafing under the current arrangement. I have [grander] plans!'
You say, 'What are these grander plans?'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'You think I want the Bellikos telling me what to do? I outsmart them every time, but I can't stand their arrogance. A suggestion here and there: modify this, fix that, and on and on -- I've had enough! I have plans for new clockworks that will put fear into Brell and his First Creation! When I'm done with construction, I'll drive the Bellikos from this husk. This will be my lab, my factory. From here I will create the ultimate clockworks of destruction and strike out at both Brell and his First! All I need now are the proper [supplies] to build them...'
You say, 'Supplies?'
Interix Tanglefuze  laughs at you again. 'I know you work for Brell. You're his good little puppet, dancing about whenever he pulls the strings. You're just the sort of _____ the Bellikos' words were meant for. Ha! You want to kill the Bellikos, and so do I, but you don't trust me. Well, I don't care. You need me; I need you. And I don't think you're smart enough to double-cross me. I know by your persuasion spell that you have contacts in Kernagir. I need some parts from there to build my clockworks. Here is a list: Mithril Ore, Golden Cogs, Silver Wires, and Voltage Regulators. Go talk to your handler and see what you can arrange. If you get me the parts, I'll help you get rid of the Bellikos.'
Your task 'Brell's Will Be Done' has been updated.
2. Speak with Gihbra Artinguard 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: It appears that Interix wants the Bellikos out of the husk too, if only for his own nefarious purposes. Perhaps Gihbra will have an idea of how to use this to your advantage.
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'I was wrong about Tanglefuze, but I don't trust him in the least. We can't just hand him all the supplies he requires and expect him not to pose a threat to us. You can find everything you need in the Shining City, but your first stop when you get there should be Percy Scrapfisher. He's in a building in the southwest of the city, near the gates. He deals with all the junk parts that the Gnomes throw out when they're done building their contraptions. If there's one thing he knows, it's where to find components for clockworks -- especially [faulty] ones.'
You say, 'Faulty ones?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Let's give Tanglefuze what he wants. His plans, along with the chaos we've already caused, should be enough to require Bellikos reinforcements. If we give him a few sub-par components, though, his clockworks shouldn't last long enough to cause any real problems for us. Besides, we can deal with Tanglefuze after the Bellikos have been taken care of.'
3. Speak with Percy Scrapfisher 0/1 (Kernagir, the Shining City)
Percy Scrapfisher [npc=34731] is found at approximately (5, 475, -50) in Kernagir.
Task Window Says: To find scrap for Tanglefuze's clockworks, Gihbra said you should look for Percy Scrapfisher, who is near the southeast gates of Kernagir.
You say, 'Hail, Percy Scrapfisher'
Percy Scrapfisher says 'Greetings, _____. It's a perfect day for sifting through junk piles, isn't it? That's why you're talking to me, right? Well, if you're looking for scrap or [junk], you've found the right Gnome!'
You say, 'I'm looking for some junk'
Percy Scrapfisher says 'Hmm...that sounds like some high-end scrap you're after. I think I saw a pile of Mithril Ore in the building west of here. You should check the building with lava tubes for a bag of Golden Cogs. Silver Wires? I think they're in a building to the northeast. And you want what? Faulty Voltage Regulators? Actually, I was carrying some of those when I stopped off at the bar last night. When I woke up today, I didn't have them anymore. I also didn't have my pants. What do you want all this for, if you don't [mind] my asking?'
You say, 'I do mind'
Percy Scrapfisher says 'Okay, sorry! Just curious!'
Task Window Says: Percy says that while in Kernagir you should find all the scrap you need - a Pile of Mithril Ore in a building west of Percy, a Bag of Golden Cogs in a building with lava tubes, a Bag of Silver Wires in a building to the northeast, and Faulty Voltage Regulators near a bar.
4. Deliver 1 Pile of Mithril Ore to Interix Tanglefuze 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Pile of Mithril Ore [item=87056] is a ground spawn at 123, 740, 53 in Kernagir.
5. Deliver 1 Bag of Golden Cogs to Interix Tanglefuze 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Bag of Golden Cogs [item=87057] is a ground spawn at 1036, 592, 135 in Kernagir (bottom floor).
6. Deliver 1 Bag of Silver Wires to Interix Tanglefuze 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Bag of Silver Wires [item=87050] is a ground spawn at 855, 123, 167 in Kernagir.
7. Deliver 1 Bag of Faulty Voltage Regulators to Interix 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Bag of Faulty Voltage Regulators [item=86982] is a ground spawn at 119, 979, 219 in Kernagir.
8. Speak with Gihbra Artinguard 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Brell's will is about to be done. You've already disrupted the Bellikos' plans for the Sporali, Mephits, Gnolls, and Dwarves. Now Tanglefuze will spring the trap we have laid. I'm sure at any moment more Bellikos will arrive to quell the unrest and put a stop to Tanglefuze's plan. Hurry: before they do, use Tanglefuze's clockworks to kill the remaining Bellikos overseers in the husk. Go see if Tanglefuze has finished his work. The time is at hand...'
9. Speak with Interix Tanglefuze 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: Interix should be done with the clockworks by now.
You say, 'Hail, Interix Tanglefuze'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'Yes, I know what's happening. Reinforcements will be here soon. Take these clockworks and kill the overseers.'
You'll get two mobs to assist you, each called "Mithril Clockwork" which you can take with you to complete the next kill steps. You don't have to use them, but they'll make quick work of the bellikos mobs. If you do use them, you don't even have to aggro the bellikos. The clockworks will do all the work.
The clockworks despawn if you die, zone, or take longer 20 minutes. If this happens, you can hail Interix again for another pair of clockwork helpers by hailing Interix again:
You say, 'Hail, Interix Tanglefuze'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'You need reinforcements? Take these!'
10. Kill 5 Bellikos overseers 0/5 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: Now is the time to strike at the Bellikos. Interix has given you powerful clockworks to deal with the overseers.
Any five bellikos count towards this step, including wardens and lecturers.
11. Hail Interix Tanglefuze 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: The Bellikos have been eliminated, for now. See what Interix wants to do with his clockworks.
You say, 'Hail, Interix Tanglefuze'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'You thought you were going to double-cross me? I told you you couldn't! I knew you gave me faulty voltage regulators, and I repaired them. Do you think I am that easily fooled? This is my cavern now. Go and deal with any Bellikos reinforcements if you must. I'll keep my clockworks with me. If the Bellikos enter here, I'll deal with them. If they manage to destroy the clockworks, I'll only create more. I cannot be stopped. Now hurry back to your master, like the good [lapdog] you are.'
You say, 'Lapdog?!'
Interix Tanglefuze  laughs. 'Knowing the truth only makes life harder to bear, doesn't it, _____?'
This step triggers the spawns of three Bellikos at the cave entrance, one of which is a named. They are not linked, but will assist each other. The Autarchian mobs in the area will also assist them.
12. Speak with the Bellikos arbiter, Bryxyx Kyzox 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: The plan has worked. A Bellikos arbiter, Bryxyx Kyzox has arrived to put down the unrest in the husk and eliminate any unwanted intruders.
You say, 'Hail, Bryyxyx Kyzox'
Bryxyx Kyzox says, 'Are you happy? Has Brell told you to do this, or are you acting of your own accord? If you have chosen this path without any [prodding], I praise you. It takes great courage to defy someone as powerful as the First and kill his beloved creations. Please continue as you wish; for I will welcome you to carry out your will as far as you are able.'
You say, 'Prodding?'
Bryxyx Kyzox says, 'But if Brell's will has overpowered your own, desist from this course. Is it really your desire to see your life spilled into lava pools over the arbitrary commands of a distant god? Whose [interest] does Brell hold in mind, yours or his?'
You say, 'Interest?'
Bryxyx Kyzox says 'If you desire, live here in the husk. Do whatever pleases you: work or do not work. And if we anger you, attempt to strike us down. You are only as [free] as you allow yourself to be.'
You say, 'Free?'
Bryxyx Kyzox says 'Anything as complex as a grouping of free creatures will fall into balance. It is the way of things. From what Brell would call chaos arises order. Dwarves mine, Gnolls hunt, and Gnomes create. It is only natural. Order without [imposition].'
You say, 'Imposition?'
Bryxyx Kyzox says 'You would return all these creatures to subjugation? You are confused, as you are under subjugation yourself. Consider my words. Put Brell's will aside; he will carry it out on his own. As I said before, if you, of your own accord, still wish to overpower me, I welcome you to try. Struggle and contention will decide whose will is greater, as it should be.'
You say, 'Hail, Bryyxyx Kyzox'
Bryxyx Kyzox says, 'It is a shame our wills could not coexist.'
You say, 'Hail, Bryyxyx Kyzox'
Bryxyx Kyzox says, 'I welcome your expression of will _____, Now I shall show you mine.'
NOTE: If you take too long and Bryxyx despawns, you can hail Interix again to respawn her:
You say, 'Hail, Interix Tanglefuze'
Interix Tanglefuze says 'I think those arbiters are still out there. Take care of them!'
You will have to go through Bryxyx's dialogue again to make her aggro.
13. Defeat Bryxyx Kyzox 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: Bryxyx is unwilling to allow you to continue your plan of sabotage.
The two adds with her are mezzable and stunnable, but Bryxyx is not. The fight is pretty straight forward.
14. Return to Gihbra Artinguard 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
Task Window Says: You have driven back the Bellikos and depleted their numbers. Gihbra will want to hear of this.
You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Your task 'Brell's Will Be Done' has been updated.
You have disturbed the balance in the husk and killed a powerful Bellikos Arbiter. Brell's First will be on the defensive.
Gihbra Artinguard says 'We've done it, _____! This is surely a blow to the Bellikos! It will take them a while to clean up the mess we've caused here. Brell's [will] has surely been done. Without him it would not have been possible.'
You say, 'Brell's will has been done?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Do not listen to the lies of the Bellikos, _____. You've seen the chaos they have caused. Without the word of Brell, the creatures of the Underfoot do not know what to do with themselves. Everything devolves into chaos. Once the Autarchians and Bellikos have been dealt with, the rule of Brell will be exerted once again over everything in the Underfoot, as it should be.'
512 platinum
Experience
~200 Brellium Tokens
Completion of this task allows you to request the group task for Volska's Husk, Kill the Hatchlings [quest=5043].Rewards:
- Brellium Token [item=88944]

You say, 'Hail, Gihbra Artinguard'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Greetings overlander. I have something most urgent that I can use your [help] with. It is hard to imagine that so many have turned from Brell, but I can't think about that now. There is still much to do and, if you are [willing] to help, we can [slow] them down a bit. I'd do it myself if it weren't for these blasted [pests]! There is much to do, and it is all so very [important].'
You say, 'Important?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'We cannot allow the Autarchians to gain control of the greken. This would not bode very well at all for those faithful to Brell. So we are left with no other [option].'
You say, 'What option?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'The Autarchians and sessiloids harvesting the eggs must be killed. Furthermore, the [hatchlings] themselves must be destroyed. '
You say, 'The hatchlings?'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'However innocent in all this they might be, they are a danger to everyone in the Underfoot should the Autarchians succeed. Will you do this for me. . . For us? Will you put a [stop] to this. . . this. . . anarchy?!'
You say, 'We'll stop it!'
Gihbra Artinguard says 'Oh thank Brell! I only hope it is enough. Brell will not be pleased when he returns! Let us hope that if you are successful, he has someplace to return too! Let me know when you are [ready].'
You have been assigned the task 'Kill the Hatchlings'.
The Autarchians are trying to harvest the greken eggs. They cannot be allowed to succeed. The lack of combat experience among the followers of Brell means that should the Autarchians succeed in gaining control of any of the greken, the ramifications could be far reaching. Gihbra has asked you to see if you can kill the hatchlings, along with any sessiloids and Autarchians currently harvesting the eggs. It is important that all greken hatchlings are destroyed.
You say, 'Ready!'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Volska's Husk - Kill the Greken Hatchlings.

Kill 10 Autarchian reapers 0/10 (Volska's Husk)
Kill 10 sessiloid attendants 0/10 (Volska's Husk)
Kill 6 greken hatchlings 0/6 (Volska's Husk)
_Kill the reapers and attendants (max hit ~8,500) throughout the nest area in order to spawn six greken hatchlings throughout these caves. Kill the hatchlings (max hit ~8,500) and then go hail the coldain Aremaas the Mouth in the central part of the zone, just outside the greken heart near the entrance to the nest._
Speak with Aremaas the Mouth 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
You say, 'Hail, Aremaas the Mouth'
Aremaas the Mouth says 'Greetings \_\_\_\_\_! Gihbra sent me to tell you that the Autarchians have already taken some of the hatchlings from the nest. They are heading for the lava pool!'
Kill 6 greken younglings 0/6 (Volska's Husk)
Kill 3 bellikos deceivers 0/3 (Volska's Husk)
_Six greken younglings (max hit ~8,500) spawn in the south part of the zone. Once they're dead, three bellikos deceivers (max hit ~9,000) spawn in the same area._
_Note: The task locks as the third bellikos deceiver dies._
Speak with Aremaas the Mouth 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
You say, 'Hail, Aremaas the Mouth'
Aremaas the Mouth says 'Well done! Let me know if you are ready to return [home.]'
See what reward you have been offered 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_An ornate chest spawns next to Aremaas inside the instance. Opening the chest completes this step and the task:_
Defeating the Bellikos and killing the hatchlings will stop the Autarchians from using the greken in their plans. As long as they are not allowed to continue harvesting the eggs...

Entered: Fri Mar 12 00:09:22 2010
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This task is the first in a series given by a bellikos named Delth in the Convorteum. She is located in the zone-in area, below the fortress at about -1380, 365, -65._
_KOS mobs wander close to her, so keep a lookout while speaking with her._
You say, 'Hail, Delth'
Delth says 'Many are the teachings of the First. Be warned, outsider, for while I appear weak I am one of the Bellikos, elite creations of the First and I have the power to kill you where you stand. Word has travelled of the outsiders from above and, while I have begun to have [doubts], I am still compelled to uphold the teachings of the First and stop any who will oppose him.'
You say, 'Doubts?'
Delth says 'For ages I have thought that there were some discrepancies between what the Apexus creations, which reside within the pyramid, and those of us out here are taught. From what I have heard you may be able to [help] me.'
You say, 'How can I help?'
Delth says 'As one of the trainers for the Bellikos envoys, I have seen and heard much. I have picked up on many things since I first became aware, and they all seem to point to the same [conclusion].'
You say, 'What conclusion?'
Delth says 'That our creator has some very sinister plans and we, the Bellikos, are but pawns in it all. I need to find a way to prove to the Apexus that something is awry and I think the first step is to compare what is being taught to the Apexus and the Bellikos. Would you be willing to [investigate]?'
You say, 'I'm willing to investigate'
Delth says 'Excellent. Now I will need you to retrieve the teachings of Bellikos, the teachings of Apexus, the Bellikos mantra, and a focus of self. If I look at these things together I should be able to identify the discrepancies that I suspect are there.'
You have been assigned the task 'Different Point of View'.
Delth wants you to gather the teachings of the Apexus and Bellikos so that she can examine them.

---

_Each step in the task opens up one by one as you complete them. None of the items in this task are pre-lootable._
Retrieve a copy of the Bellikos Mantra from a Bellikos Sermonizer 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_Bellikos sermonizers are one of the less common bellikos spawns and seem to be more common the higher into the fortress you go._
Retrieve the Teachings of the Bellikos from a Bellikos Lecturer 0/1 (Volska's Husk)
_The Bellikos Lecturer is found in the sporali caves of Volska's Husk. She has a PH and is the only bellikos spawn in there._
Retrieve the Teachings of the Apexi from an Apexus Scholar 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_Apexus scholars, like sermonizers, seem to be found most often in the higher parts of the fortress, but occasionally spawn in the lower parts. They see invisibility._
Collect a Focus of Self from a Bellikos Recruiter 0/1 (Arthicrex)
_Bellikos recruiters can be found in the zone-in area of Arthicrex. They spawn in and around the house across the dry ravine._
Return to Delth so that she can investigate the items 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_With the items in inventory, return to Delth and hail her._
You say, 'Hail, Delth'
Delth says 'Excellent \_\_\_\_\_. Now give me a moment to research these.'
_Reward:_
105 platinum
40 Brellium Tokens
Experience (1.1 AAs at Level 85)
_Completion of this task leads into a second task, "I, Bellikos"._
- Brellium Token [item=88944]

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This task is the second of three solo tasks that begin with a bellikos named Delth in the Convorteum. She is located in the zone-in area, below the fortress at about -1380, 365, -65._
_KOS mobs wander close to her, so keep a lookout while speaking with her._
You say, 'Hail, Delth'
Delth says 'First protect you outsider, your efforts have proven fruitless as there is not enough proof to convince those inside. We will have to do [more] in order to prove to them that something strange is going on.'
You say, 'More?'
Delth says 'I think that the only way to truly find out what has happened is by going through the [process] of creation yourself.'
You say, 'What process?'
Delth says 'I believe that our creator uses a byproduct of the process used to create Bellikos to create Apexus. Of course the only way to get a hold of this ingredient would be to [create] a Bellikos.'
You say, 'How do we create one?'
Delth says 'The process of putting together one of my kind will require you to gather parts from others like me who have already been animated. Unfortunately, trying to create one of us from scratch is impossible for a mere mortal. You will have to gather [parts] that are already imbued with the animation magic used by our creator.'
You say, 'Parts?'
Delth says 'You will need to gather Bellikos parts, assemble them, and then bring me the byproduct produced by that process.'
You have been assigned the task 'I, Bellikos'.
Delths wants you to gather Bellikos parts, assemble a Bellikos, and bring her the byproduct.

# Delth #3: Convorteum Coercion

**Level:** 85 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 85
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

This task is the third of three solo tasks that begin with a bellikos named Delth in the Convorteum. She is located in the zone-in area, below the fortress at about -1380, 365, -65.
KOS mobs wander close to her, so keep a lookout while speaking with her.
You say, 'Hail, Delth'
Delth says 'Hello again outsider.  Are you here to [finish] what we started?'
You say, 'I'm here to finish it'
Delth says 'I have revealed the truth and it is not a pretty one. Now I must get this information to three of the more influential [members] of the creator's elite creations.'
You say, 'Which members?'
Delth says 'Belsa, Grodnis, and Argnus need to see this information right away. It will not be easy to [reach] them so I ask that you only do this if you think you really can.'
You say, 'I'll reach them'
Delth says 'Take these samples to each of them and this should convince them of the truth.'
You have been assigned the task 'Convorteum Coercion'.
Delth wants you to show the results of her investigation to Belsa, Grodnis, and Argnus.
Deliver the sample of proof to Belsa 0/1 (The Convorteum)
Belsa is one floor up from the port-in point of the fortress. Location: -975, 1070, 375.
Belsa takes the proof and looks it over. 'What is this?' she says. 'I cannot believe Delth actually got this far. I will spread this proof throughout the ranks of those I train. Perhaps it will be enough.'
Deliver the sample of proof to Grodnis 0/1 (The Convorteum)
Grodnis is on the top floor of the fortress and has a short roam range in one of the side areas. Location: -1400, +70, +1050.
Grodnis grabs the information from you. 'Interesting findings here,' he says. 'Those who I lead will find this compelling for sure. Thank you for this outsider.'
Deliver the sample of proof to Argnus 0/1 (The Convorteum)
Argnus is on the top floor of the fortress, in the center pit. He roams around -1100, 1000, 1000, and will be the more difficult of the three turn-ins as he is surrounded by KOS mobs.
Argnus looks at you angrily. 'This is incredible, I will let the others know at once.'
Return to Delth and tell her what the others said 0/1 (The Convorteum)
You say, 'Hail, Delth'
Delth says 'Thank you again _____. Now we must wait and see if it was enough.'
Completion of this task leads into the group mission for the Convorteum.
Reward:
105 platinum
40 Brellium Tokens
Experience (1.1 AAs at Level 85)Rewards:
- Brellium Token [item=88944]
[

Entered: Fri Apr 30 15:24:13 2010
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _This is a group/mini-raid task (3-12 players) given by a bellikos named Delth in the Convorteum. She is located in the zone-in area, below the fortress at about -1380, 365, -65._
_KOS mobs wander close to her, so keep a lookout while speaking with her._
_In order to request this task, you must first have completed Delth's three solo tasks._
You say, 'Hail, Delth'
Delth says 'Greetings outsider, your endeavors have helped greatly and now the sense of self has spread throughout the others. Now we must [strike]. I could also use your assistance to [investigate] the teachings of the first, locate and assemble Bellikos [parts], or to [reach] certain members of the elite creations and deliver the truth to them.'
You say, 'Strike?'
Delth says 'Yes, I will lead the rebellion against our creator, but I fear that I will need more help. You see, I have become a target and I am expecting them to come after me first. If they capture me it may cause the rest of the rebels to give up. But you helped us find all of this and, as such, are just as committed to this all as I am. Please, won't you [join] us?'
You say, 'I'll join you'
Delth says 'Come now, \_\_\_\_\_. Our rage will be the weapon we use to break down the walls and find the truth. Tell me when you are [ready] to go.'
You have been assigned the task 'The First Corruption'.
Delth, a trainer for Bellikos envoys, now knows the truth about The First Creation's teachings, thanks to your previous efforts. And now that you have begun to spread the cult of the self inside the pyramid, Delth thinks that it is time to call upon your new allies and attack Keeper Veilyn, who holds the key to the records room.
Delth has gone on ahead, but you still need to gather your allies for the assault on the Keeper. Talk to Argnus first.
_NOTE: You'll zone into an instanced version of the Convorteum and land on the first level of the fortress in aggro range of wandering mobs. Up to three mobs can aggro you at the zone-in point if you are not invis._
You say, 'Ready!'
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...

---

_This is an escort task with waves of mobs that must be killed. It is recommended that you clear a path up to the third floor of the temple prior to hailing Argnus._
Speak With Argnus 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_Argnus is found on the first floor of the temple._
You say, 'Hail, Argnus'
Argnus says 'Is me convinced. First to Underfoot teaches freedoms and self; to Convorteum teaches slaveries and sacrificers. I bring self to Convorteum! You me go smash face of Keeper, see in room of records what really true! And we go gets Belsa and Grodnis!'
_He'll walk to the second floor to Belsa's location._
Take Argnus with you and hail Belsa 0/1 (The Convorteum)
You say, 'Hail, Belsa'
Belsa says 'Now is our time to strike. The First has been careful about keeping knowledge of the self away from those of us in the pyramid. But now that we have it, we must carry it out to its fullest extent. If we can catch Keeper Veilyn off guard, we can breach the records room and disseminate the truth about the First to all of the First's creations, and to all of the Underfoot.'
_The two will walk to Grodnis's location (also on the second floor)._
Take your allies with you and hail Grodnis 0/1 (The Convorteum)
You say, 'Hail, Grodnis'
Grodnis says '\_\_\_\_\_, you bring Argnus and Belsa! Good! I see Delth go being drugged up to jails. We make big fight, get Delth, go bash Keeper! Grodnis no afraid; have strong self!'
_The three of them will walk up the next ramp, to the second set of prison cells where you'll find Delth._
Take your allies with you and hail Delth 0/1 (The Convorteum)
You say, 'Hail, Delth'
Delth says 'You see, what the First does to those who dare to question, who dare to think? The moment I start believing what I preach, he locks me away. There are many secrets in here, Painlord, but we will lay them bare. We will strip the stones from this pyramid if need be. If I can charge up my powers, I might be able to cast a spell powerful enough to dispel the Keeper's cloak of protection. Come, let us make our assault on the records room while there is still time.'
_The four of them will walk up to the Reclamation room (the lava room with the platforms)._
Gather in the Reclamation Room 0/1 (The Convorteum)
Let your allies get into position and challenge Keeper Veilyn 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_Once the four NPCs you've been escorting reach their positions, they'll go through some dialogue. After this point, the first wave of mobs spawns._
Delth says, 'We are near the Keeper. Let us take up defensive positions while I charge the spell that will make her visible.
Keeper Veilyn says, 'Who are you to challenge the First? His servants will deal with you.'
Defeat the first wave of enemies 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_About 12 mobs in total spawn here (a mix of Bellikos and Apexi) and charge the platforms. They'll attack players as well as the NPCs. They are all mezzable._
_Keep the mobs off Delth while she casts a long spell. Then kill them. (Those you kill before she's done casting will respawn instantly.) As she completes her spell, you'll see:_
Delth shouts 'I have gained sufficient power to proceed. Get rid of those creatures quickly!'
_Note: You'll only see this emote if you're in close proximity to Delth. It's not a zonewide emote._
_Be ready for another large wave of mobs once you kill the last of those._
Keeper Veilyn says, 'The First is our master. Reject the beliefs of our enemies.'
Defeat the second wave of enemies 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_Once the above wave is dead, a second wave spawns nearby: 6-8 telmiran mobs (mezzable) immediately charge and attack players and the NPCs. In addition to those, about 10 fire mephits spawn at the windows around the large halls. They AE for damage and gravity-flux. They are mezzable and permanently rooted._
_Keep them off Delth while she casts, wait for her emote, and then kill them._
_Be ready for another large wave of mobs once you kill the last of those._
Keeper Veilyn says, 'Belief in self only leads to destruction. The Gorgex will feast upon you.'
Defeat the third wave of enemies 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_10 grekens spawn nearby and charge you (some spawn on the platforms; some spawn in the lava). They are mezzable and do not see invis. A named spawns in the center of the lava. It may be permanently rooted or tethered to the floor of the lava. It sees invis and SOS and will target players at random._
_Kill the named before killing the other grekens as they respawn if they die before the named._
_Be ready for a named mob once the last of those dies._
Keeper Veilyn says, 'The kiln brings forth your doom. When the crusher reaches you, Delth, you will die.'
Defend Dealth from Crushing Apexus 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_A Crushing Apexus spawns in the hallways outside the lava room. It slowly walks up towards Delth. You must kill it before it reaches Delth or the task fails._
_For the most part, you can beat on it without it aggroing you, but it will occasionally turn around and melee (hits quite hard). It is lurch-able and highsun-able until around 30%._
Crushing Apexus has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Crushing Apexus falls, shaking the pyramid.
Keeper Veilyn says, 'I will kill you traitors myself!'
Keeper Veilyn says, 'You might have torn my cloak, but I will unravel the thread of your lives!'
Keeper Veilyn says, 'Do you see what your love of self has brought you? You have lost the love of the Master... And your life.'
Keeper Veilyn says, 'Adventurers, you may speak to me in the throne room if you dare.'
Defend your allies from Keeper Veilyn 0/1 (The Convorteum)
Hail Keeper Veilyn in the throne room 0/1 (The Convorteum)
You say, 'Hail, Keeper Veilyn'
Keeper Veilyn says 'You are fools to come here and try to turn the children of the First against each other. He permits no dissent, and there is no self. We are his creations, and he is our master and lord. You either serve Brell or the First; there is no other path. The self is but an illusion in the sight of your god. His will is an ocean; you are but the smallest vessel. And you will drown'
Keeper Veilyn says 'You will be consumed by the wrath of the First.'
Kill 1 Keeper Veilyn 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_This is a relatively simple kill, except she occasionally AEs "Lava Ball":_
Lava Ball: Targeted AE 120', Fire (-650)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 35000
Keeper Veilyn has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Keeper Veilyn's corpse will be turned to clay by the First.
Speak with Delth 0/1 (The Convorteum)
_Delth is back on the lava platform. Hailing her spawns a chest._
Although your allies were injured, you have defeated Keeper Veilyn and gained access to the records room. Delth will continue her search for answers and will spread the Cult of Self among the servants in the Convorteum.
See what reward has been offered 0/1 (The Convorteum)
